TL;DR: Horizon3D是一种结合高斯原语与稀疏BEV特征的雷达-相机融合框架，用于长距离3D目标检测，通过关键点引导的高斯初始化、目标中心稀疏融合和双路径时间融合，在TruckScenes数据集上达到SOTA性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有雷达-相机融合方法在长距离检测中表现有限：BEV方法计算量快速增长且丢失细粒度目标细节，查询方法效率高但场景上下文不足；时间融合需要同时处理多帧累积和快速运动物体的运动建模。

Method: 1）关键点引导的高斯初始化：从雷达和相机估计的目标关键点初始化高斯原语；2）目标中心稀疏融合：在目标层面细化高斯原语，并将其溅射到BEV平面以融合目标级细节与稀疏雷达BEV上下文；3）双路径时间融合：BEV路径进行场景级累积，高斯路径进行目标级运动传播。

Result: 在TruckScenes验证集上，Horizon3D比之前最佳方法NDS提升+3.0，mAP提升+1.6，同时保持有竞争力的推理速度。

Conclusion: Horizon3D通过高斯原语与稀疏BEV特征的结合，有效解决了长距离3D检测中细粒度细节与场景上下文的权衡问题，并实现了双路径时间融合，达到了当前最优的雷达-相机3D检测性能。

Original Abstract: Long-range 3D object detection is critical for safe autonomous driving at highway speeds, yet existing radar-camera fusion methods remain limited at extended ranges. BEV-based methods capture scene-level context but incur rapidly growing computation and often lose fine-grained object detail, while query-based methods are efficient but provide limited scene-level context. Temporal fusion further requires both multi-frame accumulation for sparse distant observations and object-level motion modeling for fast-moving objects. We propose Horizon3D, a sparse radar-camera fusion framework for long-range 3D object detection that combines Gaussian primitives with sparse BEV features. Horizon3D initializes Gaussian primitives at radar- and camera-estimated object keypoints using Keypoint-Guided Gaussian Initialization, refines them through Object-Centric Sparse Fusion, and splats them onto the BEV plane to fuse object-level detail with sparse radar BEV context. It further introduces Dual-Path Temporal Fusion, which aggregates temporal cues through a BEV path for scene-level accumulation and a Gaussian path for object-level motion propagation. Experiments on TruckScenes show that Horizon3D achieves state-of-the-art radar-camera 3D detection performance. On the validation set, it outperforms the previous best method by +3.0 NDS and +1.6 mAP while maintaining competitive inference speed.

中文摘要翻译: 长距离3D目标检测对于高速自动驾驶的安全性至关重要，然而现有的雷达-相机融合方法在扩展范围内仍然表现有限。基于BEV的方法能够捕获场景级上下文，但计算量快速增长且容易丢失细粒度目标细节，而基于查询的方法效率高但提供的场景级上下文有限。时间融合进一步要求对稀疏的远距离观测进行多帧累积，并对快速移动物体进行目标级运动建模。我们提出了Horizon3D，一种结合高斯原语与稀疏BEV特征的稀疏雷达-相机融合框架，用于长距离3D目标检测。Horizon3D通过关键点引导的高斯初始化在雷达和相机估计的目标关键点处初始化高斯原语，通过目标中心稀疏融合对其细化，并将其溅射到BEV平面上以融合目标级细节与稀疏雷达BEV上下文。该方法进一步引入了双路径时间融合，通过BEV路径进行场景级累积，通过高斯路径进行目标级运动传播，从而聚合时间线索。在TruckScenes数据集上的实验表明，Horizon3D实现了当前最优的雷达-相机3D检测性能。在验证集上，它在保持有竞争力推理速度的同时，比之前的最优方法NDS提升+3.0，mAP提升+1.6。

</details>


### [31] [PiLoT v2: Pixel-to-Orthogonal Map Alignment for Free-view UAV Geo-localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31098)
*Xinyi Liu,Xiaoya Cheng,Rouwan Wu,Zhaochen Wang,Shen Yan,Maojun Zhang,Yu Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PiLoT v2提出从像素到3D网格配准转向像素到正射地图配准，利用TDOM和DSM替代重型3D网格，实现轻量级、无漂移的无人机地理定位，在保持高精度的同时大幅降低存储和计算开销。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有PiLoT系统依赖重型3D网格，导致存储开销大、地图采集复杂、计算渲染成本高，严重阻碍了在嵌入式平台上的部署。为解决这些瓶颈，需要一种轻量级但鲁棒的替代方案。

Method: 1) 利用真数字正射影像图(TDOM)和数字表面模型(DSM)作为参考底图，以CPU友好的地图裁剪操作替代GPU密集型的3D渲染；2) 训练跨视角特征配准网络，弥合2.5D正射裁剪图与自由视角倾斜无人机图像之间的几何差异；3) 将机载传感器先验（重力方向和单点激光测距）直接嵌入位姿优化流形中，增强对跨视角视觉退化的鲁棒性。

Result: PiLoT v2达到了与基于像素到3D配准的前代系统相当甚至更优的精度，同时存储和计算成本显著降低。

Conclusion: PiLoT v2通过从像素到3D网格配准转向像素到正射地图配准，成功解决了前代系统的部署瓶颈，为GNSS拒止环境下的无人机地理定位提供了一种轻量级、高精度且适合嵌入式平台的解决方案。

Original Abstract: Real-time, drift-free UAV geo-localization is essential for autonomous missions in GNSS-denied environments. The pioneering system, PiLoT, achieves high precision via Neural Pixel-to-3D Registration, aligning UAV video streams with a single rendered reference view from 3D meshes. However, its reliance on heavy 3D meshes incurs massive storage overheads, complex map acquisition, and significant computational rendering costs, severely hindering deployment on embedded platforms. To address these bottlenecks, we propose PiLoT v2, a lightweight yet robust evolution that shifts the paradigm to direct pixel-to-orthogonal map registration for free-view UAV geo-localization. By leveraging True Digital Orthophoto Maps (TDOMs) and Digital Surface Models (DSMs) as the reference substrate, PiLoT v2 replaces GPU-intensive 3D rendering with a highly efficient, CPU-friendly map cropping operation. To bridge the severe geometric discrepancy between these 2.5D orthogonal crops and free-view oblique UAV imagery, we train a cross-view feature registration network using a novel, large-scale geometrically annotated dataset. Furthermore, we integrate onboard sensor prior--specifically gravity direction and single-point laser rang--directly into the pose optimization manifold to enhance robustness against cross-view visual degradation. Experimental results demonstrate that PiLoT v2 achieves performance comparable to, or even exceeding, its Pixel-to-3D predecessor, while offering drastically lower storage and computational costs.

中文摘要翻译: 实时、无漂移的无人机地理定位对于GNSS拒止环境下的自主任务至关重要。先驱系统PiLoT通过神经像素到3D配准，将无人机视频流与从3D网格渲染的单一参考视图对齐，实现了高精度。然而，其对重型3D网格的依赖导致了巨大的存储开销、复杂的地图采集以及显著的计算渲染成本，严重阻碍了在嵌入式平台上的部署。为解决这些瓶颈，我们提出了PiLoT v2——一种轻量级且鲁棒的演进方案，它将范式转变为直接像素到正射地图配准，用于自由视角无人机地理定位。通过利用真数字正射影像图(TDOM)和数字表面模型(DSM)作为参考底图，PiLoT v2以高效、CPU友好的地图裁剪操作替代了GPU密集型的3D渲染。为弥合这些2.5D正射裁剪图与自由视角斜向无人机图像之间的严重几何差异，我们使用一个新的大规模几何标注数据集训练了一个跨视角特征配准网络。此外，我们将机载传感器先验——即重力方向和单点激光测距——直接嵌入位姿优化流形中，以增强对跨视角视觉退化的鲁棒性。实验结果表明，PiLoT v2达到了与基于像素到3D配准的前代系统相当甚至更优的性能，同时存储和计算成本大幅降低。

</details>


### [32] [Seeing Through Multiple Views: Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning via Selective Neurons for Consistent Radiology Report Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31099)
*Yucheng Chen,Jinjing Zhu,Yang Yu,Yufei Shi,Hane Naghshbandi,Jinhua Liu,Angela S. Koh,Fang Fen,Kian Eng Ong,Si Yong Yeo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出View-PNDF框架，从神经元层面解决多视图X光影像报告生成中视图间临床不一致的问题，通过检测并微调视图特异性神经元，实现参数高效的视图一致性报告生成，在标准数据集上显著提升了视图特异性报告质量。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有放射学报告生成方法在处理多视图X光影像时采用直接的特征融合方式，忽略了单模型处理不同视图时可能产生的临床不一致性和不准确性，影响性能和临床可靠性。

Method: View-PNDF包含三个模块：(i) 视图特异性神经元检测模块，识别对特定视图响应的神经元；(ii) 验证模块，量化这些神经元的存在性；(iii) 选择性微调策略，增强检测到的神经元同时保持视图无关表征。仅更新视图特异性神经元，实现低计算成本的视图一致诊断。随后使用LLM将视图特异性报告整合为完整报告。

Result: 在两个医学RRG基准上的大量实验表明，View-PNDF显著提升了视图特异性胸部X光报告生成质量，同时保持了良好的通用视图性能。

Conclusion: View-PNDF从神经元视角出发，以参数高效的方式实现了视图一致的放射学报告生成，有效缓解了多视图处理中的临床不一致问题。

Original Abstract: Recent years have seen substantial advances in radiology report generation (RRG), yet existing approaches predominantly adopt direct feature fusion when handling multi-view X-ray images. Such approaches overlook the potential clinical inconsistencies and inaccuracies arising when a single model processes different views, adversely impacting performance and clinical reliability. To this end, we introduce View-PNDF (View-specific Pattern Neuron Detection and Fine-tuning), a parameter-efficient framework that fosters view-consistent report generation from a neuronal perspective. Specifically, View-PNDF comprises: (i) a view-specific neuron detection module identifying neurons responsive to particular views, (ii) a verification module quantifying the existence of these neurons, and (iii) a selective fine-tuning strategy strengthening detected neurons while preserving view-agnostic representations. By updating only view-specific neurons, View-PNDF achieves consistent diagnoses across different views with reduced computational costs. Subsequently, we employ Large Language Models (LLMs) to consolidate the view-specific reports into a complete radiology report. Furthermore, we use traditional Natural Language Generation (NLG) metrics-based assessment on integrated reports for baseline comparison and employ LLM-based assessment (e.g., GPT-4o) on view-specific reports to capture clinical significance. Extensive experiments on two medical RRG benchmarks demonstrate that View-PNDF substantially improves view-specific chest X-ray report generation quality while maintaining robust general-view performance.

中文摘要翻译: 近年来，放射学报告生成（RRG）领域取得了显著进展，但现有方法在处理多视图X光影像时主要采用直接的特征融合方式。这种方式忽略了单模型处理不同视图时可能产生的临床不一致性和不准确性，对性能和临床可靠性产生不利影响。为此，我们提出View-PNDF（视图特异性模式神经元检测与微调），一个从神经元视角促进视图一致报告生成的参数高效框架。具体而言，View-PNDF包含：(i) 视图特异性神经元检测模块，用于识别对特定视图响应的神经元；(ii) 验证模块，用于量化这些神经元的存在性；(iii) 选择性微调策略，在增强检测到的神经元的同时保持视图无关的表征。通过仅更新视图特异性神经元，View-PNDF以较低的计算成本实现了跨视图的一致诊断。随后，我们利用大语言模型（LLMs）将视图特异性报告整合为完整的放射学报告。此外，我们对整合报告采用基于传统自然语言生成（NLG）指标的评估以进行基线对比，并对视图特异性报告采用基于LLM的评估（如GPT-4o）以捕捉临床显著性。在两个医学RRG基准上的大量实验表明，View-PNDF显著提升了视图特异性胸部X光报告生成质量，同时保持了稳健的通用视图性能。

</details>


### [33] [TaxoMIL: Taxonomy-Constrained Learning for Hierarchical Whole Slide Image Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31100)
*Chaeyeon Lee,Khang Nguyen Quoc,Jinsol Song,Yosep Chong,Kwangil Yim,Jin Tae Kwak*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TaxoMIL提出了一种基于临床分类树约束的多粒度文本生成框架，将全切片图像诊断从平面分类任务转化为层级感知的文本生成任务，在三个WSI数据集上超越了现有MIL和VLM方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有MIL方法将WSI诊断视为离散标签上的平面分类任务，忽略了临床推理中固有的层级、从粗到细的结构；而近期层次分类器和VLM要么无法捕捉相关诊断间的语义连续性，要么产生不受约束的文本生成导致分类学幻觉和父子标签违反。

Method: TaxoMIL采用双头Transformer解码器分别生成粗粒度和细粒度诊断文本，并引入分类树引导的目标函数来显式结构化学标签嵌入空间，严格将切片级视觉表征锚定在临床分类树中。

Result: 在三个不同WSI数据集上的大量实验表明，TaxoMIL一致优于最先进的MIL分类器和基于VLM的生成方法，输出准确且具有层级感知的诊断预测。

Conclusion: 将WSI诊断重新定义为多粒度文本生成任务，并施加分类树约束，能够有效解决层级分类中的语义连续性和标签一致性问题，提升诊断准确性和可解释性。

Original Abstract: Whole slide image (WSI) analysis is central to computational pathology, with multiple instance learning (MIL) emerging as the standard pipeline for slide-level diagnosis. However, conventional approaches formulate WSI diagnosis as a flat classification task over discrete labels, contradicting the inherently hierarchical, coarse-to-fine nature of clinical reasoning. Although recent hierarchical classifiers and vision-language models (VLMs) have sought to address this structural gap, they either fail to capture semantic continuity between related diagnoses or suffer from unconstrained text generation that produces taxonomic hallucinations and parent-child label violations. To address these limitations, we propose TaxoMIL, a taxonomy-constrained framework that reformulates WSI diagnosis as a multi-granularity text generation task. TaxoMIL utilizes a dual-head Transformer decoder to generate coarse- and fine-level diagnostic text, and introduces taxonomy-guided objectives that explicitly structure the label embedding space and strictly ground slide-level visual representations within the clinical taxonomy. Extensive experiments across three diverse WSI datasets demonstrate that TaxoMIL consistently outperforms state-of-the-art MIL classifiers and VLM-based generative methods, yielding accurate and hierarchy-aware diagnostic predictions. The code is released at https://github.com/QuIIL/TaxoMIL

中文摘要翻译: 全切片图像分析是计算病理学的核心，多实例学习已成为切片级诊断的标准流程。然而，传统方法将WSI诊断建模为离散标签上的平面分类任务，这与临床推理中固有的层级性、从粗到细的本质相矛盾。尽管近期层次分类器和视觉-语言模型试图弥合这一结构性差距，但它们要么无法捕获相关诊断之间的语义连续性，要么因不受约束的文本生成而产生分类学幻觉和父子标签违反。为解决这些局限，我们提出TaxoMIL——一个分类树约束框架，将WSI诊断重新定义为多粒度文本生成任务。TaxoMIL利用双头Transformer解码器生成粗粒度和细粒度诊断文本，并引入分类树引导的目标函数，显式结构化学标签嵌入空间，严格将切片级视觉表征锚定在临床分类树中。在三个不同的WSI数据集上进行的大量实验表明，TaxoMIL一致优于最先进的MIL分类器和基于VLM的生成方法，输出准确且具有层级感知的诊断预测。代码已开源在https://github.com/QuIIL/TaxoMIL。

</details>


### [34] [InfiniVerse: Occupancy Guided Unbounded Scene Generation for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31109)
*Xiaoyu Ye,Leheng Li,Xinyu Ji,Yingjie Cai,Hongda He,Xu Yan,Guanyi Zhao,Ying-Cong Chen,Bingbing Liu,Shuguang Cui,Zhen Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: InfiniVerse是一个统一的流程，能够从单帧输入生成长期、2D-3D对齐且可控的动态城市场景视频，在Waymo和nuScenes数据集上实现了SOTA性能（FID 6.4, FVD 67.97）。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在自动驾驶领域，生成真实、可控且时间连贯的城市场景是一个关键但未解决的挑战，现有方法在长距离生成、时空一致性和跨模态对齐方面存在不足。

Method: 首先从多视角帧重建3D占据表示；然后基于该表示沿任意轨迹进行自回归场景扩展；接着通过视频扩散模型将粗粒度占据网格转化为真实且时空一致的视频序列；最后提出分层草绘-细化范式，将生成的视频重新投影为图像条件反馈以增强3D占据表示，实现视觉与空间域的跨模态对齐与相互增强。

Result: 在Waymo Open Dataset和nuScenes上的大量评估表明，InfiniVerse实现了SOTA性能，FID为6.4，FVD为67.97，在持续时间和稳定性上显著优于现有基准。

Conclusion: InfiniVerse通过统一的2D-3D对齐生成流程，在长距离动态城市场景合成任务上取得了最优性能，验证了跨模态相互增强范式的有效性。

Original Abstract: Generating realistic, controllable, and temporally coherent urban environments is a critical yet unresolved challenge in the autonomous driving community. In this paper, we introduce InfiniVerse, a unified pipeline for long-range, 2D-3D-aligned, and controllable synthesis of dynamic urban scenes from a single frame. In practice, our approach first reconstructs a 3D occupancy representation from the input multi-view frame. This representation serves as a foundation for autoregressive scene extension along arbitrary trajectories. Subsequently, a video diffusion model translates the coarse occupancy grid into realistic, spatiotemporally consistent video sequences. Moreover, we propose a hierarchical sketch-and-refine paradigm, in which the generated videos are re-projected as image-conditioned feedback to enhance the 3D occupancy representation, establishing cross-modal alignment and mutual enhancement between the visual and spatial domains. Extensive evaluations on the Waymo Open Dataset and nuScenes demonstrate that InfiniVerse achieves state-of-the-art performance, with a FID of 6.4 and FVD of 67.97, significantly outperforming existing benchmarks in both duration and stability.

中文摘要翻译: 生成真实、可控且时间连贯的城市场景是自动驾驶社区中一个关键但尚未解决的挑战。在本文中，我们提出了InfiniVerse，这是一个统一的流程，用于从单帧输入进行长距离、2D-3D对齐且可控的动态城市场景合成。具体地，我们的方法首先从输入的多视角帧中重建一个3D占据表示。该表示作为沿任意轨迹进行自回归场景扩展的基础。随后，一个视频扩散模型将粗粒度占据网格转化为真实且时空一致的视频序列。此外，我们提出了一个分层草绘-细化范式，其中生成的视频被重新投影为图像条件反馈以增强3D占据表示，从而建立视觉域与空间域之间的跨模态对齐和相互增强。在Waymo Open Dataset和nuScenes上的大量评估表明，InfiniVerse实现了最先进的性能，FID为6.4，FVD为67.97，在持续时间和稳定性上显著优于现有基准。

</details>


### [35] [JacobianAvatar: Temporally Consistent Semi-rigid Avatar Reconstruction from a Monocular Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31115)
*Changyeon Won,Min-Gyu Park,Seonghwan Park,Ju Hong Yoon,Hae-Gon Jeon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出利用神经雅可比场（NJF）表示半刚体变形，结合约束泊松求解器、符号距离雅可比正则化和变形引导残差流损失，从单目视频中生成具有真实服装动态的3D人体化身，在时空一致性和几何保真度上超越现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 单目设置下生成具有复杂运动（如服装动态）的真实人体化身，需要同时建模全局和局部变形，但自遮挡区域和不可见表面的存在使得该任务极具挑战性。

Method: 利用神经雅可比场（NJF）表示半刚体变形，通过自监督神经网络预测与姿态相关的雅可比矩阵，并求解泊松方程得到变形。针对单目输入的难点，引入三个关键组件：约束泊松求解器（抑制边界伪影）、符号距离雅可比正则化（恢复腋窝、大腿等频繁遮挡区域）、变形引导残差流损失（增强运动时域一致性）。

Result: 在基准数据集和真实世界视频上的实验表明，该方法生成的化身在时域稳定性和几何一致性上均优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 提出的带三个关键组件的神经雅可比场方法能够有效从单目视频中生成高质量、时域稳定且几何一致的动态人体化身，尤其在处理自遮挡和不可见区域方面表现出色。

Original Abstract: Generating realistic human avatars in complex motions--such as clothing dynamics--requires modeling of global and local deformations which remains challenging in monocular settings. We address this problem by leveraging neural Jacobian fields (NJFs) for representing semi-rigid deformations. We train self-supervised neural networks for predicting Jacobian matrices that give the pose-dependent deformations, by solving a Poisson equation. However, monocular input presents several difficulties such as self-occluded regions and invisible surfaces. To address these issues, we introduce three key components: a constrained Poisson solver, signed distance-based Jacobian regularization, and a deformation-guided residual flow loss, which together suppress boundary artifacts, recover frequently occluded regions such as armpits and thighs, and enforce temporal consistency during motion. Experiments on benchmark and in-the-wild videos demonstrate that our method generates temporally stable and geometrically coherent avatars, outperforming state-of-the-art approaches.

中文摘要翻译: 生成具有复杂运动（如服装动态）的真实人体化身，需要对全局和局部变形进行建模，这在单目设置中仍然具有挑战性。我们通过利用神经雅可比场（NJF）表示半刚体变形来解决这一问题。我们训练自监督神经网络来预测与姿态相关的雅可比矩阵，并通过求解泊松方程得到变形。然而，单目输入存在自遮挡区域和不可见表面等困难。为了解决这些问题，我们引入三个关键组件：约束泊松求解器、基于符号距离的雅可比正则化以及变形引导的残差流损失，它们共同抑制边界伪影，恢复腋窝、大腿等频繁遮挡区域，并在运动过程中强制时域一致性。在基准数据集和真实世界视频上的实验表明，我们的方法生成的化身在时域上稳定且几何上一致，优于现有最先进方法。

</details>


### [36] [WildProp: Visual Estimation of Wildlife Body Proportions at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31125)
*Mustafa Chasmai,Aaron Sun,Subhransu Maji*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WildProp是一个无需训练的框架，通过从大规模无约束图像库中进行检索驱动的对应点匹配，估计野生动物身体比例分布，避免了有监督关键点管道的物种特异性训练。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统的群体水平形态测量依赖于受控成像或物理标本处理，规模化扩展受限。现有监督学习方法需要逐物种训练关键点检测器，无法灵活适应任意物种和用户定义的身体部位。

Method: 给定一张用户标注的标准图像，WildProp利用基础模型特征进行姿态感知检索，通过密集补丁级匹配传递部位端点，使用几何一致性过滤预测，并聚合检索图像的测量结果以估计群体水平的比例分布。

Result: 在涵盖鸟类和两栖动物的三个大型形态测量数据集上，中位相对误差为10-20%。消融实验表明姿态感知检索对稳定估计至关重要，鲁棒聚合可减轻关键点和姿态噪声。跨鸟类、青蛙、昆虫和花朵的案例研究展示了广泛适用性。

Conclusion: 精心策划的、基于网络规模图像的二维对应点匹配能够为跨物种、跨地理区域和跨季节的群体比较及子群分析提供可扩展的形态测量代理方法。

Original Abstract: Population-level morphometric measurements underpin ecological and evolutionary studies but traditionally require controlled imaging or physical specimen handling, limiting scalability. We present WildProp, a training-free framework that estimates wildlife body proportion distributions directly from large-scale, unconstrained image repositories. We cast morphometric estimation as a retrieval-driven correspondence problem: given a single user-annotated canonical image, WildProp performs pose-aware retrieval using foundation model features, transfers part endpoints via dense patch-level matching, filters predictions using geometric consistency, and aggregates measurements across retrieved images to estimate population-level ratio distributions. Unlike supervised keypoint pipelines, our approach adapts to arbitrary species and user-defined parts without per-species training. Evaluations on three large morphometric datasets spanning birds and amphibians show median relative errors of 10-20%. We further highlight the broad applicability of our approach through a number of case studies measuring various proportions across diverse taxa, including birds, frogs, insects, and flowers. Ablations demonstrate that pose-aware retrieval is critical for stable estimation, while robust aggregation mitigates keypoint and pose noise. Our results indicate that carefully curated 2D correspondences over web-scale imagery can provide scalable morphometric proxies for comparative and subgroup analyses across taxa, geography, and seasonality.

中文摘要翻译: 群体水平的形态测量是生态学和进化研究的基础，但传统方法需要受控成像或物理标本处理，限制了可扩展性。我们提出WildProp——一种无需训练的框架，可直接从大规模、无约束的图像库中估计野生动物身体比例分布。我们将形态测量估计建模为检索驱动的对应点匹配问题：给定一张用户标注的标准图像，WildProp利用基础模型特征进行姿态感知检索，通过密集补丁级匹配传递部位端点，使用几何一致性过滤预测，并聚合检索图像的测量结果以估计群体水平的比例分布。与有监督关键点管道不同，我们的方法无需逐物种训练即可适应任意物种和用户定义的身体部位。在涵盖鸟类和两栖动物的三个大型形态测量数据集上的评估显示，中位相对误差为10-20%。我们进一步通过跨多个分类群（包括鸟类、青蛙、昆虫和花朵）的案例研究，展示了该方法的广泛适用性。消融实验表明，姿态感知检索对稳定估计至关重要，而鲁棒聚合可以减轻关键点和姿态噪声。我们的结果表明，在网络规模图像上精心策划的二维对应点匹配，能够为跨物种、跨地理区域和跨季节的群体比较及子群分析提供可扩展的形态测量代理方法。

</details>


### [37] [SkillSpotter: Pose-Aware Multi-View Skilled Action Detection and Grading in Ego-Exo Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31127)
*Björn Braun,Christian Holz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SkillSpotter，一种面向增强现实眼镜或固定摄像头场景的、能够同时检测和分级熟练动作的位姿感知多视图架构。该方法通过自适应时间抑制、门控3D人体姿态融合和双向跨视图注意力三个模块，在Ego-Exo4D和HoloAssist基准上显著提升了检测与分级性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有系统在体育、烹饪、音乐等领域的增强现实或固定摄像头场景下，缺乏同时检测熟练动作并评估其执行质量的能力。尽管Ego-Exo4D提出了熟练度演示基准，但现有时间动作检测架构在分级任务上表现近乎随机，无法满足个性化实时辅导的需求。

Method: SkillSpotter采用位姿感知的多视图架构，包含三个任务特定模块：(1)自适应时间抑制——处理不同活动中熟练动作的密度变化；(2)门控3D人体姿态融合——利用人体运动学作为视觉特征的补充信号；(3)双向跨视图注意力——有效结合自我中心视角和外部视角的信息。三个模块可迁移至其他时间动作检测模型。

Result: 相比最优基线，SkillSpotter将类别特定mAP从12.40提升至21.82（+76%），平衡准确率从55.99%提升至60.40%。该方法在Ego-Exo4D之外可泛化至HoloAssist数据集，且各模块可迁移至其他时间动作检测模型并带来一致增益。

Conclusion: SkillSpotter通过专门设计的联合检测与分级架构，有效解决了在自我-外部视角设置中同时检测熟练动作并评估其执行质量的问题，显著超越了现有方法，并具有良好的模块可迁移性和跨数据集泛化能力。

Original Abstract: To enable personalized, real-time coaching using Augmented Reality glasses or fixed camera setups in domains such as sports, cooking, or music, a system must understand not just what a person does, but how well they execute an activity. In an ego-exo video setting, this requires simultaneously detecting individual skilled actions and classifying each as correct or needing improvement, which Ego-Exo4D's proficiency demonstration benchmark formalized. We first adapt seven state-of-the-art temporal action detection architectures to this task, extend the evaluation protocol to disentangle detection from grading, and show that existing methods grade near-randomly. We then introduce SkillSpotter, a pose-aware multi-view architecture that jointly detects and grades skilled actions through three task-specific modules: (1) adaptive temporal suppression to handle the varying density of skilled actions across diverse activities, (2) gated 3D body pose fusion to leverage body kinematics as a complementary signal to visual features, and (3) bidirectional cross-view attention to combine ego and exo views effectively. SkillSpotter improves class-specific mAP from 12.40 to 21.82 (+76%) and balanced accuracy from 55.99% to 60.40% over the best baseline. SkillSpotter's modules transfer to other temporal action detection models with consistent gains, and our method generalizes beyond Ego-Exo4D to HoloAssist. Code: https://github.com/eth-siplab/SkillSpotter

中文摘要翻译: 为了实现利用增强现实眼镜或固定摄像头设置在体育、烹饪或音乐等领域进行个性化的实时辅导，系统不仅要理解一个人做了什么，还必须理解其执行活动的熟练程度。在自我-外部（ego-exo）视频设置中，这需要同时检测单个熟练动作并将其分类为正确或需要改进，这正是Ego-Exo4D熟练度演示基准所规范化的任务。我们首先将七种最先进的时间动作检测架构适配到该任务，扩展评估协议以解耦检测与分级，并表明现有方法在分级上近乎随机。随后我们引入了SkillSpotter，一种位姿感知的多视图架构，通过三个任务特定模块联合检测和分级熟练动作：(1)自适应时间抑制，用于处理不同活动中熟练动作的密度变化；(2)门控3D人体姿态融合，利用人体运动学作为视觉特征的互补信号；(3)双向跨视图注意力，有效结合自我视角和外部视角。相比最优基线，SkillSpotter将类别特定mAP从12.40提升至21.82（+76%），平衡准确率从55.99%提升至60.40%。SkillSpotter的各模块可迁移至其他时间动作检测模型并带来一致增益，且我们的方法可泛化至Ego-Exo4D之外的HoloAssist数据集。代码：https://github.com/eth-siplab/SkillSpotter

</details>


### [38] [MSNN-LINet: Cross-Modal Learning via Continuous Linear Integration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31135)
*Gabriel Clinger*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LINet提出了一种三流并行网络（RGB、深度、集成流），通过线性集成卷积（LIConv2d）算子实现逐层连续跨模态融合，并解决了初始化病理和路径坍塌问题，在SUN RGB-D场景分类中达到SOTA。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有RGB-D多模态架构中，特征融合被当作离散的、启发式事件（早期融合过早纠缠表征，晚期融合隔离表征到最终层，混合或注意力方法需要架构试错来放置中间融合模块），缺乏一种结构化的连续跨模态学习机制。

Method: (1) 设计三流并行网络：RGB流、深度流和集成流，其中集成流通过LIConv2d算子在每层接收两个模态流的原始滤波信号并在非线性激活前进行融合（模拟神经元胞体整合机制）；(2) 提出1/N常数初始化替代Kaiming初始化以解决梯度病理；(3) 采用渐进模态丢弃（从零开始递增空白概率）防止路径坍塌。

Result: 在SUN RGB-D 19类场景分类上，从零训练的LINet（ResNet18规模）达到45.2%平均类别准确率，优于此前所有从零训练结果；采用ScanNet域内RGB-D预训练后提升至49.6%。

Conclusion: LINet通过连续跨模态集成算子和配套的训练策略（常数初始化、渐进模态丢弃）有效解决了多模态融合中的结构妥协问题，验证了逐层连续融合在RGB-D场景分类中的优势。

Original Abstract: We present LINet (Linear Integration Network), a Multi-Stream Neural Network (MSNN) for RGB-D scene classification. Current multi-modal architectures treat feature fusion as a discrete, ad-hoc event: early fusion entangles representations prematurely, late fusion isolates them until the final layer, and hybrid or attention-based methods require architectural guesswork to place intermediate fusion blocks. LINet addresses this structural compromise by maintaining three dedicated parallel streams (RGB, depth, and integration) where a novel Linear Integration Convolution (LIConv2d) operator enables continuous cross-modal learning at every layer. The integration stream receives raw filtered signals from both modality streams and combines them before the nonlinear activation threshold, conceptually inspired by somatic integration preceding the neuronal firing decision. Implementing continuous integration exposes a critical initialization pathology: Kaiming initialization of the bridging weights scrambles gradients before they reach the stream backbones, producing a failure mode that resembles overfitting but is corrupted gradient flow. A 1/N constant initialization mitigates this. We employ progressive modality dropout, a curriculum adapted to continuous fusion in which blanking probability increases from zero, preventing pathway collapse, a form of negative co-learning, by forcing robust independent stream representations. Trained from scratch on SUN RGB-D 19-class scene classification, LINet reaches 45.2% mean class accuracy at ResNet18 scale, outperforming prior from-scratch results, and rises to 49.6% with in-domain RGB-D (ScanNet) pretraining.

中文摘要翻译: 本文提出LINet（线性集成网络），一种用于RGB-D场景分类的多流神经网络（MSNN）。当前的多模态架构将特征融合视为离散的、启发式事件：早期融合过早纠缠表征，晚期融合将表征隔离直到最终层，而混合或基于注意力的方法则需要架构上的试错来确定中间融合模块的位置。LINet通过维护三个专用的并行流（RGB流、深度流和集成流）解决了这一结构妥协问题，其中一种新颖的线性集成卷积算子（LIConv2d）使每一层都能进行连续的跨模态学习。集成流从两个模态流接收原始滤波信号，并在非线性激活阈值之前将它们组合起来，概念上受到神经元胞体整合先于放电决策的启发。实现连续集成暴露了一个关键的初始化病理：桥接权重的Kaiming初始化在梯度到达流骨干网络之前就使其混乱，产生一种看似过拟合但实为梯度流损坏的失败模式。1/N常数初始化缓解了这一问题。我们采用渐进模态丢弃，这是一种适用于连续融合的课程策略，其中空白概率从零开始递增，通过迫使形成鲁棒的独立流表征来防止路径坍塌——一种负协同学习的形式。在SUN RGB-D 19类场景分类上从零训练的LINet在ResNet18规模下达到45.2%的平均类别准确率，优于此前所有从零训练的结果，并在使用域内RGB-D（ScanNet）预训练后提升至49.6%。

</details>


### [39] [FROST: Training-Free Few-Shot Segmentation with Frozen Features and Nonparametric Statistics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31136)
*Junghwan Park*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FROST是一种无需训练的小样本分割器，将参考前景和背景视为DINOv3特征球面上的两个点云，通过非参数密度比对查询令牌进行标注，在17个遥感基准上超越现有方法，且模型尺寸最小。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有小样本分割方法要么在标注片段上训练分割器（受限于训练分布），要么将类别退化为有损的特征摘要（如单原型、聚类原型等），无法保留多模态类别的内部结构。论文认为类别更适合用分布而非点来描述，且冻结的自监督特征已包含足够结构来直接估计该分布。

Method: FROST是一种无需训练的小样本分割方法。它将参考前景和背景视为冻结DINOv3特征单位球面上的两个点云，通过非参数密度比（核密度估计）对查询图像的每个令牌进行标注。贝叶斯规则在等先验条件下将决策阈值固定为零。随着参考样本增加，密度估计的方差减小，决策边界更加清晰。所有超参数（如核带宽、空间门控）均从支持集中读取而非调参。

Result: 在17个遥感基准上，FROST超越了所有无需训练和基于学习的方法。从单张标注示例开始就领先5.6 mIoU，且随着支持集增大优势进一步扩大，同时模型尺寸保持最小。

Conclusion: 类别更适合用分布而非点来描述，冻结的自监督特征已包含足够结构来直接估计分布；非参数密度比方法可以有效实现无需训练的小样本分割，并在遥感领域取得显著优势。

Original Abstract: Few-shot segmentation asks a model to delineate a target class in a query image from only a handful of annotated examples, a setting most acute in remote sensing, where labels are scarce and the imagery departs sharply from the natural images on which vision backbones are pretrained. Prevailing approaches either train a segmenter on labelled episodes, which raises accuracy within the training distribution but binds the model to it, or reduce each class to a lossy summary of frozen features, a single prototype, a few cluster prototypes, or a discrete clustering, none of which preserves the internal structure of a multimodal class. We argue that a class is better described by a distribution than by a point, and that frozen self-supervised features already carry enough structure to estimate that distribution directly. We introduce FROST, a training-free few-shot segmenter that treats the reference foreground and background as two point clouds on the unit sphere of frozen DINOv3 features and labels each query token by a nonparametric density ratio, with a threshold the Bayes rule fixes at zero under equal priors. Because the variance of a density estimate shrinks as its sample grows, the decision sharpens as references accumulate, and every remaining quantity from the kernel bandwidth to the spatial gate is read from the support set rather than tuned. We develop FROST for overhead imagery, where a class is typically a scatter of many small and dissimilar instances that a density tracks but a lossy summary blurs. Across seventeen remote-sensing benchmarks FROST surpasses both training-free and learning-based methods, leading by 5.6 mIoU from a single annotated example and widening its lead as the support set grows, all while remaining among the smallest models compared. Code is available at https://github.com/jhpark-ai/FROST.

中文摘要翻译: 小样本分割要求模型仅从少量标注示例中描绘查询图像中的目标类别，这一设定在遥感领域尤为严峻——该领域标签稀缺，且影像与视觉骨干网络预训练所用的自然图像差异显著。主流方法要么在标注片段上训练分割器（这在训练分布内提高了精度，但也将模型束缚于该分布），要么将每个类别退化为冻结特征的有损摘要——单个原型、少量聚类原型或离散聚类——这些方法都无法保留多模态类别的内部结构。我们认为，类别更适合用分布而非点来描述，且冻结的自监督特征已包含足够结构来直接估计该分布。本文提出FROST，一种无需训练的小样本分割器，它将参考前景和背景视为冻结DINOv3特征单位球面上的两个点云，通过非参数密度比对每个查询令牌进行标注，贝叶斯规则在等先验条件下将决策阈值固定为零。由于密度估计的方差随样本增长而缩小，决策边界随参考样本积累而愈加清晰，从核带宽到空间门控的所有剩余量均从支持集中读取而非调参。我们为遥感影像设计了FROST——在遥感场景中，一个类别通常由许多稀疏且异质的小实例散布而成，密度能够追踪这些实例，而有损摘要则会模糊它们。在17个遥感基准上，FROST超越了所有无需训练和基于学习的方法，从单张标注示例开始就领先5.6 mIoU，且随着支持集增大优势进一步扩大，同时模型尺寸在所有对比模型中保持最小。代码开源：https://github.com/jhpark-ai/FROST。

</details>


### [40] [WaterGen: Decoupling Scene and Medium in Underwater Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31147)
*Jiayi Wu,Tianfu Wang,Tianyi Xiong,Dehao Yuan,Xiaomin Lin,Md Jahidul Islam,Cornelia Fermuller,Christopher Metzler,Yiannis Aloimonos*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WaterGen提出了一种解耦式水下图像生成方法，将场景内容生成与水体介质效应分开建模，在潜扩散框架中实现多样化场景生成与可控水体效果的结合，并证明其合成数据能有效提升水下恢复与分割任务的下游性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有水下图像生成方法无法同时兼顾真实感、多样性和对水体介质效应的独立可控性——要么具备可控性但真实感或多样性有限，要么生成真实场景但无法准确独立地建模水体效应。

Method: 将水下图像合成分解为两个阶段：(1) 使用无退化的水下图像微调潜扩散U-Net，使其学习生成不含介质退化、多样且真实的水下场景潜编码；(2) 将物理准确的水体介质退化合成建模为作用于这些潜编码的条件解码过程，实现对水下外观的完全控制。

Result: 利用WaterGen构建的大规模合成水下数据集在场景结构上具有多样性，在水体效应和伪标签上具有准确性；这些合成数据能持续提升水下图像恢复和语义分割的下游性能。

Conclusion: 通过解耦场景生成与介质建模，WaterGen在保持生成多样性和真实感的同时，实现了对水下外观的精确可控，合成的数据对下游任务有实际增益。

Original Abstract: Underwater computer vision tasks, such as detection, restoration, and segmentation, are limited by the scarcity of large-scale and diverse training data. We introduce WaterGen, a method for generating large-scale, realistic, and diverse underwater images that provides independent control of the scene and water medium conditions. Our approach treats underwater image generation as the decoupled control of two factors: realistic and diverse scene content (what is in the image), and accurate and controllable water medium effects (what the water does to the image). Existing methods generally achieve only part of this objective: they either provide controllability with limited realism or diversity, or generate realistic scenes without accurately and independently modeling water-medium effects. Our key insight, that allows us to avoid this compromise, is that scene generation and medium modeling can be decoupled within a latent diffusion framework, enabling diverse scene generation together with accurate and controllable underwater appearance. To do this, we decompose underwater image synthesis into two stages. First, we fine-tune the latent diffusion U-Net using degradation-free underwater images so that it learns to generate diverse and realistic latent embeddings of underwater scene content without medium-induced degradation. Second, we formulate the physically accurate medium degradation synthesis as a conditional decoding process applied to these latent embeddings. This decoupled design allows our model to generate diverse scenes with full control of underwater appearance. We leverage WaterGen to build large-scale synthetic underwater datasets that are diverse in scene structures and accurate in water effects and pseudo-labels. We demonstrate that our synthetic data consistently improve downstream performance in underwater restoration and semantic segmentation.

中文摘要翻译: 水下计算机视觉任务（如检测、恢复和分割）受限于大规模且多样化训练数据的稀缺。本文提出WaterGen，一种生成大规模、真实且多样化的水下图像的方法，能够独立控制场景和水体介质条件。我们的方法将水下图像生成视为两个因素的解耦控制：真实且多样化的场景内容（图像中有什么）以及准确且可控的水体介质效应（水对图像做了什么）。现有方法通常只实现了部分目标：它们要么在有限真实感或多样性的前提下提供可控性，要么生成真实场景但无法准确独立地建模水体介质效应。我们的关键洞察——使我们能够避免这种折中——在于场景生成和介质建模可以在潜扩散框架内解耦，从而在实现多样化场景生成的同时获得准确且可控的水下外观。为此，我们将水下图像合成分解为两个阶段。首先，我们使用无退化的水下图像对潜扩散U-Net进行微调，使其学会生成多样化且真实的、不含介质退化的水下场景内容潜编码。其次，我们将物理准确的水体介质退化合成表述为作用于这些潜编码的条件解码过程。这种解耦设计使我们的模型能够生成多样化场景，同时完全控制水下外观。我们利用WaterGen构建大规模合成水下数据集，这些数据集在场景结构上多样，在水体效应和伪标签上准确。我们证明，这些合成数据能够持续提升水下恢复和语义分割的下游性能。

</details>


### [41] [PruneGround: Plug-and-play Spatial Pruning for 3D Visual Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31148)
*Duc Cao Dinh,Khai Le-Duc,Florent Draye,Chris Ngo,Terry Jingchen Zhang,Bernhard Schölkopf,Zhijing Jin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PruneGround是一个即插即用的3D视觉定位框架，通过语言引导的空间剪枝、多视角描述重构和基于LLM的定位器，在三个点云基准上实现了最先进的结果。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有3DVG方法通常对整个场景进行推理，导致预测模糊且计算成本高。而许多指代表达依赖局部空间上下文，仅对应有限的局部区域，因此有必要设计一种利用局部空间先验的高效定位方法。

Method: PruneGround包含三个核心组件：(1) 语言引导空间剪枝（LGSP），利用冻结的视觉语言模型识别语言相关区域；(2) 多视角条件描述重构（MCDR），将复杂表达式分解为简化的目标-锚点关系并通过多视角推理补充缺失的空间线索；(3) LLM-Grounder，将检测预训练的空间大语言模型重用于语言条件定位，在剪枝区域内对齐点云和语言表示。

Result: 在三个点云基准上，该方法在ScanRefer全部三个设置以及Nr3D/Sr3D的10个设置中的9个上取得了最先进的结果。

Conclusion: 通过语言引导的空间剪枝有效缩小搜索范围，结合多视角描述重构和LLM Grounder，实现了更精确高效的3D视觉定位。

Original Abstract: 3D Visual Grounding (3DVG) aims to localize target objects in 3D scenes given natural language descriptions. Existing approaches typically perform reasoning over the entire scene, leading to ambiguous predictions and high computational cost, especially in cluttered environments. We observe that many referential expressions rely on local spatial context and often correspond to restricted spatial regions rather than the full scene. Motivated by this insight, we propose PruneGround, an effective plug-and-play framework for 3DVG built upon three key components. First, we introduce Language-Guided Spatial Pruning (LGSP), which leverages a frozen Vision Language Model (VLM) to identify language-relevant regions, thereby reducing spatial computation and grounding candidates in the narrower search space. Second, we propose MultiView-Conditioned Description Reformulation (MCDR), which decomposes complex expressions into simplified target-anchor relations and augments missing spatial cues through multi-view reasoning. Finally, we propose LLM-Grounder, which repurposes a detection-pretrained spatial LLM into a language-conditioned grounding model by aligning point cloud and linguistic representations within the pruned region. Extensive experiments on the three most popular point cloud benchmarks demonstrate that our method achieves state-of-the-art results on all three ScanRefer settings and on 9 out of 10 Nr3D/Sr3D settings. Code and models are publicly available: https://github.com/leduckhai/PruneGround

中文摘要翻译: 3D视觉定位（3DVG）旨在根据自然语言描述在3D场景中定位目标物体。现有方法通常对整个场景进行推理，导致预测模糊且计算成本高，尤其在杂乱环境中。我们观察到，许多指代表达依赖于局部空间上下文，且往往对应受限的空间区域而非整个场景。基于这一洞见，我们提出PruneGround，一个有效的即插即用式3DVG框架，包含三个关键组件。首先，我们引入语言引导空间剪枝（LGSP），利用冻结的视觉语言模型（VLM）识别与语言相关的区域，从而减少空间计算并在更窄的搜索空间中定位候选对象。其次，我们提出多视角条件描述重构（MCDR），将复杂表达式分解为简化的目标-锚点关系，并通过多视角推理补充缺失的空间线索。最后，我们提出LLM-Grounder，通过在对齐剪枝区域内的点云和语言表示，将检测预训练的空间大语言模型（LLM）重用于语言条件定位。在三个最流行的点云基准上进行的大量实验表明，我们的方法在ScanRefer全部三个设置以及Nr3D/Sr3D的10个设置中的9个上取得了最先进的结果。代码和模型已公开：https://github.com/leduckhai/PruneGround

</details>


### [42] [Rethinking Foundation Model Collaboration: Enhancing Specialized Models through Proxy Task Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31157)
*Hongyi Lin,Yang Liu,Jinhua Zhao,Xiaobo Qu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出FAT框架，将基础模型与专用模型协作视为任务分解而非模型替代。专用模型生成结构化假设，基础模型通过上下文代理推理（如选择或验证）来优化假设，在多个感知任务上取得一致提升。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 基础模型在具身智能的结构化预测任务中能力不足（如几何和数值估计弱于专用模型），因此需要探索两者如何协作，而非直接用基础模型替代专用模型。

Method: 提出FAT（基础模型增强的任务专用推理框架），将结构化预测分解为三部分：1）专用模型在原生输出空间生成几何和物理有效的假设；2）信息空间重建，将假设转换为多模态候选表示；3）基础模型执行有界代理任务（如选择或验证），对重建后的多模态候选进行推理。实例化为ProxySelect，使用视觉-语言模型。

Result: 在2D目标检测、3D目标检测、轨迹预测和语义分割四个任务上，ProxySelect一致提升专用基线模型性能，且大幅优于直接使用基础模型回归，计算成本更低。

Conclusion: 专用模型保持任务特定结构，基础模型通过上下文代理推理优化假设，这是一种通用协作原则。

Original Abstract: Foundation models are increasingly integrated into embodied intelligence systems, but directly assigning them structured prediction tasks requires precise geometric and numerical estimation, where specialized models often remain stronger. This capability mismatch raises a key question: should foundation models replace task-specific predictors, or should they collaborate through tasks better aligned with their strengths? We propose FAT, a foundation-model-augmented task-specific reasoning framework that treats collaboration as task decomposition rather than model replacement. FAT decomposes structured prediction into specialist prediction, information-space reconstruction, and foundation-model proxy reasoning. The specialist generates geometrically and physically valid hypotheses in the native output space, while the foundation model performs a bounded proxy task, such as selection or verification, over reconstructed multimodal candidates. We instantiate this principle as ProxySelect with a vision--language model. Across 2D object detection, 3D object detection, trajectory prediction, and semantic segmentation, ProxySelect consistently improves specialized baselines and substantially outperforms direct foundation-model regression at lower computational cost. These results suggest a general collaboration principle: specialized models preserve task-specific structure, while foundation models refine their hypotheses through contextual proxy reasoning.

中文摘要翻译: 基础模型正日益被集成到具身智能系统中，但直接分配结构化预测任务给它们需要精确的几何和数值估计，而专用模型在这些方面往往仍然更强。这种能力不匹配引发了一个关键问题：基础模型应该取代任务专用预测器，还是应该通过与其优势更契合的任务进行协作？我们提出FAT，一个基础模型增强的任务专用推理框架，将协作视为任务分解而非模型替代。FAT将结构化预测分解为专用预测、信息空间重建和基础模型代理推理。专用模型在原生输出空间中生成几何和物理有效的假设，而基础模型则在重建的多模态候选上执行有界的代理任务（如选择或验证）。我们将该原则实例化为ProxySelect，使用视觉-语言模型。在2D目标检测、3D目标检测、轨迹预测和语义分割四个任务上，ProxySelect一致提升了专用基线模型，并在较低计算成本下大幅优于直接使用基础模型进行回归。这些结果表明了一个通用协作原则：专用模型保持任务特定结构，而基础模型通过上下文代理推理来优化它们的假设。

</details>


### [43] [Reasoning-aware Speculative Decoding for Efficient Vision-Language-Action Models in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31160)
*Anh Dung Dinh,Simon Khan,Flora Salim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出双推理器系统（Routine Reasoner + Deliberative Reasoner），通过FlatRoPE和Action-aware RL两项技术，将VLA自动驾驶规划器的因果推理链推理时间加速约4倍。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: VLA自动驾驶规划器需要先进行自回归因果推理链（CoC）再生成轨迹，但推理步骤延迟高，成为自动驾驶操作约束下的瓶颈。现有方法将所有token同等对待，忽略了大多数token只是常规延续、少数token需要新视觉信息这一差异。

Method: 将推理拆分为两条专用路径：(1) Routine Reasoner处理常规延续，关注轨迹历史token；(2) Deliberative Reasoner（未修改的VLA目标模型）处理意外情况，关注当前视觉证据。采用推测解码架构让两者协作。提出FlatRoPE（一维旋转位置编码打破目标模型三维M-RoPE的旋转对称性，将注意力从视觉token重定向到轨迹历史token）和Action-aware RL（AARL，使用动作质量奖励+静态参考KL锚点的后训练阶段）。

Result: 双推理器系统相比原始Alpamayo规划器，推理步骤运行时间减少约4倍。

Conclusion: 通过将推理拆分为常规延续（Routine）和需要新证据的深思熟虑（Deliberative）两条专用路径，结合FlatRoPE和AARL技术，可在不修改目标模型的情况下大幅加速VLA规划器的推理过程。

Original Abstract: Modern Vision-Language-Action (VLA) planners for autonomous driving emit a chain-of-causation (CoC) reasoning step \emph{before} producing a trajectory. The reasoning is autoregressive and dominates inference latency, while the trajectory head is parallel and cheap. Latency is an operational constraint in autonomous driving, so accelerating the reasoning step is the central problem we address. We observe that CoC reasoning has two qualitatively different needs: most tokens continue routine setup that follows naturally from the ego-trajectory history, and a small fraction encode commitments that require fresh visual evidence about an unexpected situation. We split this reasoning into two specialized paths: a \emph{routine reasoner} that handles the predictable continuation by attending to trajectory history, and a \emph{deliberative reasoner} (the unmodified VLA target) that handles novel cases by attending to current visual evidence, using the speculative decoding framework as the architectural template for how the two paths cooperate. Unlike standard speculative decoding, our routine reasoner is not a smaller replica of the target; the two reasoners are deliberately specialized to read different parts of the prompt. We propose two techniques to realize this. First, we introduce \textbf{FlatRoPE}, a 1D rotary positional embedding in the draft that breaks the rotational symmetry of the target's 3D M-RoPE, redirecting attention away from visual tokens and onto trajectory-history tokens. Second, we introduce \textbf{Action-aware RL (AARL)}, a post-training stage that uses an action-quality reward together with a static-reference KL anchor. Together, our two-reasoner system reduces the reasoning-step running time by approximately $4\times$ relative to the original Alpamayo planner.

中文摘要翻译: 现代的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）自动驾驶规划器在生成轨迹之前，会先输出一个因果推理链（CoC）。该推理过程是自回归的，主导了推理延迟，而轨迹头是并行的且计算成本低廉。延迟是自动驾驶的操作性约束，因此加速推理步骤是我们解决的核心问题。我们观察到，CoC推理存在两种性质上不同的需求：大部分token延续的是常规设置，自然跟随自车轨迹历史；少数token编码的是需要对意外情况获取新视觉证据的承诺。我们将这一推理拆分为两条专用路径：一个\textbf{常规推理器}（routine reasoner），通过关注轨迹历史来处理可预测的延续；一个\textbf{深思熟虑推理器}（deliberative reasoner，即未经修改的VLA目标模型），通过关注当前视觉证据来处理新异情况，并采用推测解码框架作为两条路径协作的架构模板。与标准推测解码不同，我们的常规推理器并非目标模型的更小副本；两个推理器被有意专门化，以读取提示的不同部分。我们提出两项技术来实现这一点。首先，我们引入\textbf{FlatRoPE}，一种草案模型中的一维旋转位置编码，打破目标模型三维M-RoPE的旋转对称性，将注意力从视觉token重定向到轨迹历史token。其次，我们引入\textbf{动作感知强化学习（AARL）}，一个使用动作质量奖励和静态参考KL锚点的后训练阶段。综上所述，我们的双推理器系统相比原始Alpamayo规划器，推理步骤运行时间减少了约4倍。

</details>


### [44] [Seeing Through the Weights: Privacy Leakage in Scene Coordinate Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31164)
*Oleksii Nasypanyi,Jaemin Cho,Utku Ozbulak,Byungkon Kang,Francois Rameau*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现场景坐标回归（SCR）模型虽然被认为具有隐私保护特性，但实际上可以通过查询攻击从模型中重建训练环境的3D几何结构甚至外观信息，揭示了此类方法在实际部署中的隐私泄露风险。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有文献声称SCR方法通过将场景隐式编码在神经网络参数中而非显式存储图像或地图，因此具有隐私保护特性。本文旨在证明这一假设在实际中是不成立的，揭示SCR模型存在的隐私泄露风险。

Method: 提出一种基于查询的攻击方法：1）使用与目标场景无关的代理图像批量查询SCR模型，获取密集的像素级3D坐标；2）通过小输入扰动下的稳定性筛选可靠点，在白盒设置中进一步精炼；3）跨独立查询批次累积稳定点以恢复场景几何结构；4）从恢复的3D表示中反转网络特征，从任意视角合成图像以恢复外观信息。

Result: 在室内和室外数据集上的实验表明，该方法能够以高几何保真度重建训练环境的大部分区域，并能恢复近似的颜色外观，暴露可识别的布局和潜在的敏感场景元素。

Conclusion: SCR表示并非天然具有隐私保护特性，当此类系统部署在私人或安全关键空间中时存在真实的安全风险，直接反驳了文献中声称SCR表示具有隐私保护特性的观点。

Original Abstract: Scene Coordinate Regression (SCR) methods are increasingly adopted for visual localization. In these approaches, the scene is implicitly encoded within a neural network that regresses a 3D world coordinate for each image pixel. Because the scene is represented only through the network parameters and not stored explicitly as images or maps, such methods are often assumed to be privacy-preserving. In this work, we show that this assumption is incorrect in practice.
  Specifically, we introduce a query-based attack that reconstructs the 3D geometry of the training environment from an SCR model under different levels of model access. To do so, we repeatedly query the model with batches of proxy images unrelated to the target scene to obtain dense pixel-wise 3D coordinates. Reliable points are identified through their stability under small input perturbations and can be further refined in a white-box setting. These stable points are accumulated across independent query batches to recover the scene geometry. From the recovered 3D representation, we also invert the network features to synthesize images from arbitrary viewpoints, revealing additional appearance information.
  Experiments on indoor and outdoor datasets demonstrate that substantial portions of training environments can be reconstructed with high geometric fidelity. Beyond geometry, we also recover an approximate color appearance, which exposes recognizable layout and potentially sensitive scene elements. This directly contradicts claims in the literature that SCR representations are privacy-preserving by design, and reveals a real risk when such systems are deployed in private or security-critical spaces. The project page is available at https://jaeminch0.github.io/seeing-through-the-weights-privacy-leakage-in-scene-coordinate-regression.

中文摘要翻译: 场景坐标回归（SCR）方法越来越多地被用于视觉定位。在这些方法中，场景被隐式编码在一个神经网络中，该网络为每个图像像素回归一个3D世界坐标。由于场景仅通过网络参数表示，而不以图像或地图的形式显式存储，这类方法通常被认为具有隐私保护特性。在本文中，我们证明这一假设在实际中并不成立。具体而言，我们引入了一种基于查询的攻击方法，能够在不同模型访问级别下从SCR模型中重建训练环境的3D几何结构。为此，我们反复使用与目标场景无关的代理图像批次查询模型，以获取密集的逐像素3D坐标。通过小输入扰动下的稳定性识别可靠点，并在白盒设置中进一步精炼这些点。这些稳定点跨独立查询批次累积，以恢复场景几何结构。从恢复的3D表示出发，我们还反转网络特征以从任意视角合成图像，揭示额外的外观信息。在室内和室外数据集上的实验表明，训练环境的大部分区域可以以高几何保真度重建。除了几何结构外，我们还能恢复近似的颜色外观，暴露可识别的布局和潜在的敏感场景元素。这直接反驳了文献中声称SCR表示天然具有隐私保护特性的说法，并揭示了当此类系统部署在私人或安全关键空间时存在的真实风险。项目页面见 https://jaeminch0.github.io/seeing-through-the-weights-privacy-leakage-in-scene-coordinate-regression。

</details>


### [45] [Beyond Single Character: Evaluating MLLMs for Sentence-Level Oracle Bone Inscription Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31169)
*Ziqi Li,Zijian Chen,Tingzhu Chen,Guangtao Zhai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了S-OBI基准，用于评估多模态大语言模型在句子级别的甲骨文理解能力。通过字形替换和组合生成清晰的句子级甲骨文实例，并设计了语义匹配、语义槽提取和上下文推理任务。实验表明现有MLLMs在句子级甲骨文理解上表现不佳，视觉感知错误会传播导致预测错误。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有AI辅助甲骨文研究主要集中在字符级别，忽略了完整占卜刻辞中的长文本连贯性和上下文依赖关系。

Method: 1. 从95片带有专家验证翻译的原始拓片中，用来自现有OBI数据集的清晰字形样本替换原始拓片中的字符，同时保留整体铭文结构和语义组织。2. 基于生成的句子级实例，设计语义匹配、语义槽提取和上下文推理三类任务，构建695个问答对。

Result: 当代MLLMs在句子级甲骨文理解上表现不佳，特别是在掩码区域，未掩码区域的视觉感知错误会沿着推理链传播，导致掩码字符的预测错误，表明当前模型的句子级理解仍然强烈依赖于字符级识别。

Conclusion: S-OBI提供了一个诊断性基准，用于评估MLLMs能否超越孤立的字符识别，转向结构化的铭文级理解。

Original Abstract: Existing AI-assisted oracle bone inscription (OBI) visual recognition and understanding studies mainly focus on character-level, ignoring the long-form textual coherence and contextual dependencies embedded in complete divination charges. Recently, the powerful visual perception capabilities of multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have opened new possibilities for OBI information processing. In this work, we introduce S-OBI, a novel benchmark for evaluating MLLMs in Sentence-level OBI understanding. Instead of using noisy and incomplete rubbings as the visual input, S-OBI synthesizes clear and standardized sentence-level OBI instances through glyph substitution and composition. According to 95 original rubbings with translations that have been identified, corrected, and verified by experts, we replace characters in the original rubbings with corresponding clean glyph samples sourced from existing OBI datasets while preserving the overall inscriptional structure and semantic organization. This mitigates the influence of low-level distortions and enables a more focused evaluation of sentence-level OBI understanding. Based on this, we design semantic matching, semantic slot extraction, and contextual reasoning tasks and obtain 695 question-answer pairs. Experiments reveal the inferiority of contemporary MLLMs on sentence-level OBI understanding. In particular, visual perception errors in unmasked regions propagate through the reasoning chain, leading to erroneous predictions for masked characters, which indicates that sentence-level OBI understanding in current models remains strongly dependent on character-level recognition. Overall, S-OBI provides a diagnostic benchmark for evaluating whether MLLMs can move beyond isolated character recognition toward structured inscription-level understanding.

中文摘要翻译: 现有的AI辅助甲骨文视觉识别与理解研究主要集中在字符级别，忽略了完整占卜刻辞中蕴含的长文本连贯性和上下文依赖关系。最近，多模态大语言模型强大的视觉感知能力为甲骨文信息处理开辟了新的可能性。在本工作中，我们提出了S-OBI，一个用于评估多模态大语言模型在句子级甲骨文理解能力的新型基准。S-OBI不使用嘈杂且不完整的拓片作为视觉输入，而是通过字形替换与组合合成清晰且标准化的句子级甲骨文实例。根据95片经专家鉴定、校正和验证的原始拓片及其译文，我们使用来自现有甲骨文数据集的对应清晰字形样本替换原始拓片中的字符，同时保留整体的铭文结构与语义组织。这减轻了低级扭曲的影响，从而能够更集中地评估句子级甲骨文理解能力。在此基础上，我们设计了语义匹配、语义槽提取和上下文推理任务，并获得了695个问答对。实验揭示了当代多模态大语言模型在句子级甲骨文理解上的不足。特别是，未掩码区域中的视觉感知错误会沿着推理链传播，导致对掩码字符的错误预测，这表明当前模型在句子级甲骨文理解中仍然强烈依赖于字符级识别。总体而言，S-OBI提供了一个诊断性基准，用于评估多模态大语言模型能否超越孤立的字符识别，转向结构化的铭文级理解。

</details>


### [46] [HSDF-Lane: Height-Aligned Signed Distance Field with Semantic Lane Prior for 3D Lane Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31172)
*Jiyong Boo,Byeongin Joung,Hyemin Yang,Kuk-Jin Yoon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HSDF-Lane通过隐式建模高度对齐的符号距离场（HSDF）并结合车道感知语义位置编码（LSPE），在单目3D车道检测中达到SOTA性能，同时实现了精确的高度图估计和几何-语义联合建模。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有单目3D车道检测方法存在两个主要局限：基于平面假设的BEV投影方法会导致实际道路上的几何畸变；而近年来的显式高度图方法仍依赖稀疏锚点回归，且恢复的几何信息仅用于空间变换而非语义理解。为了克服这些局限，作者提出了一种隐式联合建模几何结构与语义信息的方法。

Method: 提出HSDF-Lane框架，主要包含两个创新：1) 在密集采样的3D特征体上隐式建模高度对齐的符号距离场（HSDF），通过可微渲染联合生成精确的高度图和表面对齐的特征；2) 提出车道感知语义位置编码（LSPE），将源自表面对齐特征的车道存在先验注入Transformer查询中，实现几何结构与语义引导的耦合。

Result: 在OpenLane基准上，HSDF-Lane在3D车道检测和高度图估计两项任务上均达到最优（SOTA）性能。

Conclusion: 通过隐式符号距离场对道路表面进行连续建模，并结合语义先验的位置编码，HSDF-Lane有效解决了单目3D车道检测中几何失真与几何-语义分离的问题，证明了联合建模几何结构与语义信息对于提升3D车道检测性能的重要性。

Original Abstract: Monocular 3D lane detection plays a critical role in autonomous driving, yet recovering reliable 3D geometry from a single image remains challenging due to inherent depth ambiguity. Prior methods project image features into Bird's-Eye-View (BEV) space under a flat-ground assumption, causing geometric distortion on real-world roads. Recent methods instead predict explicit height maps to capture non-planar surfaces, but still rely on sparse anchor-based regression and exploit the recovered geometry merely for spatial transformation rather than semantic understanding. To overcome these limitations, we propose HSDF-Lane, which implicitly models the road surface as a Height-aligned Signed Distance Field (HSDF) over a densely sampled 3D feature volume. Through differentiable rendering, the HSDF jointly produces an accurate height map and surface-aligned features. We further introduce Lane-aware Semantic Positional Encoding (LSPE), which injects a lane-existence prior derived from the surface-aligned features into the transformer queries, coupling geometric structure with semantic guidance. Extensive experiments on the OpenLane benchmark show that HSDF-Lane achieves state-of-the-art performance in both 3D lane detection and height map estimation.

中文摘要翻译: 单目3D车道检测在自动驾驶中起着关键作用，但由于固有的深度模糊性，从单张图像中恢复可靠的3D几何结构仍然具有挑战性。先前的方法在平面地面假设下将图像特征投影到鸟瞰图（BEV）空间中，这会导致实际道路上出现几何畸变。近年来的方法转而预测显式高度图以捕获非平面地表，但仍然依赖于稀疏的锚点回归，且所恢复的几何结构仅用于空间变换而非语义理解。为克服这些局限，我们提出了HSDF-Lane，该方法在密集采样的3D特征体上隐式地将道路表面建模为高度对齐的符号距离场（HSDF）。通过可微渲染，HSDF联合生成了精确的高度图和表面对齐的特征。我们进一步引入了车道感知语义位置编码（LSPE），该方法将源自表面对齐特征的车道存在先验注入到Transformer查询中，从而将几何结构与语义引导耦合起来。在OpenLane基准上的大量实验表明，HSDF-Lane在3D车道检测和高度图估计两项任务上均达到了最优性能。

</details>


### [47] [GaussianMap: Learning Gaussian Representation for Multi-Sensor Online HD Map Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31177)
*Hongyu Lyu,Julie Stephany Berrio Perez,Mao Shan,Stewart Worrall*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GaussianMap提出了一种基于自适应高斯场景表示的在线高精地图构建框架，通过可学习的高斯基元替代传统固定分辨率BEV网格，在nuScenes和Argoverse 2数据集上实现了相机-only和相机-激光雷达融合设置下的最优性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统方法采用固定分辨率的密集BEV网格表示场景，但地图元素在空间上是稀疏的且需要精细的几何定位，这种均匀分配的表示方式存在冗余且对矢量化地图预测效果不佳。

Method: 提出GaussianMap框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) 可学习的高斯场景表示，由一组位于BEV平面上的高斯基元构成，每个基元编码局部区域的几何属性和特征向量；(2) 前馈式高斯编码器，通过高斯交互建模和多传感器特征聚合逐步优化基元；(3) 高斯散射模块，将优化后的高斯表示投射为BEV特征图并解码为矢量化地图预测。

Result: 在nuScenes和Argoverse 2数据集上的大量实验表明，GaussianMap在相机-only和相机-激光雷达融合设置下均达到了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: 自适应高斯表示能够有效分配模型容量到地图相关区域，克服了固定分辨率BEV网格的冗余问题，为在线高精地图构建提供了一种更高效、更精确的方案。

Original Abstract: Autonomous driving systems benefit from high-definition (HD) maps that provide critical information about road infrastructure. The online construction of HD maps offers a scalable approach to generate local vectorized maps from onboard sensor observations. Existing methods commonly adopt bird's-eye-view (BEV) features as the intermediate scene representation, encoding the surrounding space with fixed-resolution dense grids. However, map elements are spatially sparse yet require fine-grained geometric localization, making uniformly allocated BEV representations redundant and less effective for vectorized map prediction. In this work, we propose GaussianMap, an online HD map construction framework that learns an adaptive Gaussian representation of the surrounding scene. This representation consists of a set of Gaussian primitives on the BEV plane, each encoding a flexible local region with geometric properties and a feature vector, allowing the model to allocate representational capacity to map-relevant regions. To generate such a representation from sensor observations, we introduce a feed-forward Gaussian encoder that progressively refines these primitives through Gaussian interaction modeling and multi-sensor feature aggregation. The refined Gaussian representation is then splatted into a BEV feature map and decoded into vectorized map predictions. Extensive experiments on nuScenes and Argoverse 2 datasets demonstrate that GaussianMap achieves state-of-the-art performance in both camera-only and camera-LiDAR fusion settings. Our code will be made publicly available.

中文摘要翻译: 自动驾驶系统受益于高精地图（HD maps）提供的道路基础设施关键信息。在线构建高精地图提供了一种可扩展的方法，从车载传感器观测中生成局部矢量化地图。现有方法通常采用鸟瞰图（BEV）特征作为中间场景表示，使用固定分辨率的密集网格对周围空间进行编码。然而，地图元素在空间上是稀疏的，同时需要细粒度的几何定位，这使得均匀分配的BEV表示存在冗余，且对矢量化地图预测效果不佳。本文提出GaussianMap，一种在线高精地图构建框架，学习周围场景的自适应高斯表示。该表示由一组位于BEV平面上的高斯基元构成，每个基元编码一个具有几何属性和特征向量的灵活局部区域，使模型能够将表示能力分配到与地图相关的区域。为从传感器观测中生成这种表示，我们引入了一个前馈式高斯编码器，通过高斯交互建模和多传感器特征聚合逐步优化这些基元。优化后的高斯表示随后被散射（splatting）为BEV特征图，并解码为矢量化地图预测。在nuScenes和Argoverse 2数据集上的大量实验表明，GaussianMap在仅使用相机以及相机-激光雷达融合设置下均达到了最先进的性能。我们的代码将公开提供。

</details>


### [48] [Learning to Deny: Action Denial in Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31187)
*Raiyaan Abdullah,Shehreen Azad,Yogesh Singh Rawat*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出UCF101-AD基准数据集，用于评估多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）对动作否定（Action Denial）的识别能力。实验发现20个SOTA模型在肯定动作上准确率超85%，但在否定场景下均低于50%，暴露出模型无法因果推理动作是否真实发生的严重缺陷。作者提出CausalAct因果图公式，通过自然语言提示注入因果线索，显著降低误报率。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有MLLMs在视频理解中表现出色，但缺乏对“否定”场景的评估——即尽管上下文线索强烈暗示某个动作可能发生，但该动作实际上并未发生。作者旨在揭示模型在否认动作方面的盲点，并推动因果推理能力的研究。

Method: 构建UCF101-AD基准，为每个肯定视频配对一个否定视频，两者保留相同的人物、物体、地点和运动线索，仅缺失定义性动作。提出CausalAct因果图公式，通过自然语言提示关联场景、交互和运动，引导模型进行因果推理。

Result: 20个SOTA MLLMs在肯定动作上准确率超85%，但在否定动作上全部低于50%（部分模型仅10%左右）。引入CausalAct因果线索后，假阳性率显著降低，证明否定是一种可学习的推理技能。

Conclusion: 现代视频理解模型存在严重的因果推理缺陷——倾向于确认合理动作而非验证动作是否真实发生。UCF101-AD为诊断和提升多模态模型的因果推理能力提供了新视角和评估工具。

Original Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have rapidly advanced video understanding, achieving strong zero-shot and few-shot recognition across standard benchmarks. Yet their ability to deny an action by recognizing when an activity is not happening despite strong contextual cues remains largely unexplored. We introduce UCF101-AD, a large-scale benchmark consisting of paired Action-Presence and Action-Denial clips, designed to evaluate this capacity for denial. Each negative video in UCF101-AD preserves the same contextual and motion cues, including persons, objects, and locations, as its positive counterpart, but the defining action itself is explicitly absent. Evaluating 20 state-of-the-art MLLMs reveals a consistent failure: models that exceed 85% accuracy on the positive action classes collapse below 50% on their action-denial counterparts, indicating a strong inclination to affirm plausible actions rather than verify that they truly occur. This exposes a critical blind spot in modern video understanding: the inability to reason causally about whether a motion actually happens. To probe this issue, we explore a causal graph formulation, CausalAct, which expresses scene structure through natural-language prompts linking context, interaction, and motion. Incorporating such causal cues substantially reduces false positives, demonstrating that denial is a learnable reasoning skill. UCF101-AD provides a new lens for diagnosing and improving causal reasoning in multimodal models. Dataset and relevant code: https://github.com/raiyaan-abdullah/Learn-to-Deny.

中文摘要翻译: 多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在视频理解方面取得了快速进展，在标准基准上实现了强大的零样本和少样本识别能力。然而，它们通过识别尽管存在强烈上下文线索但活动并未发生来否认一个动作的能力仍基本未被探索。我们提出了UCF101-AD，一个大规模基准数据集，由成对的“动作存在”和“动作否定”视频片段组成，旨在评估这种否定能力。UCF101-AD中的每个负视频与其正视频保留了相同的上下文和运动线索，包括人物、物体和地点，但定义性动作本身被明确地缺失。对20个最先进的MLLMs进行评估揭示了一个一致性的失败：在肯定动作类别上准确率超过85%的模型，在其对应的动作否定类别上准确率跌至50%以下，表明模型强烈倾向于确认看似合理的动作，而不是验证它们是否真实发生。这暴露了现代视频理解中的一个关键盲点：缺乏对运动是否实际发生进行因果推理的能力。为了探究这一问题，我们探索了一种因果图公式CausalAct，它通过自然语言提示表达场景结构，将上下文、交互和运动联系起来。融入这类因果线索显著减少了假阳性，表明否定是一种可学习的推理技能。UCF101-AD为诊断和改进多模态模型中的因果推理提供了新视角。数据集及相关代码见：https://github.com/raiyaan-abdullah/Learn-to-Deny。

</details>


### [49] [Distilling Temporal Coherence into 2D Networks for Transrectal Ultrasound Prostate Video Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31198)
*Dong Yeong Kim,JunGyu Lee,Jaewon Choi,June Young Seo,Myeongseop Kim,Jinwook Choi,Taek Min Kim,Young-Gon Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种时序一致性学习框架（DTC-TRUS），通过将时间一致性蒸馏到2D网络中，在保持单帧推理效率的同时实现实时前列腺超声视频分割，并引入置信加权时序一致性损失和双尺度原型对齐模块克服不稳定区域干扰，在SUN-SEG和TRUS-V基准上达到最先进水平。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统2D方法忽略时序上下文导致帧间不一致，而3D方法延迟过高无法满足实时需求；同时，前列腺周围声学环境因生理运动和探头压力而波动，直接施加时序约束会从不稳定区域传播错误梯度，亟需一种兼顾效率与时序一致性的方法。

Method: (1) 置信加权时序一致性目标：基于光流残差计算每个像素的置信度，选择性衰减不稳定区域的梯度贡献；(2) 双尺度原型对齐模块：通过对比学习分别优化局部边界和全局语义特征的原型一致性；(3) 基于几何等变性的伪标签生成与知识蒸馏，避免逐帧密集标注需求。

Result: 在SUN-SEG和自建TRUS-V基准（2,679帧）上，该方法在实时推理速度下实现了最先进的分割精度和时序一致性。

Conclusion: 所提出的时序一致性学习框架有效解决了2D网络帧间不一致与3D网络高延迟之间的矛盾，在临床实时经直肠超声前列腺分割中表现出优越的精度和时序稳定性。

Original Abstract: Real-time video segmentation of the prostate in Transrectal Ultrasound (TRUS) is essential for image-guided interventions. While conventional 2D methods suffer from inter-frame inconsistencies by disregarding temporal context, 3D architectures incur prohibitive latency. To resolve this dilemma, we present a Temporally Consistent Learning Framework that distills temporal coherence into a 2D network during training, preserving single-frame inference efficiency. Our design is driven by a key clinical observation: the prostate exhibits geometric stability, whereas the surrounding acoustic environment fluctuates due to physiological motion and transducer pressure. Because conventional temporal constraints propagate erroneous gradients from these unstable regions, we introduce a Confidence-Weighted Temporal Consistency objective derived from optical flow warping residuals, selectively attenuating contributions from unreliable regions. Complementing this pixel-wise constraint, a Dual-scale Prototype Alignment Module enforces semantic coherence through contrastive optimization of local boundary and global semantic features. Furthermore, to eliminate the need for dense per-frame video annotations, we employ geometric equivariance-based pseudo-labeling with knowledge distillation from a pretrained teacher. Extensive experiments on SUN-SEG and our newly introduced TRUS-V benchmark (2,679 frames) demonstrate state-of-the-art accuracy and temporal consistency at real-time speed. Code and dataset are available at https://github.com/DYDevelop/DTC-TRUS.

中文摘要翻译: 经直肠超声中前列腺的实时视频分割对于图像引导介入至关重要。尽管传统2D方法因忽略时序上下文而存在帧间不一致问题，3D架构则会产生难以承受的延迟。为了解决这一困境，我们提出了一种时序一致性学习框架，在训练时将时序一致性蒸馏到2D网络中，从而保持单帧推理效率。我们的设计源于一个关键的临床观察：前列腺具有几何稳定性，而周围声学环境因生理运动和换能器压力而波动。由于传统时序约束会从不稳定区域传播错误梯度，我们引入了基于光流残差的置信加权时序一致性目标，选择性地衰减不可靠区域的贡献。作为这一像素级约束的补充，双尺度原型对齐模块通过对比优化局部边界和全局语义特征来强制执行语义一致性。此外，为消除对密集逐帧视频标注的需求，我们采用了基于几何等变性的伪标签生成，并结合从预训练教师模型进行的知识蒸馏。在SUN-SEG和我们新引入的TRUS-V基准（2,679帧）上的大量实验表明，该方法在实时速度下达到了最先进的精度和时序一致性。代码和数据集可在 https://github.com/DYDevelop/DTC-TRUS 获取。

</details>


### [50] [ExPLoRe: Expert Patch-Level Loss Routing for Multi-Objective Masked Image Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31201)
*Konstantinos Georgiou,Maofeng Tang,Hairong Qi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ExPLoRe利用Soft MoE的调度权重作为每patch可学习的损失系数，通过损失耦合机制实现多目标掩码图像建模中内容相关的空间自适应损失加权，在ImageNet-1K和ADE20K上取得优于非MoE基线的性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有MIM方法使用全局标量加权多目标损失，忽略了不同图像块之间的空间异质性，即不同patch在不同目标上应有不同的侧重。

Method: 将Soft MoE的调度权重重新用作每patch可学习的损失系数，并允许损失梯度通过调度权重反向传播到router，从而实现损失耦合（loss-coupling），使不同patch在不同目标上获得差异化权重。

Result: 在ImageNet-1K上，ViT-Base在Token+CLS组合下k-NN提升0.5%、线性探针提升4.4%；Token+Pixel组合下k-NN提升2.2%，线性探针达80.6%，微调精度85.3%。消融实验证实损失耦合是关键机制，阻断梯度后性能下降1.6%。在下游迁移中，提出的适应策略使MoE微调提升1.5%，在ADE20K上弥合2.5-2.9 mIoU分割差距。

Conclusion: ExPLoRe通过损失耦合的Soft MoE调度权重实现了内容相关的逐patch自适应损失加权，有效提升了多目标MIM的性能，并通过下游适应策略使MoE模型在分割任务上达到或超越非MoE基线。

Original Abstract: Multi-objective masked image modeling (MIM) combines complementary learning signals (token distillation, CLS alignment, and pixel reconstruction) but existing methods weight these objectives with global scalars, ignoring spatial heterogeneity across patches. We present ExPLoRe (Expert Patch-Level Loss Routing), which repurposes Soft Mixture of Experts (MoE) dispatch weights as learned, per-patch loss coefficients. The key mechanism is loss-coupling: allowing loss gradients to flow through dispatch weights to the router enables content-dependent specialization, where different patches receive different emphases across objectives. A detach ablation confirms loss-coupling as the core mechanism, degrading performance by 1.6% when gradients are blocked. On ImageNet-1K with ViT-Base, ExPLoRe improves over non-MoE baselines on two objective combinations (Token+CLS: +0.5% k-NN, +4.4% linear probe; Token+Pixel: +2.2% k-NN), achieving 80.6% linear probe and 85.3% finetuning accuracy, competitive with published methods. For downstream transfer, we develop adaptation recipes (Freeze Routing, Expert Dropout, and Freeze Attention) that improve MoE finetuning by +1.5% over the vanilla MoE, and close a 2.5--2.9 mIoU segmentation gap so that MoE models match or exceed non-MoE baselines on ADE20K.

中文摘要翻译: 多目标掩码图像建模（MIM）结合了互补的学习信号（Token蒸馏、CLS对齐和像素重建），但现有方法使用全局标量对这些目标进行加权，忽略了不同图像块之间的空间异质性。我们提出了ExPLoRe（专家级逐块损失路由），该方法将Soft混合专家（MoE）的调度权重重新用作可学习的、逐块的损失系数。其关键机制是损失耦合：允许损失梯度通过调度权重反向传播至路由器，从而实现内容相关的专业化，即不同图像块在不同目标上获得不同的侧重。消融实验中阻断梯度后性能下降1.6%，证实了损失耦合是核心机制。在ImageNet-1K上使用ViT-Base，ExPLoRe在两种目标组合下均优于非MoE基线（Token+CLS：k-NN提升0.5%，线性探针提升4.4%；Token+Pixel：k-NN提升2.2%），线性探针达到80.6%，微调精度达到85.3%，与已发表方法具有竞争力。在下游迁移方面，我们开发了适应策略（冻结路由、专家丢弃和冻结注意力），使MoE微调相比原始MoE提升1.5%，并在ADE20K上弥合了2.5-2.9 mIoU的分割差距，使MoE模型达到或超过非MoE基线。

</details>


### [51] [AC3S: Adaptive Conditioning for 3D-Aware Synthetic Data Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31204)
*Eric Ji,Qiran Hu,Wufei Ma,Sarthak Jain,Yingying Li,Minh N. Do,Yaoyao Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出AC3S框架，通过自适应条件调制和基于多智能体视觉语言模型的3D感知提示生成，在保持照片真实感的同时实现精确3D结构对齐，用于高质量合成数据集的可扩展生成。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于扩散模型的图像生成方法虽能生成逼真图像，但在精确控制3D结构和姿态一致性方面存在困难；利用边缘图等视觉提示引导扩散模型的方法常因过度条件化导致伪影，损害图像真实感和数据集质量。

Method: 提出AC3S框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) 自监督视觉提示调制器，动态调整ControlNet条件化强度，防止过度条件化，保留扩散模型的生成表达能力；(2) 多智能体视觉语言模型框架，编写与底层几何结构对齐的、具有3D感知能力的详细提示。

Result: 大量实验表明，该方法显著提升了图像质量和下游任务效用，能够生成具有精确2D和3D标注的高质量合成数据集。

Conclusion: 自适应条件化机制能够有效平衡3D结构对齐与照片真实感，多智能体VLM框架可增强语义一致性和多样性，两者结合实现了高质量合成数据集的可扩展生成。

Original Abstract: Synthetic data generation has emerged as a powerful tool for improving data scalability in computer vision. Recent diffusion-based pipelines have demonstrated strong photorealism. However, how to enforce precise 3D structure and pose consistency in generated images remains challenging. Existing methods leverage visual prompts such as edge maps to guide diffusion models, but often suffer from over-conditioning artifacts that degrade image realism and limit dataset quality. In this paper, we present a diffusion-based image generation framework that enforces 3D structural alignment while preserving photorealism through adaptive conditioning. Our framework, Adaptive Conditioning for 3D-Aware Synthetic Data Generation (AC3S), introduces a self-supervised visual prompt modulator that dynamically adjusts the strength of ControlNet conditioning, preventing over-conditioning and enabling the diffusion model to retain its generative expressiveness. To further enhance diversity and semantic consistency, we develop a multi-agent vision language model framework that composes detailed and 3D-aware prompts aligned with the underlying geometric structure. Together, these components enable the scalable generation of high-quality synthetic datasets with accurate 2D and 3D annotations. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method significantly improves image quality and downstream utility.

中文摘要翻译: 合成数据生成已成为提升计算机视觉中数据可扩展性的强大工具。最近的基于扩散的流水线展现了较强的照片真实感。然而，如何在生成图像中强制实现精确的3D结构和姿态一致性仍然具有挑战性。现有方法利用边缘图等视觉提示来引导扩散模型，但常因过度条件化伪影而损害图像真实感并限制数据集质量。在本文中，我们提出了一种基于扩散的图像生成框架，通过自适应条件化在保持照片真实感的同时实现3D结构对齐。我们的框架——面向3D感知合成数据生成的自适应条件化（AC3S），引入了一种自监督视觉提示调制器，可动态调整ControlNet条件化的强度，防止过度条件化，使扩散模型保留其生成表达能力。为进一步增强多样性和语义一致性，我们开发了一个多智能体视觉语言模型框架，用于编写与底层几何结构对齐的、具有3D感知能力的详细提示。这些组件共同实现了具有精确2D和3D标注的高质量合成数据集的可扩展生成。大量实验表明，我们的方法显著提升了图像质量和下游任务效用。

</details>


### [52] [AA: A Multi-view Multimodal Dataset for Screen-based Gaze Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31211)
*Chang Liu,Jiaqi Liu,Zhoutong Ye,Xinjie Shen,Chun Yu,Yuanchun Shi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了AA多视角多模态屏幕注视估计数据集，包含8个屏幕摄像头和2个侧摄像头，支持多视角和多模态学习，旨在提升遮挡和视角变化下的鲁棒性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有注视估计数据集多基于单视角，难以应对视角变化和遮挡问题，缺乏多视角多模态的标准化基准。

Method: 采集8个屏幕安装摄像头和2个侧视角摄像头的同步面部观测数据，配合受控注视目标，提供全局面部和局部区域裁剪的多模态数据。

Result: 提供了独立于被试的评估划分和标准化数据处理流程，支持可重复研究。

Conclusion: AA数据集填补了多视角多模态注视估计数据集的空白，可促进更鲁棒的注视估计模型研究。

Original Abstract: We present AA, a multi-view multimodal dataset for screen-based gaze estimation. The dataset captures synchronized facial observations from eight fixed screen-mounted cameras and two additional side-view cameras, paired with precise screen-space gaze targets collected under controlled fixation conditions. Each sample contains multi-view face observations together with structured facial region crops, enabling multimodal learning from both global and local visual cues. Unlike existing single-view gaze datasets, AA provides multi-view coverage from both screen-mounted and side-mounted perspectives, enabling more robust modeling under viewpoint variation and occlusion. The dataset includes subject-independent evaluation splits and a standardized data processing pipeline to support reproducible research in gaze estimation.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出了AA，一个用于屏幕注视估计的多视角多模态数据集。该数据集从八个固定的屏幕安装摄像头和两个额外的侧视角摄像头捕获同步的面部观测数据，并配以在受控固视条件下收集的精确屏幕空间注视目标。每个样本包含多视角面部观测数据以及结构化的面部区域裁剪，从而支持从全局和局部视觉线索进行多模态学习。与现有的单视角注视数据集不同，AA提供了来自屏幕安装和侧面安装视角的多视角覆盖，从而能够在视角变化和遮挡情况下实现更鲁棒的建模。该数据集包括独立于被试的评估划分和标准化的数据处理流程，以支持注视估计领域的可重复研究。

</details>


### [53] [CooperScene: Multi-Modal Cooperative Autonomy Benchmark with C-V2X Communication Characterization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31219)
*Bo Wu,Ruoshen Mo,Justin Yue,Yanyu Zhang,Janice Nguyen,Guoyuan Wu,Amit Roy-Chowdhury,Matthew J. Barth,Hang Qiu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CooperScene是一个高保真协作自动驾驶数据集，包含真实C-V2X通信特性、多模态传感器和3GPP标准兼容的通信数据，用于评估多智能体协同感知在真实部署场景中的性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有数据集忽视了真实部署中的复杂性，如有限通信带宽及其动态变化、异构传感模态、以及超出单一协作伙伴的可扩展性问题。

Method: 构建包含三个CAV和一个RSU的多场景协作数据集，配备多模态传感器和商用C-V2X通信设备，实现严格的传感器与智能体同步、厘米级定位与空间对齐、精确跨模态标定，以及3GPP标准兼容的C-V2X通信记录。

Result: 数据集包含59K帧、344K个物体标注，覆盖交叉口、高速公路匝道和停车场等场景，所有标注以10Hz频率、全局一致3D标签形式提供。

Conclusion: CooperScene为评估多智能体扩展性和实际部署性能建立了严格基准，填补了真实C-V2X通信特性与协作自动驾驶数据集之间的空白。

Original Abstract: Cellular vehicle-to-everything (C-V2X) enables cooperative perception, prediction, and planning beyond the field of view of individual agents. However, existing datasets often overlook the complexities of real-world deployment, such as limited communication bandwidth and its dynamics, heterogeneous sensing modalities, and scalability beyond a single cooperative partner. In this paper, we introduce CooperScene, a high-fidelity cooperative autonomy dataset with real-world C-V2X communication characterization. The dataset is organized into diverse scenes, including intersections, highway ramps, and parking lots. These scenes involve three connected and autonomous vehicles (CAVs) and one infrastructure roadside unit (RSU), all equipped with multi-modal sensors and commercial off-the-shelf C-V2X communication radios. All scenes are annotated with globally consistent 3D labels at 10 Hz, totaling 344K objects across 59K frames, underpinned by tight sensor- and agent-synchronization, centimeter-level localization and spatial alignment, precise cross-modality calibration, and 3GPP-standard-compliant C-V2X communication. CooperScene establishes a rigorous benchmark for evaluating multi-agent scaling and actual performance in real-world deployable settings. Project website for data and benchmark: https://cisl.ucr.edu/CooperScene

中文摘要翻译: 蜂窝车联网（C-V2X）使能了超越单个智能体视野的协同感知、预测与规划。然而，现有数据集往往忽视了真实部署中的复杂性，例如有限的通信带宽及其动态变化、异构的传感模态，以及超出单一协作伙伴的可扩展性。本文介绍了CooperScene——一个具有真实C-V2X通信特性表征的高保真协作自动驾驶数据集。该数据集按多样化场景组织，包括交叉口、高速公路匝道和停车场。这些场景涉及三辆网联自动驾驶汽车（CAV）和一个基础设施路侧单元（RSU），所有节点均配备多模态传感器和商用现货C-V2X通信设备。所有场景以10Hz频率进行全局一致的三维标注，总计59K帧中包含344K个物体，依托严格的传感器与智能体同步、厘米级定位与空间对齐、精确的跨模态标定，以及符合3GPP标准的C-V2X通信。CooperScene为评估多智能体扩展性和实际可部署环境中的真实性能建立了严格的基准。数据和基准项目网站：https://cisl.ucr.edu/CooperScene

</details>


### [54] [ForgeDrive: Bidirectional Cross-Conditioning for Unified Visual-Action Generation in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31226)
*Xuchang Zhong,He Zheng,Chenxu Zhao,Tianxiong Lv,Hangqi Fan,Bohua Wang,Yushan Liu,Zhihao Liao,Leigang Luo,Congyang Zhao,Yang Cai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ForgeDrive提出了一种"先行动后想象"（act-then-imagine）的统一自回归扩散框架，通过视觉-动作交叉条件化，将未来分解为逐时间步的帧-动作对，在推理时先生成动作再生成未来帧，避免了传统"先想象后行动"范式中视觉生成误差级联到规划的问题，在NAVSIM上超越现有规划器。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统的"先想象后行动"（imagine-then-act）范式中，视觉生成阶段的误差会级联到后续的动作规划中，导致模型性能受限。作者希望打破这种单向依赖，让动作生成不再依赖干净的未来帧，而是让动作反过来提升未来帧生成的质量。

Method: 提出ForgeDrive框架：(1) 将未来分解为逐时间步的帧-动作对序列，每个动作与其对应视觉观测交织；(2) 训练时解耦两种模态的扩散时间步，引入UniDiffuser风格噪声调度器，使模型能够从任一模态推断另一模态；(3) 推理时提出"act-then-imagine"范式，先生成动作（无需干净未来帧作为前提），再用生成的动作提升未来帧生成精度；(4) 增加未来自我状态预测模块进一步增强规划能力。

Result: 在NAVSIM上的大量实验表明，ForgeDrive不仅将驾驶模拟、规划和视觉里程计统一到单个模型中，而且在不使用任何后训练策略的情况下，超越了现有的强规划器。

Conclusion: ForgeDrive通过"先行动后想象"的推理范式有效解决了视觉生成误差级联问题，验证了动作生成可以内化为模型在训练中习得的能力，且生成的动作能反过来提升未来帧生成质量，实现了更鲁棒的自动驾驶规划。

Original Abstract: World-model-based autonomous driving endows the model with the ability to understand scene evolution. Yet this promise is undermined by the prevailing imagine-then-act paradigm, which allows errors from the more challenging visual generation stage to cascade into action planning. We introduce ForgeDrive, a unified autoregressive diffusion framework with visual-action cross-conditioning that closes this gap through act-then-imagine paradigm. ForgeDrive factorizes the future as a sequence of per-timestep frame-action pairs, intertwining each action with its corresponding visual observation. During training, we decouple the diffusion timesteps of the two modalities and introduce a UniDiffuser-style noise scheduler to get the ability to infer either modality from its counterpart and deepen understanding of relationships between images and actions. At inference, we propose a novel act-then-imagine inference paradigm, and find that at each step, action generation is a capability internalized during training, requiring no clean future frame as a prerequisite at inference time; instead, the generated action can improve the accuracy of future frame generation, which in turn enhances the quality of the next action. Additionally, we augment each step with future ego-status prediction, further sharpening planning ability. Extensive experiments on NAVSIM demonstrate that ForgeDrive not only unifies driving simulation, planning, and visual odometry into a single model, but also outperforms existing strong planners without any post-training strategy.

中文摘要翻译: 基于世界模型的自动驾驶赋予了模型理解场景演化的能力。然而，这种前景被当前主流的"先想象后行动"（imagine-then-act）范式所削弱——该范式允许更具挑战性的视觉生成阶段的误差级联到动作规划中。我们提出了ForgeDrive，一个具有视觉-动作交叉条件化的统一自回归扩散框架，通过"先行动后想象"（act-then-imagine）范式弥补了这一差距。ForgeDrive将未来分解为逐时间步的帧-动作对序列，将每个动作与其对应的视觉观测交织在一起。在训练过程中，我们解耦了两种模态的扩散时间步，并引入了UniDiffuser风格的噪声调度器，从而获得从一种模态推断另一种模态的能力，加深了对图像与动作之间关系的理解。在推理时，我们提出了一种新颖的"先行动后想象"推理范式，并发现在每一步中，动作生成是训练过程中内化的一种能力，在推理时不需要干净的未来帧作为前提；相反，生成的动作可以提高未来帧生成的准确性，这反过来又提升了下一个动作的质量。此外，我们为每一步增加了未来自我状态预测，进一步提升了规划能力。在NAVSIM上的大量实验表明，ForgeDrive不仅将驾驶模拟、规划和视觉里程计统一到单个模型中，而且在不使用任何后训练策略的情况下，超越了现有的强规划器。

</details>


### [55] [UHD-MFF: Shattering Barriers in Multi-Focus Ultra-High-Definition Image Fusion via Learnable Lookup Tables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31242)
*Yibing Zhang,Xunpeng Yi,Qinglong Yan,Yeda Wang,Han Xu,Jiayi Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出首个超高清多焦点图像融合数据集UHD-MFF和专用查找表框架UMF-LUT，通过粗区域查找表（C-LUT）和细节边缘查找表（D-LUT）的分工协作，在超高清场景下实现高效、可部署的实时4K多焦点融合，性能超越现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有MFF方法局限于低分辨率图像，在UHD场景面临三大障碍：数据可用性（缺乏UHD融合数据集）、模型适应性（现有模型难以处理超高分辨率）、部署可行性（计算开销过大）。

Method: 提出UMF-LUT框架，包含两个专门化查找表模块：C-LUT在低分辨率尺度上联合查询多梯度线索和语义线索，进行区域级决策；D-LUT在高分辨率尺度上利用高效拉普拉斯线索提供补充性的边缘级决策信息。

Result: 在视觉保真度和量化指标上均超越SOTA方法，能够实现实时4K多焦点融合，并展现出在智能手机上部署的潜力。

Conclusion: 该方法有效推动了多焦点图像融合向超高清成像场景发展，兼具高性能与强部署性。

Original Abstract: With the advancement of imaging technology, ultra-high-definition images have become increasingly essential in modern visual applications. However, existing multi-focus image fusion remains largely confined to low-resolution images and faces three major barriers in UHD scenarios, namely data availability, model adaptability, and deployment feasibility, which severely hinder its practical application. To shatter these barriers, first, we propose the UHD-MFF dataset, the first large-scale ultra-high-resolution multi-focus fusion dataset. Second, we propose a scale-specialized lookup-table framework tailored for ultra-high-resolution images, termed as UMF-LUT. It consists of Coarse-Region Lookup Table (C-LUT) and Detail-Edge Lookup Table (D-LUT). Specifically, C-LUT performs joint queries of multiple gradient cues and semantic cues at low-resolution scales to enable region-level decision-making. Also, D-LUT operates at high-resolution scales, leveraging efficient Laplacian cues to provide complementary edge-level decision information. Such a design makes the model particularly well-suited for ultra-high-resolution multi-focus image fusion. Finally, it offers strong deployability with minimal computational overhead, enabling real-time 4K multi-focus fusion and showing promising potential for smartphone. Extensive experiments demonstrate that it outperforms SOTA methods in both visual fidelity and quantitative metrics. It effectively advances the development of multi-focus image fusion toward ultra-high-resolution imaging scenarios. The code is available at https://github.com/zyb5/UHD-MFF.

中文摘要翻译: 随着成像技术的进步，超高清图像在现代视觉应用中变得日益重要。然而，现有的多焦点图像融合仍主要局限于低分辨率图像，在超高清场景下面临三大障碍：数据可用性、模型适应性以及部署可行性，这严重阻碍了其实际应用。为打破这些障碍，首先，我们提出了UHD-MFF数据集，这是首个大规模超高清多焦点融合数据集。其次，我们提出了一种专为超高清图像量身定制的尺度专门化查找表框架，称为UMF-LUT。它包含粗区域查找表（C-LUT）和细节边缘查找表（D-LUT）。具体而言，C-LUT在低分辨率尺度上对多梯度线索和语义线索进行联合查询，实现区域级决策；D-LUT在高分辨率尺度上运行，利用高效的拉普拉斯线索提供补充性的边缘级决策信息。这种设计使得该模型特别适用于超高清多焦点图像融合。最后，该模型具备强部署能力，计算开销极小，可实现实时4K多焦点融合，并展现出在智能手机上应用的潜力。大量实验表明，该方法在视觉保真度和量化指标上均优于现有最先进方法，有效推动了多焦点图像融合向超高清成像场景的发展。代码开源在https://github.com/zyb5/UHD-MFF。

</details>


### [56] [HyperVLP: Enhancing Hierarchical Surgical Video-Language Pre-training in Hyperbolic Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31245)
*Yaojun Hu,Kun Yuan,Nassir Navab,Haochao Ying,Jian Wu,Nicolas Padoy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种双曲空间下的手术视频-语言预训练框架，通过显式保持层次结构（动作→步骤→阶段）并缓解结构假阴性问题，在零样本和小样本手术阶段识别任务上取得了一致提升。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有手术视觉-语言基础模型将多层级、异质的文本信号（动作叙述、步骤标题、摘要文本）压缩到单一平面嵌入空间，忽略了跨层级的语义包含关系（动作属于步骤、步骤组成阶段），导致长程依赖建模能力减弱。

Method: 提出双曲手术视频-语言预训练框架，利用双曲空间表达层次结构；通过缓解由过程上下文诱导的结构假阴性，并强制父阶段与其子步骤之间的语义一致性，来显式保持层级结构。

Result: 在多个手术基准数据集上的大量实验表明，该方法在跨手术流程和机构的零样本和小样本阶段识别任务上取得了一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模手术知识的层次结构（而非塌缩到平面空间），可以更有效地利用视频和语言之间的跨层级语义包含关系，提升下游手术阶段识别的泛化能力。

Original Abstract: Surgical vision-language foundation models typically adopt educational materials, such as surgical lecture videos, to transfer surgical knowledge encoded in language into visual representations. These knowledge are multi-dimensional and hierarchical: fine-grained action cues appear in narration, mid-level key steps are summarized in subsection headings, and global procedural context, such as patient history and surgical strategy, is described in abstract texts. Prior work largely collapses these heterogeneous signals into a single flat embedding space, implicitly assuming independence across hierarchy levels. However, this is suboptimal because it ignores cross-level semantic containment, e.g., actions belong to steps, steps compose phases, weakens long-range dependency modeling. To this end, we propose a hyperbolic surgical video-language pre-training framework that explicitly preserves the hierarchical structure by mitigating structural false negatives induced by procedural context and enforcing semantic consistency between parent phases and their constituent child steps. Extensive experiments on multiple surgical benchmarks show consistent gains in zero- and few-shot phase recognition across procedures and institutions.

中文摘要翻译: 手术视觉-语言基础模型通常采用教育材料（如手术讲座视频）来将语言编码的手术知识迁移到视觉表示中。这些知识是多维且分层的：细粒度的动作线索出现在叙述中，中层级的关键步骤总结在子节标题中，全局过程上下文（如患者病史和手术策略）则在摘要文本中描述。先前的工作大多将这些异质信号压缩到一个单一的平面嵌入空间中，隐含地假设层级之间相互独立。然而这是次优的，因为它忽略了跨层级的语义包含关系（例如动作属于步骤、步骤构成阶段），削弱了长程依赖建模能力。为此，我们提出了一种双曲手术视频-语言预训练框架，通过缓解由过程上下文诱导的结构假阴性，并强制父阶段与其组成子步骤之间的语义一致性，来显式保持层次结构。在多个手术基准上的大量实验表明，该方法在跨手术流程和机构的零样本和小样本阶段识别任务上取得了一致的性能提升。

</details>


### [57] [Rethinking the Role of Feature Engineering and Learning Strategies in Few-Shot Hidden Emotion Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31249)
*Xiaochuan Guo,Jihao Gu,Haixu Liu,Yuxin Liu,Qi Wang,Yufei Wang,Fei Wang,Kun Li,Dan Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种紧凑的多模态时序建模框架，集成2D/3D骨架、面部Blendshape、DINOv2/v3、X-CLIP和Gemini语义先验等多源特征，通过交叉注意力机制（Base作为Query，Offset作为Key/Value）消除个体静态偏差，并采用基于多实例学习的自适应池化提取瞬时情绪、抑制背景噪声，最终在第四届EI-MIGA-IJCAI挑战赛Track 3中取得第一名（测试准确率0.76923）。文章还揭示了通用视觉基础模型在微动态任务中的表示坍缩现象，以及由于捷径学习和死记硬背导致的排行榜驱动的伪泛化。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 长视频中隐式情绪证据弱且稀疏，现有方法难以有效提取微动态情绪特征，且通用视觉基础模型在该任务中存在表示坍缩与伪泛化问题。

Method: 提出紧凑多模态时序建模框架：1) 集成2D/3D骨架、面部Blendshape、DINOv2/v3、X-CLIP、Gemini语义先验等多源特征；2) 设计交叉注意力机制，以静态姿态特征Base为Query、动态微动差分特征Offset为Key/Value，捕捉局部相对速度以消除个体身份和体型带来的静态偏差；3) 采用基于多实例学习的自适应池化方法，提取瞬时情绪并抑制长序列背景噪声。

Result: 在第四届EI-MIGA-IJCAI挑战赛Track 3中取得第一名，测试准确率0.76923；揭示了通用视觉基础模型在微动态任务中存在的表示坍缩现象，以及由捷径学习和死记硬背导致的公共排行榜驱动的伪泛化机制。

Conclusion: 所提出的紧凑多模态时序建模框架能有效解决长视频中弱稀疏隐式情绪证据的挑战，同时指出通用视觉基础模型在微动态任务中易陷入表示坍缩和伪泛化，需警惕排行榜驱动评估的局限性。

Original Abstract: In this paper, we present the solution developed by our team, XInsight Lab, which achieved first place in Track 3 of the 4th EI-MIGA-IJCAI Challenge with a test accuracy of 0.76923. To address the challenge of weak and sparse implicit emotion evidence in long videos, this paper extends the winning solution from the previous competition and proposes a compact multi-modal temporal modeling framework. The framework integrates and evaluates the effects of multi-source features, including 2D/3D skeletons, facial expression Blendshapes, DINOv2/v3 vision foundation models, X-CLIP video features, and Gemini semantic priors. Architecturally, we propose a cross-attention mechanism that utilizes static pose features, denoted as Base, as the Query and dynamic micro-motion differential features, denoted as Offset, as the Key and Value. By capturing local relative velocities, this mechanism eliminates static biases related to individual body shape and identity. Concurrently, an adaptive pooling method based on Multiple Instance Learning is employed to extract instantaneous emotions while suppressing background noise in long sequences. Finally, the paper reveals the representation collapse phenomenon of general vision foundation models in micro-dynamic tasks, and analyzes the underlying mechanisms where networks fall into public-leaderboard-driven pseudo-generalization due to shortcut learning and rote memorization.

中文摘要翻译: 本文介绍了由XInsight Lab团队开发的解决方案，该方案在第四届EI-MIGA-IJCAI挑战赛Track 3中以0.76923的测试准确率获得第一名。针对长视频中弱且稀疏的隐式情绪证据这一挑战，本文延用了上一届比赛的优胜解决方案，并提出了一种紧凑的多模态时序建模框架。该框架集成并评估了多源特征的效果，包括2D/3D骨架、面部表情Blendshape、DINOv2/v3视觉基础模型、X-CLIP视频特征以及Gemini语义先验。在架构上，本文提出了一种交叉注意力机制，将表示为Base的静态姿态特征作为查询（Query），将表示为Offset的动态微动差分特征作为键（Key）和值（Value）。通过捕捉局部相对速度，该机制消除了与个体体型和身份相关的静态偏差。同时，采用基于多实例学习的自适应池化方法来提取瞬时情绪，同时抑制长序列中的背景噪声。最后，本文揭示了通用视觉基础模型在微动态任务中的表示坍缩现象，并分析了由于捷径学习和死记硬背导致网络陷入公共排行榜驱动的伪泛化的内在机制。

</details>


### [58] [Decodable Is Not Grounded: A Vision-Ablation Arbiter for VLM Spatial Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31257)
*Chih-Ting Liao,Fei Shen,Xin Cao,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现，通过线性探针和基于steering的恢复方法来读取视觉-语言模型（VLM）中的潜在知识会系统性高估模型实际对图像的“接地（grounding）”程度。作者提出一种简单的因果控制方法——用灰色空白图像替换原始图像——作为仲裁者（arbiter），可以清晰区分出三种接地机制：真正的接地（依赖视觉）、先验（独立于视觉）以及令人惊讶的“反转接地”（可解码、可因果控制，但符号被错误部署）。实验表明，在14个跨系列模型上，水平轴是接地的，垂直轴是先验的，深度轴是反转的，且反转现象随模型规模扩大而出现。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 标准方法（线性探针+steering恢复）会系统性高估VLM对图像的接地程度，即模型可能并非真正“看”了图像，而是利用了语言先验或反转的视觉信号。现有评估手段无法区分这些情况，需要一种更可靠的自校准方法来判断模型是否真正接地。

Method: 提出“空白图像仲裁者”（blank-image arbiter）：将原始图像替换为灰色空白图像，比较模型在正常图像和空白图像下的空间推理表现。如果模型真正接地（vision-dependent），表现应显著下降；如果只是先验（vision-independent），表现应保持不变；如果反转（inverted），表现反而会上升。此外，使用无训练投影（training-free projection）和训练后的低秩编辑（trained low-rank edit）进行steering，并对比旋转（rotation）等几何操作。

Result: （1）在14个VLM（6个语言模型家族，2B-27B）上，水平轴接地，垂直轴为先验，深度轴为反转。（2）反转接地在7/8个模型上复制，且随模型规模扩大而出现。（3）反转的修复方式随几何结构而异：干净模型上无训练旋转即可匹配训练编辑效果，分布式的反转需要训练后的低秩编辑，构成一个按模型修正复杂度排列的频谱。

Conclusion: 空白图像仲裁者是一种廉价、自校准的因果控制方法，能干净地区分接地感知、反转感知和先验替代。论文主张这种方法应作为VLM中潜在知识和steering声明的默认控制手段。

Original Abstract: The standard way to read latent knowledge out of a model, a linear probe confirmed by a steering recovery, can systematically overstate what a vision-language model (VLM) actually grounds in the image. We show this on spatial reasoning, where the error is invisible to both probing and steering yet exposed by a one-line causal control: replacing the image with a gray blank.
