Probes decode the within-axis answer at 73--97% across axes, and a training-free projection lifts a near-chance axis from 59% to 79%, exactly the signature of unlocking latent knowledge. The blank-image arbiter refutes it, revealing three grounding regimes that probing conflates: an axis can be grounded (vision-dependent, correct), a prior (vision-independent, with its decode and its apparent recovery a directional default rather than perception), or, surprisingly, inverted: decodable, causally controllable, but deployed with the wrong sign, so the model scores below chance and the error requires looking. The taxonomy holds across the studied VLMs: in fourteen models spanning six language-model families and 2B--27B, horizontal is grounded, vertical is a prior, and depth is inverted, with the inversion emerging at scale within families. The decode-versus-deploy inversion replicates on seven of eight models across five families, and the minimal edit that re-deploys it varies with geometry: a training-free rotation matches a trained edit on the cleanest model, while distributed inversions need a trained low-rank edit, tracing a per-model correction-complexity spectrum. The cheap, self-calibrating arbiter cleanly separates grounded perception, inverted perception, and prior substitution; we argue it should be a default control for latent-knowledge and steering claims in VLMs.

中文摘要翻译: 从模型中读取潜在知识的标准方法——线性探针配合steering恢复——会系统性高估视觉-语言模型（VLM）实际对图像的“接地（grounding）”程度。我们在空间推理任务上证明了这一点，探针和steering都看不出该误差，但一个简单的因果控制——用灰色空白图像替换原图——就能将其暴露。探针对各个轴的内轴答案解码准确率达73-97%，一个无训练投影将接近随机的轴从59%提升至79%，这正是解锁潜在知识的典型特征。但空白图像仲裁者反驳了这一点，揭示了探针所混淆的三种接地机制：一个轴可以是接地（依赖视觉、正确）、先验（独立于视觉，其解码和表面上的恢复只是一种方向性默认而非感知），或者令人意外地——反转：可解码、可因果控制、但以错误的符号部署，导致模型得分低于随机水平，且该错误需要观察才能发现。该分类学在所研究的VLM中保持一致：在14个涵盖6个语言模型家族、参数规模2B-27B的模型中，水平轴是接地的，垂直轴是先验的，深度轴是反转的，且反转现象在家族内随规模扩大而出现。解码-部署的反转在5个家族的8个模型中的7个上得到复现，修复该反转的最小编辑随几何结构而变化：在干净模型上，无训练旋转即可匹配训练后的编辑效果，而分布式的反转需要训练后的低秩编辑，这勾勒出每个模型修正复杂度的频谱。这种廉价、自校准的仲裁者能干净地区分接地感知、反转感知和先验替代；我们主张它应成为VLM中潜在知识和steering声明的默认控制方法。

</details>


### [59] [WarpHammer: Densifying Scene Warps with 3D Object Priors for Extreme View Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31258)
*Michael Green,Gavriel Habib,Dvir Samuel,Tal Berkovitz Shalev,Issar Tzachor,Rami Ben-Ari,Or Litany*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WarpHammer是一种无需训练的框架，通过从3D生成先验中提取显式物体重建来增强投影条件新视角合成（NVS），解决了大角度轨道运动下投影变得稀疏、生成器丢失像素内容和相机线索的问题，并能融合来自外部源的辅助视角以提升几何保真度。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有投影条件NVS方法在小视角变化下表现良好，但在大角度轨道运动下，投影变得稀疏且遮挡严重，导致生成器丢失像素内容和隐含的相机线索，产生镜像伪影。作者希望解决这一失效模式，且无需对基础模型进行微调。

Method: 提出WarpHammer框架：1) 利用原生3D生成先验（如SAM3D）对场景中物体进行显式3D重建；2) 将该重建物体叠加到投影场景中，补充缺失的前景表面并遮挡不可见的背景点，从而恢复外观和相机线索；3) 进一步利用该显式物体表征，通过预训练的多视角几何基础模型联合处理参考图像和外部辅助视图，预测统一点云并融合到物体重建中，无需用户提供辅助视图的相机位姿。

Result: 在五个基准数据集上，WarpHammer在强基线方法崩溃的大视角偏差下仍能生成稳定的新视角，并且是首个能够自然融合来自外部源、位姿未知的辅助物体视图的场景级NVS方法。

Conclusion: WarpHammer通过显式3D物体重建增强投影场景，无需训练即可解决大角度NVS的失效问题，同时开辟了融合外部辅助视图的新能力，显著提升了新视角合成的鲁棒性和几何真实性。

Original Abstract: Projection-conditioned novel view synthesis (NVS) warps an explicit 3D reconstruction of the input view into the target camera and conditions a generator on the warped rendering. This works well for small viewpoint changes but degrades sharply under large orbital motion: the warp becomes sparse around the orbited object, where hidden surfaces dominate the new view and mirror-like artifacts emerge, causing the generator to lose both pixel content and the implicit camera cue carried by the warp. We introduce WarpHammer, a training-free framework that resolves this failure mode by augmenting the warped scene with an explicit 3D reconstruction of the object obtained from a native 3D generative prior (e.g., SAM3D). The reconstructed object adds missing foreground surfaces and occludes background points that should no longer be visible, restoring both appearance and camera cues without fine-tuning the base model. The same explicit object representation further unlocks a capability current NVS pipelines do not support: incorporating auxiliary views of the object from sources outside the target scene, for example, a casual snapshot of a car paired with a manufacturer studio shot of the same model. We process the reference and auxiliary images jointly with a pretrained multi-view geometry foundation model, which predicts a unified point cloud that we fuse into the 3D object reconstruction. This yields substantially more faithful geometry than single-image reconstruction, without requiring user-provided camera poses for the auxiliary views. On five benchmarks, WarpHammer produces stable novel views at viewpoint deviations where strong baselines collapse, and is the first scene-level NVS method that can naturally fuse auxiliary, pose-unknown object views from an external source.

中文摘要翻译: 投影条件新视角合成（NVS）将输入视图的显式三维重建变形到目标相机视角，并在变形渲染结果上调节生成器。这种方法在小视角变化下表现良好，但在大角度轨道运动下性能急剧下降：围绕被旋转物体周围，变形变得稀疏，隐藏表面主导了新视图，并出现镜面伪影，导致生成器丢失像素内容以及变形所携带的隐含相机线索。我们提出WarpHammer，一种无需训练的框架，通过从原生3D生成先验（如SAM3D）中获取物体的显式三维重建来增强变形场景，从而解决这一失效模式。重建的物体补充了缺失的前景表面，并遮挡了不应再可见的背景点，无需微调基础模型即可恢复外观和相机线索。相同的显式物体表示还解锁了当前NVS管线不支持的能力：融合来自目标场景之外的外部来源的物体辅助视图，例如，将一辆汽车的随手拍快照与该车型的制造商摄影棚照片配对。我们使用预训练的多视角几何基础模型联合处理参考图像和辅助图像，预测出一个统一的点云，并将其融合到物体三维重建中。与单图像重建相比，这产生了更加保真的几何结构，且无需用户提供辅助视图的相机位姿。在五个基准数据集上，WarpHammer在强基线方法崩溃的大视角偏差下仍能生成稳定的新视图，并且是首个能够自然融合来自外部源、位姿未知的辅助物体视图的场景级NVS方法。

</details>


### [60] [Learning from Failure: Inference-Time Self-Improvement for Computer-Use Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31270)
*Xueqiao Sun,Xiaohan Wang,Ludwig Schmidt,Serena Yeung-Levy,Yuhui Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种失败驱动的自我改进范式，通过利用LLM诊断失败模式并生成代码补丁来提升计算机使用智能体的性能，在OSWorld基准上将OpenCUA-72B的成功率从42.3%提升至48.9%，无需额外训练成本。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的自改进范式仅利用成功轨迹进行微调，丢弃了大量包含丰富模型弱点信息的失败轨迹，导致数据利用效率低下。

Method: 采用LLM对失败轨迹进行诊断，分析失败模式，提出推理时解决方案，并生成经人工轻量验证的代码补丁，从而升级智能体能力。

Result: 在OSWorld基准上，基于OpenCUA-72B模型，将成功率从42.3%提升至48.9%（提升6.6个百分点），无需额外训练成本，仅需少量推理开销。

Conclusion: 失败驱动的自我改进是成功驱动流程的有效补充，能够在不增加训练成本的情况下更高效地提升智能体性能。

Original Abstract: Computer-use agents, which leverage multimodal large language models (MLLMs) to operate computers and complete tasks, have attracted significant attention for their utility and versatility. A major challenge in developing these agents is collecting large-scale, high-quality trajectories. The standard approach generates synthetic data through a self-improving loop: an agent is placed in a verifiable environment and iteratively fine-tuned on its successful trajectories. Despite its effectiveness, this paradigm exploits only successful trajectories and discards the failed ones, even though failures carry rich information about a model's weaknesses. In this work, we explore a complementary failure-driven self-improvement loop, a data-centric paradigm that turns failed trajectories into agent improvements. Specifically, we employ an LLM to diagnose failure modes, propose inference-time solutions, and generate code patches -- lightly verified by humans -- that upgrade the agent. We validate this approach with the state-of-the-art OpenCUA-72B model on the OSWorld benchmark, improving the success rate from 42.3% to 48.9%, a gain of 6.6 percentage points, without any additional training cost and with only modest inference overhead. Our results demonstrate that failure-driven self-improvement is a viable complement to success-based pipelines, enabling more efficient agent improvement.

中文摘要翻译: 计算机使用智能体借助多模态大语言模型来操作计算机并完成任务，因其实用性和通用性而受到广泛关注。开发这类智能体的一个主要挑战是收集大规模、高质量的轨迹数据。标准方法通过自改进循环生成合成数据：将智能体置于可验证环境中，并对其成功轨迹进行迭代微调。尽管有效，但这种范式仅利用成功轨迹而丢弃失败轨迹，尽管失败中包含了模型弱点的丰富信息。本文探索了一种互补的失败驱动自改进循环，这是一种以数据为中心的范式，将失败轨迹转化为智能体的改进。具体而言，我们利用LLM诊断失败模式，提出推理时解决方案，并生成经人工轻量验证的代码补丁来升级智能体。我们在OSWorld基准上使用最先进的OpenCUA-72B模型验证了该方法，将成功率从42.3%提升至48.9%，提升了6.6个百分点，且无需额外训练成本，仅需适度的推理开销。结果表明，失败驱动的自我改进是成功驱动流程的有效补充方案，能够实现更高效的智能体改进。

</details>


### [61] [CLIMB: Centroid-Based Hierarchical Memory for Online Continual Self-Supervised Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31275)
*Julien Lefebvre,Stefan Duffner,Mathieu Lefort*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CLIMB提出了一种结合层次化质心记忆库与知识蒸馏的在线持续自监督学习方法，在无标签数据流中学习表示，无需任务边界信息，在多个基准上超越现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有OCSSL方法要么仅依赖重放缓冲区利用潜空间结构，要么仅依赖正则化，缺乏两者的有效结合，导致表示漂移问题未能充分解决。

Method: 提出层次化质心记忆库（CLIMB），在有限存储容量下将相似图像分组为质心，为对比学习提供难判别样本，同时保持观测分布的多样性；结合对重放样本的知识蒸馏来限制表示漂移。

Result: 在Split CIFAR-100和Split ImageNet-100数据集上，包括标准基准和新提出的不规则任务分布协议下，CLIMB均优于现有最先进的OCSSL方法。

Conclusion: 层次化质心记忆库与知识蒸馏的结合能有效提升在线持续自监督学习的性能，在无标签、无任务边界且受内存约束的条件下取得更优的表示学习效果。

Original Abstract: Online Continual Self-Supervised Learning (OCSSL) aims to learn representations from a continuous stream of unlabeled data, without knowledge of task boundaries and under memory constraints. Existing methods rely either on replay buffers that exploit latent space structure, or on regularization alone. We present CLIMB (Continual Learning with Intelligent Memory Bank), which combines both simultaneously. Our method introduces a hierarchical centroid-based memory, bounded in total number of stored images, combined with knowledge distillation on replayed examples to limit representation drift. The memory groups similar images into centroids, providing hard-to-discriminate examples for contrastive learning while covering the diversity of observed distributions. Experiments on Split CIFAR-100 and Split ImageNet-100, on standard benchmarks from the state-of-the-art as well as a new protocol with irregular task distributions show that CLIMB outperforms state-of-the-art OCSSL methods.

中文摘要翻译: 在线持续自监督学习（OCSSL）旨在从连续的无标签数据流中学习表示，无需任务边界信息，且受内存约束。现有方法要么依赖利用潜空间结构的重放缓冲区，要么仅依赖正则化。本文提出CLIMB（Continual Learning with Intelligent Memory Bank），同时结合了两种策略。该方法引入了一种层次化质心记忆库，对存储的图像总数进行上限约束，并结合对重放样例的知识蒸馏以限制表示漂移。该记忆库将相似图像分组为质心，为对比学习提供难判别样本，同时覆盖观测分布的多样性。在Split CIFAR-100和Split ImageNet-100上的实验，包括采用现有最先进方法的标准基准以及一种包含不规则任务分布的新协议，均表明CLIMB优于现有的最先进OCSSL方法。

</details>


### [62] [Editing Everything Everywhere All at Once](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31278)
*Fabio Quattrini,Carmine Zaccagnino,Enis Simsar,Marta Tintoré Gazulla,Rita Cucchiara,Alessio Tonioni,Silvia Cascianelli*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MICE 是一种无需训练的、基于多模态扩散 Transformer 的多实例图像编辑策略，通过调控联合注意力中的加性偏置，实现多个区域的同时编辑，有效抑制语义干扰和属性泄露。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有方法在单次前向传播中编辑图像多个元素时，语义干扰导致属性泄露和编辑解耦效果差，尤其随着编辑数量增加问题更严重。

Method: MICE 修改联合注意力的加性偏置，调控实例级编辑指令、潜在特征和上下文 token 之间的交互：允许实例内部注意力、惩罚相邻区域 token 间的交互、抑制无关的跨实例注意力。

Result: 在 LoMOE-Bench 和新提出的 MICE-Bench（平均每图 8.5 个并发编辑）上，MICE 在视觉质量保持和编辑指令忠实度方面优于强基线方法和近期竞争者。

Conclusion: MICE 是一种有效的无需训练的多实例图像编辑策略，能在保持全局视觉一致性的同时实现属性绑定，且支持可扩展的并发编辑。

Original Abstract: Editing multiple elements of an image in a single forward pass is a practical alternative to multi-turn image manipulation, offering improved efficiency and potentially better harmonization. However, when several instructions target different regions, semantic interference often leads to attribute leakage and poor edit disentanglement, especially as the number of edits increases. In this work, we propose MICE (Multi-Instance Concurrent Editing), a training-free strategy for scalable multi-instance image editing with Multimodal Diffusion Transformers. MICE modifies the additive bias of joint attention to regulate interactions between instance-specific edit instructions, latent, and context tokens identified via user-provided segmentation masks. Specifically, MICE allows intra-instance attention, penalizes interactions between neighboring region tokens, and suppresses unrelated cross-instance attention. As a result, our method enforces attribute binding while preserving global visual consistency. We evaluate MICE on LoMOE-Bench and introduce MICE-Bench, a more challenging benchmark with an average of 8.5 concurrent edits per image. The experiments demonstrate that our approach outperforms strong baselines and recent competitors in terms of visual quality preservation and faithfulness to the editing instructions.

中文摘要翻译: 在单次前向传播中编辑图像的多个元素，是多轮图像操作的一种实用替代方案，具有更高的效率和潜在更好的协调性。然而，当多条指令针对不同区域时，语义干扰常导致属性泄露和编辑解耦能力下降，尤其随着编辑数量的增加。本文提出 MICE（多实例并发编辑），一种面向多模态扩散 Transformer 的、无需训练的可扩展多实例图像编辑策略。MICE 修改联合注意力的加性偏置，以调控由用户提供分割掩码标识的实例级编辑指令、潜在特征和上下文 token 之间的交互。具体地，MICE 允许实例内部注意力、惩罚相邻区域 token 之间的交互、并抑制无关的跨实例注意力。由此，我们的方法在保持全局视觉一致性的同时强制执行属性绑定。我们在 LoMOE-Bench 上评估 MICE，并引入了 MICE-Bench——一个更具挑战性的基准，平均每张图像包含 8.5 个并发编辑。实验结果表明，在视觉质量保持和编辑指令忠实度方面，我们的方法优于强基线方法和近期竞争者。

</details>


### [63] [Deep Spectral Models for Robust Dental Shape Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31293)
*Tibor Kubík,François Guibault,Michal Španěl,Hervé Lombaert*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 ToothForge，一种基于深度谱生成框架的牙冠形态建模方法，通过同步谱嵌入在低维空间中学习牙齿形状流形，在有限数据集下实现与空间方法相当甚至更优的重建与生成性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 牙冠形态的精确建模对诊断、正畸规划和计算机辅助修复设计至关重要，但适用于训练此类模型的数据集通常规模有限。现有方法难以在保持几何可解释性的同时有效处理不同样本间连接性不一致的问题。

Method: 提出 ToothForge 深度谱生成框架，在谱域中通过同步谱嵌入学习三维牙齿形状的潜在流形。该方法利用谱同步技术缓解拉普拉斯-贝尔特拉米特征基的不稳定性，从而在低维空间中实现高效学习，并能处理不同连接性的样本。

Result: 通过鲁棒性分析、消融研究以及与基于PCA的统计形状模型和基于点的生成框架的基准对比，结果表明同步谱建模在重建和生成性能上达到或超过空间方法，同时保持了紧凑性和几何可解释性。

Conclusion: 所提出的紧凑同步系数和低维学习空间使该框架特别适用于牙科和医学领域中常见的数据集有限场景，以及在需要保证各诊所形状之间连接一致性的实际应用中具有可行性。

Original Abstract: Accurate modeling of dental crown morphology is fundamental for diagnosis, orthodontic planning, and computer-aided restoration design. However, datasets suitable for training such models are typically limited in size. We present ToothForge, a deep spectral generative framework that models dental crown geometries from compact, intrinsic representations. By operating in the spectral domain, ToothForge learns a latent manifold of 3D tooth shapes through synchronized spectral embeddings, ensuring consistent modeling across samples with varying connectivity. Spectral synchronization mitigates the instability of Laplace-Beltrami eigenbases and enables efficient learning in a low-dimensional space. The framework is thoroughly evaluated through robustness analysis, ablation studies, and benchmarking against PCA-based statistical shape models and point-based generative frameworks. Results show that synchronized spectral modeling achieves reconstruction and generative performance comparable to or exceeding spatial approaches, while maintaining compactness and geometric interpretability. Together, the compact synchronized coefficients and low-dimensional learning space make the framework particularly suitable for limited datasets, as often encountered in dental and medical domains, and applicable in real-world scenarios where guaranteeing consistent connectivity across shapes from various clinics is unrealistic.

中文摘要翻译: 牙冠形态的精确建模是诊断、正畸规划以及计算机辅助修复设计的基础。然而，适用于训练此类模型的数据集通常规模有限。我们提出 ToothForge，一个深度谱生成框架，通过紧凑的、内在的表示对牙冠几何形状进行建模。通过在谱域中操作，ToothForge 通过同步谱嵌入学习三维牙齿形状的潜在流形，确保跨不同连接性样本的一致性建模。谱同步缓解了拉普拉斯-贝尔特拉米特征基的不稳定性，并支持在低维空间中高效学习。该框架通过鲁棒性分析、消融研究以及与基于PCA的统计形状模型和基于点的生成框架的基准对比进行了全面评估。结果表明，同步谱建模在重建和生成性能上达到或超过空间方法，同时保持了紧凑性和几何可解释性。总之，紧凑的同步系数和低维学习空间使该框架特别适用于牙科和医学领域中常遇到的有限数据集情境，并且在需要保证来自不同诊所的形状之间连接一致性的现实场景中具有良好的适用性。

</details>


### [64] [Wavelet-Optimized Pseudo-3D Accelerated Diffusion Model for Truncated Computed Laminography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31318)
*Genyuan Zhang,Junyao Wang,Chuandong Tan,Fenglin Liu,Yongning Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于小波优化伪3D加速扩散模型（CL-DM），通过扩展重建目标至数据不完全区域并引入严格数据一致性约束，有效解决了计算分层成像（CL）中的视场限制导致的截断伪影问题，显著提升了成像范围和扫描效率。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有CL重建方法受视场限制导致投影数据截断，产生严重重建伪影；深度学习方法多依赖2D架构且缺乏严格数据一致性约束，同时将伪影去除局限在视场内，丢弃了视场外可能恢复的信息。

Method: 提出CL-DM模型：利用2D扩散模型进行切片聚合，结合3D基于模型的迭代重建（MBIR）方法确保严格数据一致性；沿z方向引入小波正则化、平移不变机制和低频保持策略以缓解切片间不连续性；设计3D快速采样架构加速推理。

Result: 大量仿真和真实实验表明，CL-DM能有效消除截断伪影，恢复高保真、连续的3D结构，性能优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 本文通过扩展重建目标到数据不完全区域并设计伪3D加速扩散模型，为CL截断重建提供了兼顾成像质量与效率的有效方案。

Original Abstract: Computed Laminography (CL) is a key technology for the nondestructive testing of large plate-shaped objects. However, field-of-view (FOV) limitations inevitably lead to truncation of projected data, an ill-posed inverse problem that causes severe reconstruction artifacts. Existing deep learning methods typically rely on 2D architectures that lack rigorous data consistency constraints. Furthermore, they conventionally confine artifact removal strictly to the FOV, discarding potentially recoverable information outside it. To overcome these limitations, we first introduce a comprehensive CL FOV analysis, categorizing the space into data-complete, data-incomplete, and data-free regions. By extending our reconstruction target to encompass the data-incomplete region, we significantly expand the effective imaging range and enhance scanning efficiency. To achieve this, we propose a novel wavelet-optimized pseudo-3D accelerated diffusion model for CL truncation reconstruction (CL-DM). Our method utilizes a standard 2D diffusion model for slice aggregation, combined with a 3D model-based iterative reconstruction (MBIR) method to ensure strict data consistency. To mitigate inter-slice discontinuities, we introduce wavelet regularization along the z-direction, paired with a translation-invariant (TI) mechanism and a low-frequency preservation strategy. Finally, we introduce a 3D fast sampling architecture, significantly accelerating inference speed. Extensive simulations and real-world experiments demonstrate that CL-DM is superior in effectively eliminating truncation artifacts and restoring high-fidelity, continuous 3D structures.

中文摘要翻译: 计算分层成像（CL）是大尺寸板状物体无损检测的关键技术。然而，视场（FOV）限制不可避免地导致投影数据截断，这是一个不适定的逆问题，会造成严重的重建伪影。现有的深度学习方法通常依赖缺乏严格数据一致性约束的2D架构，并且它们习惯性地将伪影去除严格限制在视场内，丢弃了视场外可能恢复的信息。为克服这些局限，我们首先引入全面的CL视场分析，将空间划分为数据完全区域、数据不完全区域和无数据区域。通过将重建目标扩展至包含数据不完全区域，我们显著扩大了有效成像范围并提升了扫描效率。为实现这一目标，我们提出了一种新颖的小波优化伪3D加速扩散模型用于CL截断重建（CL-DM）。该方法利用标准2D扩散模型进行切片聚合，并结合3D基于模型的迭代重建（MBIR）方法以确保严格的数据一致性。为缓解切片间的不连续性，我们沿z方向引入小波正则化，并配以平移不变（TI）机制和低频保持策略。最后，我们引入一种3D快速采样架构，显著加速推理速度。大量仿真和真实实验表明，CL-DM在有效消除截断伪影以及恢复高保真、连续3D结构方面表现优异。

</details>


### [65] [Accelerated Likelihood Maximization for Diffusion-based Versatile Content Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31323)
*Hyunsoo Lee,Inwoo Hwang,Young Min Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为加速似然最大化（ALM）的训练无关采样策略，通过直接在采样过程中优化未观测区域，实现了全局一致且高效的内容生成，在多个数据领域和任务中优于现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有扩散模型在基于部分输入生成多样化、连贯且合理的内容时存在局限：基于训练的方法计算成本高且泛化能力差，而基于训练无关的方法虽效率高但未显式优化未观测变量，导致全局不一致。

Method: 提出ALM（加速似然最大化），一种集成到反向扩散过程中的训练无关采样策略，直接优化未观测区域而非通过预生成区域约束间接影响，并引入加速策略提升计算效率。

Result: 在多个数据领域和任务上的实验结果表明，ALM一致性地优于当前最先进的方法，在保持性能的同时显著提高了计算效率。

Conclusion: ALM建立了一种强大的通用内容生成范式，有效解决了扩散模型在部分输入条件下生成内容的全局一致性和计算效率问题。

Original Abstract: Generating diverse, coherent, and plausible content from partially given inputs remains a fundamental challenge for diffusion models. Existing approaches face clear limitations: training-based approaches offer strong task-specific results but require costly computation, and they generalize poorly across tasks. Training-free approaches offer better efficiency, but they do not explicitly optimize over unobserved variables, leading to globally inconsistent results. To address these limitations, we introduce Accelerated Likelihood Maximization (ALM), a novel training-free sampling strategy integrated into the reverse diffusion process that significantly extends the applicability of diffusion models beyond simple generation tasks. Unlike previous methods that implicitly influence missing regions through pre-generated region constraints, we directly optimize the unobserved region during the sampling process, enabling globally coherent and plausible generation. Furthermore, we incorporate an acceleration strategy that significantly improves computational efficiency without sacrificing performance. Experimental results demonstrate that ALM consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods in various data domains and tasks, establishing a powerful paradigm for versatile content generation.

中文摘要翻译: 从部分给定的输入生成多样化、连贯且合理的内容仍然是扩散模型面临的一个基本挑战。现有方法存在明显的局限性：基于训练的方法虽然能获得强大的任务特定结果，但计算成本高昂且跨任务泛化能力差。基于训练无关的方法效率更高，但它们没有显式优化未观测变量，导致全局不一致的结果。为解决这些局限，我们提出了加速似然最大化（ALM），一种集成到反向扩散过程中的新型训练无关采样策略，显著扩展了扩散模型在简单生成任务之外的应用范围。与以往通过预生成区域约束间接影响缺失区域的方法不同，我们在采样过程中直接优化未观测区域，从而实现全局连贯且合理的生成。此外，我们引入了一种加速策略，在不牺牲性能的情况下显著提高了计算效率。实验结果表明，ALM在多个数据领域和任务中一致性地优于最先进方法，为通用内容生成建立了一种强大的范式。

</details>


### [66] [Bridging Video Understanding and Generation in a Unified Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31326)
*Yuqi Wang,Runyi Li,Ruoyu Feng,Renjie Chen,Wenfeng Lin,Mingyu Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Vega是一个统一视频理解和生成的框架，通过共享词汇表联合建模文本与视觉表示，并采用自回归预测与扩散渲染的混合架构，在视频生成和理解基准上均取得优异性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有研究在图像领域的统一生成与理解建模上已有探索，但视频领域仍未被充分研究。核心挑战在于视频理解需要紧凑、有判别性的语义表示，而视频生成需要保留视觉细节和时间一致性的密集信号。视频天然同时包含空间语义和时间动态，比静态图像更适合统一多模态建模。

Method: 提出Vega统一框架，通过共享词汇表联合建模文本与视觉表示；采用混合架构：自回归模型负责预测关键帧的语义视觉令牌，提供结构化表示；扩散模块基于该表示渲染密集、高分辨率的视频帧。

Result: 在视频生成基准VBench和视频理解基准VideoMME上均取得强性能。

Conclusion: 混合自回归预测与扩散渲染的架构能够有效统一视频理解与生成任务，兼顾紧凑语义表示和密集细节生成。

Original Abstract: Recently, unified image generation and understanding have been extensively explored. However, extending such unified modeling paradigms to the video domain remains largely underexplored. A central challenge is that video understanding favors compact, discriminative semantic representations, whereas video generation requires dense signals that preserve visual details and temporal coherence. Videos naturally capture both spatial semantics and temporal dynamics, making them a more suitable modality for unified multimodal modeling compared to static images. In this paper, we propose Vega, a unified framework that bridges video understanding and generation. Vega leverages a shared vocabulary to jointly model text and visual representations and employs a hybrid architecture combining autoregressive (AR) prediction with diffusion-based rendering. Specifically, the AR model focuses on predicting semantically meaningful visual tokens for keyframes, providing a structured representation that guides the diffusion module in rendering dense, high-resolution video frames. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Vega achieves strong performance on video generation benchmarks such as VBench and video understanding benchmarks like VideoMME.

中文摘要翻译: 最近，统一的图像生成与理解已被广泛探索。然而，将此类统一建模范式扩展到视频领域仍未得到充分研究。一个核心挑战在于：视频理解倾向于紧凑的、有判别性的语义表示，而视频生成则需要保留视觉细节和时间一致性的密集信号。视频天然同时捕捉空间语义和时间动态，使其相比静态图像成为更适合统一多模态建模的模态。在本文中，我们提出Vega，一个统一视频理解与生成的框架。Vega利用共享词汇表联合建模文本和视觉表示，并采用结合自回归预测与扩散渲染的混合架构。具体而言，自回归模型专注于预测关键帧中具有语义意义的视觉令牌，提供结构化的表示以指导扩散模块渲染密集的高分辨率视频帧。大量实验表明，Vega在VBench等视频生成基准和VideoMME等视频理解基准上均取得了优异的性能。

</details>


### [67] [Patient-Level Elbow Abnormality Detection: Leakage-Aware Evaluation of Learned Preprocessing, Calibration, and Triage-Oriented Operating Points](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31348)
*Ahmed Sallam,Ahmet Kaplan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究系统评估了在MURA肘部X光片上，基于预处理流程（含轻量级DnCNN模块）对面向分诊的骨科异常检测任务的影响。结果表明，预处理策略相较于原始输入DenseNet121基线未带来一致的判别性能提升，且增益因配置而异。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 探究在有泄漏感知的患者级评估协议下，学习的预处理流程（尤其是轻量级DnCNN模块）能否为骨科异常检测任务带来一致的判别和校准性能提升。

Method: 使用MURA数据集中的肘部X光片，在患者级检测任务中比较多种预处理流程（含/不含轻量级DnCNN学习预处理模块），以DenseNet121为基线，评估判别指标（AUROC、PR-AUC）和校准指标（ECE、Brier score），并结合高特异性的验证集操作点分析。

Result: 不同预处理策略间的差异较小且依赖具体配置；原始输入结合DnCNN前端降低了ECE和Brier score，但CLAHE结合DnCNN未改善校准效果；所有预处理策略均未在全部指标上产生一致的判别优势，原始输入DenseNet121基线仍具竞争力。

Conclusion: 在患者级评估下，预处理带来的增益是适度的且依赖于具体配置；原始输入的DenseNet121基线在整个实验中保持竞争力，没有哪种预处理策略能在所有指标上产生一致的判别优势。

Original Abstract: In this study, we examine learned preprocessing pipelines in the context of triage-oriented orthopedic abnormality detection task using elbow radiographs from MURA dataset. The evaluation focuses on patient-level detection of musculoskeletal abnormalities under a leakage-aware protocol. We compare multiple preprocessing pipelines, with and without a lightweight DnCNN module as a learned preprocessing component, to assess their impact on discrimination and calibration. Performance is assessed using discrimination metrics (AUROC, PR-AUC), calibration measures (ECE, Brier score), and validation-selected operating point analysis targeting high specificity. Results show that differences across preprocessing strategies are modest and configuration-dependent, with no consistent discrimination advantage over the raw-input DenseNet121 baseline. The raw and diverse inputs combined with the DnCNN front-end showed reduced ECE and Brier score, while CLAHE combined with DnCNN did not improve calibration. Overall, the results suggest that under patient-level evaluation, preprocessing gains are modest and configuration-dependent; the raw-input DenseNet121 baseline remains competitive throughout, and no tested preprocessing strategy produced a consistent discrimination advantage across all metrics.

中文摘要翻译: 在本研究中，我们在MURA数据集的肘部X光片上，以面向分诊的骨科异常检测任务为背景，考察了学习的预处理流程。评估在泄漏感知协议下进行，聚焦于患者级肌肉骨骼异常检测。我们比较了多种预处理流程（含或不含轻量级DnCNN模块作为学习的预处理组件），以评估其对判别性能和校准性能的影响。使用判别指标（AUROC、PR-AUC）、校准指标（ECE、Brier score）以及针对高特异性的验证集操作点分析进行评估。结果表明，不同预处理策略间的差异较小且依赖于具体配置，没有任何策略相较于原始输入DenseNet121基线产生一致的判别优势。原始和多样化输入结合DnCNN前端降低了ECE和Brier score，而CLAHE结合DnCNN未改善校准效果。总体而言，结果表明在患者级评估下，预处理增益是适度的且依赖于配置；原始输入的DenseNet121基线在整个实验中保持竞争力，没有哪种经过测试的预处理策略能在所有指标上产生一致的判别优势。

</details>


### [68] [RCL-Mamba: A Dual-domain State Space Model for Measurement-oriented Image Restoration in Rotational Sparse-View Scanning Computed Laminography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31353)
*Xuyang Duan,Genyuan Zhang,Zhenjiang Dong,Chuandong Tan,Zihao Wang,Junyao Wang,Fenglin Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RCL-Mamba，一种基于状态空间模型的双域图像恢复网络，用于解决旋转扫描计算机分层成像（RCL）中因连续稀疏扫描导致的旋转模糊和稀疏伪影问题，在实现8倍加速扫描的同时保持重建质量。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: RCL在快速检测中采用连续稀疏扫描，但引入两大跨域退化问题：投影域的旋转模糊（因曝光期间角度积分效应）和图像域的稀疏伪影（因数据不完全）。现有方法难以同时处理这两种不同域的退化。

Method: 提出RCL-Mamba，一种面向测量的双域状态空间模型（SSM）图像恢复网络，采用级联联合处理策略：先在投影域校正旋转模糊，再在图像域抑制稀疏伪影。同时设计Mamba-CNN双分支模块，自适应平衡大尺度模糊校正与局部细节恢复。

Result: 在模拟数据集和真实PCB扫描数据上，RCL-Mamba在模糊去除、伪影抑制和结构保持方面优于现有基线。线剖面的结构测量验证了其更好地保留了过孔/焊盘边界和细导线轮廓。扫描视角从512减少到64时，检测效率提升约8倍且不牺牲重建质量。

Conclusion: RCL-Mamba为高吞吐RCL检测提供了一种鲁棒的面向测量的恢复方案，在保持结构测量保真度的同时显著提升检测效率。

Original Abstract: Rotational Scanning Computed Laminography (RCL) is widely utilized for the Non-Destructive Testing(NDT) of large planar components. However, to facilitate rapid inspection, continuous sparse-view scanning is often employed, where the angular integration effect during exposure induces rotational blur in the projection domain. Furthermore, the data incompleteness inherent in sparse sampling manifests as sparse artifacts in the reconstructed image domain. To address these cross-domain degradations, this paper proposes RCL-Mamba, a measurement-oriented dual-domain State Space Model (SSM)-based image restoration network. The framework adopts a cascaded joint processing strategy: it first corrects the rotational blur in the projection domain and subsequently suppresses the sparse artifacts in the image domain. Additionally, we design a Mamba-CNN dual-branch module to adaptively balance large-scale blur correction with local detail recovery. Evaluations on both simulated datasets and real-world Printed Circuit Board (PCB) scans demonstrate that RCL-Mamba outperforms existing baselines in blur removal, artifact suppression, and structural preservation. Line-profile-based structural measurement further verifies that the proposed method better preserves via/pad boundaries and slender trace profiles. Crucially, by reducing the required scanning views from 512 to 64, our method enhances inspection efficiency by approximately 8-fold without compromising reconstruction quality, offering a robust measurement-oriented restoration solution for high-throughput RCL inspection with improved structural measurement fidelity.

中文摘要翻译: 旋转扫描计算机分层成像（RCL）广泛用于大型平面组件的无损检测（NDT）。然而，为促进快速检测，常采用连续稀疏扫描，其中曝光期间的角度积分效应在投影域引入旋转模糊。此外，稀疏采样固有的数据不完全性在重建图像域表现为稀疏伪影。为解决这些跨域退化问题，本文提出RCL-Mamba，一种面向测量的双域状态空间模型（SSM）图像恢复网络。该框架采用级联联合处理策略：首先校正投影域的旋转模糊，随后抑制图像域的稀疏伪影。此外，我们设计了Mamba-CNN双分支模块，以自适应地平衡大尺度模糊校正与局部细节恢复。在模拟数据集和真实印刷电路板（PCB）扫描数据上的评估表明，RCL-Mamba在模糊去除、伪影抑制和结构保持方面优于现有基线。基于线剖面的结构测量进一步验证了该方法能更好地保留过孔/焊盘边界及细导线轮廓。至关重要的是，通过将所需扫描视角从512减少至64，我们的方法在不牺牲重建质量的前提下将检测效率提升约8倍，为高吞吐RCL检测提供了一种鲁棒的面向测量的恢复方案，提升了结构测量保真度。

</details>


### [69] [Language-Assisted Super-Resolution from Real-World Low-Resolution Patches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31363)
*Joonkyu Park,Kyoung Mu Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出LA-SR框架，利用视觉-语言模型将未配对超分辨率问题映射到语言空间，通过语言引导损失实现真实场景下的单图像超分辨率。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有超分辨率方法依赖人工合成降质（如手工核函数或相机ISP调整）来生成低分辨率图像，但这类合成降质无法捕捉真实低分辨率图像的复杂降质模式，导致模型在实际应用中泛化能力差。

Method: 提出LA-SR框架，核心思想是将未配对超分辨率问题重新定义到语言空间。利用视觉-语言模型将图像投影到同时表征内容和质量的语义丰富空间中，并设计两种语言引导损失：语言内容损失（保持语义保真度）和语言质量损失（增强感知真实性）。

Result: LA-SR能够有效对真实低分辨率输入进行超分辨率重建，生成比传统基于合成数据训练的方法更真实的输出，克服了合成数据方法的局限性。

Conclusion: 利用单张高质量图像中不同深度区域天然存在分辨率差异这一观察，结合视觉-语言模型在语言空间中进行对齐，能够有效解决真实场景下的未配对超分辨率问题。

Original Abstract: Single image super-resolution aims to reconstruct high-resolution (HR) images from low-resolution (LR) inputs. Training SR models typically requires paired HR-LR data, which is difficult to obtain in reality. As a result, most methods synthesize LR images by artificially degrading HR images with handcrafted kernels or camera ISP adjustments. However, these synthetic degradations fail to capture the complexity of real LR images, leading to poor generalization in practice. To address this, we observe that even within a single high-quality image, regions at different depths exhibit varying resolutions, where distant regions act as LR patches and closer ones as HR patches. This allows the extraction of real, degradation-induced LR patches from real images. Since these LR patches lack paired HR counterparts, we propose LA-SR (Language Assistant for SR), a novel framework for unpaired SR. The key idea of LA-SR is to redefine unpaired SR in the language space, using vision-language models to bridge the LR-HR gap. LA-SR projects images into a semantically rich space representing both content and quality, and applies two language-guided losses: linguistic content loss to preserve semantic fidelity, and linguistic quality loss to enhance perceptual realism. With this alignment, LA-SR effectively super-resolves real LR inputs, producing realistic outputs that overcome the limitations of synthetic-data-trained methods.

中文摘要翻译: 单图像超分辨率旨在从低分辨率（LR）输入中重建高分辨率（HR）图像。训练超分辨率模型通常需要配对的HR-LR数据，而这在实际中难以获取。因此，大多数方法通过人工设计的核函数或相机ISP调整对HR图像进行人为降质来合成LR图像。然而，这些合成降质无法捕捉真实LR图像的复杂性，导致模型在实践中泛化能力差。为解决这一问题，我们观察到即使在一张高质量图像中，不同深度区域的图像分辨率也不同——远处区域相当于LR块，近处区域相当于HR块。这使我们能够从真实图像中提取出由真实降质过程产生的LR块。由于这些LR块缺乏配对的HR对应物，我们提出了LA-SR（Language Assistant for SR），一种用于未配对超分辨率的新框架。LA-SR的核心思想是将未配对超分辨率问题重新定义到语言空间中，利用视觉-语言模型来桥接LR-HR之间的差距。LA-SR将图像投影到一个同时表征内容与质量的语义丰富空间中，并应用两种语言引导的损失：语言内容损失以保持语义保真度，以及语言质量损失以增强感知真实性。通过这种对齐，LA-SR能够有效地对真实LR输入进行超分辨率重建，生成超越合成数据训练方法局限性的逼真输出。

</details>


### [70] [Domain Adaptive Object Detection via Dual-Stream Bilevel-Cycle Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31373)
*Yannan Chen,Wenqiang Wang,Ruoyu Chen,Jiancheng Wang,Mingbo Yang,Yaowei Wang,Wei Wang,Xiaochun Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出双流双层循环优化框架，将Cycle Self-Training（CST）扩展到目标检测领域，通过双重流设计同时处理分类和回归任务，并利用Mean Teacher和额外归一化解决训练不稳定和损失爆炸问题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: CST在图像分类中表现优异，但将其扩展到目标检测面临三大挑战：三种不可靠伪标签（分类错误、定位错误及其组合）限制性能上限；多目标检测导致训练不稳定；回归坐标数值范围过宽导致损失爆炸。

Method: 提出双流双层循环优化框架：(1) 将CST同时应用于分类和回归两个流；(2) 基于Mean Teacher构建CST以防止训练不稳定；(3) 采用额外归一化将回归边界框映射到标准化空间以解决损失爆炸问题；(4) 提供了回归边界的理论推导。

Result: 在四个跨域标准场景上的大量实验表明，所提框架取得了显著结果。

Conclusion: 通过双流设计和理论保障，成功将CST从图像分类扩展到目标检测，有效解决了伪标签不可靠、训练不稳定和损失爆炸等关键问题。

Original Abstract: Cycle self-training (CST) breaks the shared classifier assumption of the standard self-training framework, which is effective for unsupervised domain adaptation and exploits unlabeled target data by training with target pseudo-labels. CST introduces a target classifier and employs an inner-outer loop updating strategy, addressing the issue of unreliable pseudo-labels and enabling pseudo-labels to generalize across domains. Despite its success in image classification, extending CST to object detection faces three main challenges. First, the upper bound of CST in object detection is constrained by three types of unreliable pseudo-labels, such as classification error alone, localization error alone, and their combination. Second, since object detection involves detecting multiple target objects, directly applying CST leads to training insta bility. Third, a wider numerical range of regression coordinates leads to exploding losses. To this end, we apply CST to both classification and regression and propose the Dual-Stream Bilevel-Cycle Optimization framework. Specifically, we construct CST upon Mean Teacher to prevent training instability and use extra normalization to map the regression bounding box into a standardized space, effectively addressing exploding losses. Also, we provide a theoretical derivation of the regression bound. Extensive experiments across four cross domain standard scenarios demonstrate that our framework achieves considerable results.

中文摘要翻译: 循环自训练（CST）打破了标准自训练框架的共享分类器假设，该方法在无监督域适应中表现有效，通过使用目标伪标签训练来利用未标记的目标数据。CST引入目标分类器并采用内外循环更新策略，解决了不可靠伪标签的问题，并使伪标签能够跨域泛化。尽管在图像分类中取得了成功，但将CST扩展到目标检测面临三大挑战。首先，CST在目标检测中的上限受到三种类型不可靠伪标签的制约，即仅分类错误、仅定位错误以及两者的组合。其次，由于目标检测涉及检测多个目标对象，直接应用CST会导致训练不稳定。第三，回归坐标数值范围过宽会导致损失爆炸。为此，我们将CST同时应用于分类和回归，并提出双流双层循环优化框架。具体而言，我们在Mean Teacher之上构建CST以防止训练不稳定，并使用额外归一化将回归边界框映射到标准化空间，从而有效解决损失爆炸问题。同时，我们提供了回归边界的理论推导。在四个跨域标准场景上的大量实验表明，我们的框架取得了显著的结果。

</details>


### [71] [MAPE: Defending Against Transferable Adversarial Attacks Using Multi-Source Adversarial Perturbations Elimination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31378)
*Xinlei Liu,Jichao Xie,Tao Hu,Peng Yi,Yuxiang Hu,Shumin Huo,Zhen Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对可迁移对抗攻击，提出多源对抗扰动消除（MAPE）防御方法，包含单源对抗扰动消除（SAPE）机制和预训练模型概率调度算法（PPSA），在黑盒攻击下对CIFAR-10和Mini-ImageNet分别达到95.1%和71.5%以上的平均防御率。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 可迁移对抗攻击具有高隐蔽性和检测难度，现有防御方法难以有效应对来自不同替代模型的多样化可迁移攻击。

Method: MAPE包含两个核心组件：(1) SAPE：采用通道注意力U-Net作为防御模型，利用预训练模型（如ResNet）生成的对抗样本来训练，消除已知的对抗扰动；(2) PPSA：引入模型差异量化和负动量，策略性地调度多个预训练模型，最大化训练过程中对抗样本的差异，提升防御模型消除对抗扰动的鲁棒性。

Result: 以ResNet-34为目标模型的黑盒攻击场景下，在CIFAR-10上平均防御率超过95.1%，在Mini-ImageNet上超过71.5%，达到当时最优性能。

Conclusion: MAPE能够有效消除各类对抗样本中的对抗扰动，为来自不同替代模型的攻击提供鲁棒防御。

Original Abstract: Neural networks are vulnerable to meticulously crafted adversarial examples, leading to high-confidence misclassifications in image classification tasks. Due to their consistency with regular input patterns and the absence of reliance on the target model and its output information, transferable adversarial attacks exhibit a notably high stealthiness and detection difficulty, making them a significant focus of defense. In this work, we propose a deep learning defense known as multi-source adversarial perturbations elimination (MAPE) to counter diverse transferable attacks. MAPE comprises the single-source adversarial perturbation elimination (SAPE) mechanism and the pre-trained models probabilistic scheduling algorithm (PPSA). SAPE utilizes a thoughtfully designed channel-attention U-Net as the defense model and employs adversarial examples generated by a pre-trained model (e.g., ResNet) for its training, thereby enabling the elimination of known adversarial perturbations. PPSA introduces model difference quantification and negative momentum to strategically schedule multiple pre-trained models, thereby maximizing the differences among adversarial examples during the defense model's training and enhancing its robustness in eliminating adversarial perturbations. MAPE effectively eliminates adversarial perturbations in various adversarial examples, providing a robust defense against attacks from different substitute models. In a black-box attack scenario utilizing ResNet-34 as the target model, our approach achieves average defense rates of over 95.1\% on CIFAR-10 and over 71.5\% on Mini-ImageNet, demonstrating state-of-the-art performance.

中文摘要翻译: 神经网络在图像分类任务中容易受到精心设计的对抗样本的干扰，导致高置信度的错误分类。由于可迁移对抗攻击与正常输入模式具有一致性，且不依赖于目标模型及其输出信息，因而表现出极高的隐蔽性和检测难度，成为防御研究的重点。本文提出一种名为多源对抗扰动消除（MAPE）的深度学习防御方法，用于应对多样化的可迁移攻击。MAPE包括单源对抗扰动消除（SAPE）机制和预训练模型概率调度算法（PPSA）。SAPE采用精心设计的通道注意力U-Net作为防御模型，并利用预训练模型（如ResNet）生成的对抗样本进行训练，从而实现对已知对抗扰动的消除。PPSA引入模型差异量化和负动量，策略性地调度多个预训练模型，从而在防御模型训练过程中最大化对抗样本之间的差异，增强其消除对抗扰动的鲁棒性。MAPE能有效消除各类对抗样本中的对抗扰动，为来自不同替代模型的攻击提供鲁棒防御。在以ResNet-34为目标模型的黑盒攻击场景下，本方法在CIFAR-10上平均防御率超过95.1%，在Mini-ImageNet上超过71.5%，展现出当时最优的性能。

</details>


### [72] [MS-Resampler: Multi-Scope Visual Resampling for Efficient Multimodal LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31383)
*Zhongyang Li,Yaqian Li,Faming Fang,Rinyoichi Takezoe,Zi-Hao Bo,Cheng Qian,Mo Guang,Guixu Zhang,Kaiwen Long*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MS-Resampler，一种多范围视觉重采样框架，通过注入显式空间范围先验到重采样注意力中，实现从局部到全局的多粒度视觉信息聚合，在十个多模态基准上一致优于传统单范围重采样器。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有MLLM中的重采样器采用单一固定的聚合范围（全局交叉注意力），会模糊细粒度的局部证据，在固定token预算下难以同时捕捉局部细节和全局上下文。

Method: MS-Resampler实例化多个范围特定的重采样器，通过向重采样注意力中注入显式空间范围先验，使每个分支在特定粒度上聚合视觉信息（从局部到全局），然后自适应融合各分支输出得到最终视觉表示。

Result: 在十个公开多模态基准上，MS-Resampler相比传统单范围重采样器，在视觉理解和多模态推理上取得一致性提升，且仅引入极小计算开销。

Conclusion: 多范围视觉重采样策略能有效平衡局部细节与全局上下文信息的捕捉，是提升MLLM视觉表征能力的轻量级有效方案。

Original Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) typically employ resampling-based projectors to transform dense visual features into a compact token sequence for language modeling. Most existing resamplers adopt a single, fixed aggregation scope via global cross-attention, which can blur fine-grained local evidence and limit the ability to capture both local details and global context within a fixed token budget. In this work, we propose MS-Resampler, a multi-scope visual resampling framework for MLLMs. MS-Resampler instantiates multiple scope-specific resamplers by injecting explicit spatial scope priors into the resampling attention, enabling each branch to aggregate visual information at a particular granularity from local to global. The outputs of these scope-specific resamplers are then adaptively fused to produce the final visual representations for language modeling. Extensive experiments on ten public multimodal benchmarks show that MS-Resampler consistently improves visual understanding and multimodal reasoning over conventional single-scope resamplers, while introducing only minimal computational overhead.

中文摘要翻译: 多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）通常采用基于重采样（resampling）的投影器，将稠密的视觉特征转换为紧凑的token序列用于语言建模。现有的大多数重采样器通过全局交叉注意力（global cross-attention）采用单一固定的聚合范围，这可能会模糊细粒度的局部证据，并限制在固定token预算内同时捕捉局部细节和全局上下文的能力。本文提出MS-Resampler，一种面向MLLMs的多范围视觉重采样框架。MS-Resampler通过向重采样注意力中注入显式的空间范围先验，实例化多个范围特定的重采样器，使每个分支能够在从局部到全局的特定粒度上聚合视觉信息。随后，这些范围特定重采样器的输出被自适应地融合，生成用于语言建模的最终视觉表示。在十个公开多模态基准上的大量实验表明，相比传统单范围重采样器，MS-Resampler在视觉理解和多模态推理上取得一致性提升，且仅引入极小的计算开销。

</details>


### [73] [One Video, One World: Turning Monocular Video into Physical 4D Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31388)
*Junhao Chen,Boran Zhang,Mingjin Chen,Henghaofan Zhang,Saining Zhang,Congcong Zhu,Hao Zhao,Ruqi Huang,Zhihao Li,Yufei Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OVOW提出首个无需训练的框架，能从单目视频重建实例级、仿真就绪的4D网格场景，通过四阶段流程（视觉语言模型发现实例、类别感知重建、迭代优化、物理组装）生成水密网格，并建立首个结构化Video-to-4D评估基准。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有4D重建虽渲染质量高，但输出（隐式场、高斯原语、点云）缺乏物理仿真和具身AI所需的水密拓扑、实例分离和标准化物理接口，因此需要一种能直接生成仿真就绪的4D网格场景的方法。

Method: 四阶段流程：（1）视觉语言模型发现、标注并分类所有实例的运动类型；（2）类别感知重建为刚体生成实例级网格，为可变形体生成拓扑一致的网格序列；（3）迭代渲染-匹配-优化过程恢复度量尺度和6-DoF位姿轨迹；（4）物理基础组装确保地面接触和物体间支撑。所有运动通过顶点变形直接建模，无需类别先验或骨架绑定。

Result: 在两个合成基准测试（静态和4D）中，OVOW在布局和几何精度上达到最佳，光度和语义误差最低；单目视频运行速度比基线快一到两个数量级；下游物理仿真验证了物理稳定性。

Conclusion: OVOW成功弥合了视觉重建与物理仿真之间的差距，首个实现了从单目视频到实例级、仿真就绪4D网格场景的训练自由重建，同时建立的基准和合成引擎为未来4D世界模型和具身AI提供了支持。

Original Abstract: We introduce \textbf{OVOW}, the first training-free system that reconstructs \emph{instance-level, simulation-ready} 4D mesh scenes from a single monocular video. Recent 4D reconstruction achieves impressive rendering quality, but its outputs (\eg, implicit fields, Gaussian primitives, or point clouds) lack the watertight topology, instance separation, and standardized physical interfaces required by physics simulators and embodied AI. OVOW closes this gap with a four-stage pipeline: a vision-language model discovers, labels, and motion-classifies all instances; category-aware reconstruction yields per-instance meshes for rigid objects and topology-consistent mesh sequences for deformable ones; an iterative render-match-optimize procedure recovers metric scale and 6-DoF pose trajectories; and physics-grounded assembly enforces ground contact and inter-object support. Crucially, we model all motion, rigid and non-rigid, through direct vertex deformation without category-specific priors or skeleton rigging, producing watertight mesh scenes ready for downstream physics simulation and editing. We further establish the first benchmark for \emph{structured Video-to-4D} evaluation, with metrics for geometric correctness, instance separation, and physical plausibility beyond visual fidelity; the same pipeline doubles as a scalable engine for \emph{synthesizing} paired video-to-4D simulation data for future 4D world models and embodied AI. Across two synthetic benchmarks (static and 4D), OVOW attains the best overall layout and geometry accuracy and the lowest photometric and semantic error among all baselines, and on monocular video runs one to two orders of magnitude faster than the baselines, while downstream physics simulation confirms its physical stability.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出\textbf{OVOW}，这是首个无需训练的系统，能够从单目视频重建\emph{实例级、仿真就绪}的4D网格场景。现有的4D重建方法虽取得了令人印象深刻的渲染质量，但其输出（如隐式场、高斯原语或点云）缺乏物理仿真器和具身AI所需的水密拓扑、实例分离和标准化物理接口。OVOW通过四阶段流程弥补了这一差距：视觉语言模型发现、标注并对所有实例进行运动分类；类别感知重建为刚体生成实例级网格，为可变形体生成拓扑一致的网格序列；迭代渲染-匹配-优化过程恢复度量尺度和6自由度位姿轨迹；物理基础组装确保地面接触和物体间支撑。关键地，我们通过直接顶点变形建模所有运动（刚性和非刚性），无需类别特定先验或骨架绑定，生成可直接用于下游物理仿真和编辑的水密网格场景。我们进一步建立了首个\emph{结构化视频到4D}评估基准，包含超越视觉保真度的几何正确性、实例分离和物理合理性指标；同一流程还可作为可扩展引擎，用于\emph{合成}配对的视频到4D仿真数据，以支持未来4D世界模型和具身AI。在两个合成基准测试（静态和4D）中，OVOW在所有基线中取得了最佳的整体布局和几何精度以及最低的光度和语义误差，在单目视频上的运行速度比基线快一到两个数量级，同时下游物理仿真验证了其物理稳定性。

</details>


### [74] [Visual Semantic Entropy: Do Vision Language Models Recognize Visual Ambiguity?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31407)
*Ta Duc Huy,Trang Nguyen,Townim Chowdhury,Ankit Yadav,Minh-Son To,Zhibin Liao,Johan W. Verjans,Vu Minh Hieu Phan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出视觉语义熵（VSE）方法，仅扰动图像而非文本，通过聚类生成答案并计算质量加权离散度来估计视觉歧义不确定性，在五个VLM和五个VQA基准上达到SOTA。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于熵的不确定性估计方法（如语义熵SE）在视觉语言模型中因过置信的视觉嵌入抑制输出多样性而失效；而基于输入扰动的方法（文本释义或图文联合扰动）产生的多样性往往由文本变化主导，无法反映真实的视觉歧义。

Method: 提出视觉语义熵（VSE）：仅对图像进行随机扰动（保持文本查询固定），通过聚类生成答案形成语义原型，并计算原型的质量加权离散度作为不确定性度量。

Result: 在五个现代视觉语言模型和五个VQA基准上的广泛评估表明，VSE有效捕获视觉歧义，在VLM不确定性估计上达到了新的SOTA。

Conclusion: 仅扰动图像而保持文本固定，配合语义原型聚类与质量加权离散度计算，能更准确地估计视觉语言模型在视觉歧义输入上的不确定性。

Original Abstract: Vision-language models can produce confident answers on visually ambiguous inputs, resulting in biased predictions. Common entropy-based methods, such as Semantic Entropy (SE), rely on output diversity. Yet our analysis shows that overconfident visual embeddings suppress output diversity under stochastic decoding, causing SE to underestimate uncertainty in such cases. Recent methods instead probe output diversity through input perturbations, including textual paraphrasing or joint text-image perturbations, and show improved performance. We study these approaches and reveals that the resulting variability is often dominated by textual changes rather than visual evidence, causing uncertainty estimates to reflect prompt sensitivity rather than visual ambiguity. We therefore propose Visual Semantic Entropy (VSE), which perturbs only the image to probe nearby visual variations while keeping the text query fixed. VSE measures uncertainty by clustering generated answers into semantic prototypes and computing the mass-weighted dispersion among them. Extensive evaluation across five modern vision-language models and five diverse VQA benchmarks demonstrates that VSE effectively captures visual ambiguity, establishing a new state-of-the-art for VLM uncertainty estimation.

中文摘要翻译: 视觉语言模型可能在视觉上模糊的输入上产生自信的答案，从而导致有偏的预测。常见的基于熵的方法（如语义熵）依赖于输出的多样性。然而，我们的分析表明，在随机解码下，过置信的视觉嵌入抑制了输出的多样性，导致语义熵在此类情况下低估了不确定性。近期的方法转而通过输入扰动（包括文本释义或图文联合扰动）来探测输出多样性，并显示出改进的性能。我们研究了这些方法，并发现由此产生的多样性通常由文本变化主导而非视觉证据，导致不确定性估计反映的是提示敏感性而非视觉歧义。因此，我们提出了视觉语义熵（VSE），该方法仅扰动图像以探测附近的视觉变化，同时保持文本查询不变。VSE通过将生成的答案聚类为语义原型并计算原型的质量加权离散度来度量不确定性。在五个现代视觉语言模型和五个多样化VQA基准上的广泛评估表明，VSE有效捕获了视觉歧义，在VLM不确定性估计上建立了新的最先进水平。

</details>


### [75] [Temporal Preservation over Processing: Diagnosing and Designing Spatiotemporal Single-Stage Video Detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31421)
*Karam Tomotaki-Dawoud,Anna Hilsmann,Peter Eisert,Sebastian Bosse*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文从诊断和架构两个方向解决单阶段视频目标检测器是否真正利用时序上下文的问题：提出TemporalLens诊断框架，通过受控扰动揭示模型行为差异；提出YOLO-3D实时时空检测器，证明保持骨干网络中的时序深度是性能提升的主要驱动力。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有单阶段视频目标检测器在时间关键型应用中被广泛部署，但标准指标（如mAP）仅奖励正确预测，无法区分模型是真正利用时序上下文推理，还是仅依赖单个信息帧。这一评估盲区阻碍了对模型行为的深入理解与改进。

Method: 1) 提出TemporalLens诊断框架：通过受控扰动、结构化遮挡、时序打乱、冗余注入和分辨率退化等测试，检测模型是否跨时间使用信息。2) 提出YOLO-3D：基于YOLOv8的模块化实时时空检测器，通过保持骨干网络中的时序深度来融合多帧信息。

Result: TemporalLens揭示了mAP不可见的行为差异：堆叠帧的2D模型在目标帧被移除时性能崩溃，而时空模型可从早期帧恢复预测，这是真实时序依赖的标志。YOLO-3D在32帧平均下，仅通过保持骨干网络中的时序深度即可在mAP@50上提升3.7个百分点（跨尺度平均）。

Conclusion: 所提出的诊断框架（TemporalLens）与架构（YOLO-3D）共同将“该检测器是否真正使用时序推理”这一模糊问题转化为可测量、可操作的具体问题。

Original Abstract: Single-stage video object detectors are increasingly deployed in time-critical applications, yet it remains unclear whether these models genuinely reason over temporal context or merely exploit a single informative frame-a gap hidden by standard metrics, which reward correct predictions regardless of how they are reached. We address this from two complementary directions: first, we propose TemporalLens, a model-agnostic diagnostic framework probing temporal dependence through controlled perturbations, structured occlusions, temporal shuffling, redundancy injection, and resolution degradation, revealing whether a detector actually uses information across time. Applied to stacked-frame 2D detectors and our YOLO-3D architecture, it exposes behavioural differences invisible to mAP: stacked 2D models collapse when the target frame is removed, while spatiotemporal models recover predictions from earlier frames, a signature of real temporal reliance. Second, we detail YOLO-3D, a modular real-time spatiotemporal detector built on YOLOv8, and show that simply preserving temporal depth through the backbone is the dominant performance driver (+3.7 pp mAP@50 at 32 frames averaged across scales). Together, the diagnostics and architecture turn "does this detector reason over time?" into a measurable, actionable question.

中文摘要翻译: 单阶段视频目标检测器越来越多地部署在时间关键型应用中，但目前尚不清楚这些模型是真正基于时序上下文进行推理，还是仅仅利用了单个信息帧——这一差距被标准指标所掩盖，因为标准指标仅奖励正确预测，而不关心预测是如何得出的。我们从两个互补方向来解决这一问题：首先，我们提出TemporalLens，一个与模型无关的诊断框架，通过受控扰动、结构化遮挡、时序打乱、冗余注入和分辨率退化来探测时序依赖关系，揭示检测器是否真正跨时间使用信息。将该框架应用于堆叠帧的2D检测器以及我们的YOLO-3D架构后，它暴露了mAP无法识别的行为差异：堆叠帧的2D模型在目标帧被移除时性能崩溃，而时空模型可以从早期帧恢复预测，这是真实时序依赖的标志。其次，我们详细介绍了YOLO-3D，一个基于YOLOv8构建的模块化实时时空检测器，并证明了仅通过保持骨干网络中的时序深度就是性能提升的主要驱动力（在32帧平均下，跨尺度mAP@50提升3.7个百分点）。综合来看，该诊断框架与架构共同将“该检测器是否使用时序推理”转化为一个可测量、可操作的问题。

</details>


### [76] [No Prompt, No Leaks: A Robust Generative Steganography Framework via Prompt-Free Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31427)
*Jingwen Cai,Fen Xiao,Shuhua Deng,Xieping Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种无提示的扩散图像隐写框架，通过级联仿射耦合模块和风格语义先验控制生成过程，结合预测-校正机制优化轨迹，在安全性、重建精度和可控性上达到最优性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于文本提示的隐写扩散模型受限于文本提示的灵活性不足，难以生成高质量的隐写图像并精确恢复秘密图像。

Method: 1) 级联仿射耦合模块(CACM)建立秘密图像与其潜表示之间的双射确定性映射；2) 将风格语义集成到扩散过程中控制潜表示，确保视觉不可感知性；3) 引入预测-校正机制，通过当前状态与预测下一状态的反馈迭代优化生成轨迹，缓解无条件反向过程中的轨迹偏差。

Result: 在安全性、秘密图像重建准确性和可控性方面，所提方法相比现有最先进方法取得了具有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: 无提示的扩散隐写框架通过风格语义先验和预测-校正机制有效提升了隐写图像质量和秘密信息恢复精度。

Original Abstract: Generative image steganography synthesizes stego images directly from secret information to achieve inherent security advantages. Latent Diffusion Models (LDMs) have recently emerged as a fundamental image steganography framework that modulates secret latent representations with text prompts. Limited by the inflexibility of text prompts, these methods still struggle to generate high-quality stego images and accurately recover secret images. In this work, we propose a prompt-free diffusion image steganography framework that integrates style semantic priors to control more robust and reliable stego image generation. Specifically, a Cascaded Affine Coupling Module (CACM) establishes a bijective, deterministic mapping between a secret image and its latent representation. Then, style semantics are integrated into the diffusion process to control latent representation and ensure visual imperceptibility in the generated stego images. To mitigate trajectory deviations stemming from the unconditioned reverse process, a predictor-corrector mechanism is introduced to iteratively refine the generation trajectory via feedback from the current and predicted next states. Extensive experimental results show that the proposed method achieves competitive performance compared to state-of-the-art methods in terms of security, secret image reconstruction accuracy and controllability.

中文摘要翻译: 生成式图像隐写通过直接从秘密信息合成隐写图像，实现了固有的安全性优势。潜扩散模型(LDMs)最近作为一种基础的图像隐写框架出现，通过文本提示调节秘密潜表示。受限于文本提示的灵活性不足，这些方法仍然难以生成高质量的隐写图像并精确恢复秘密图像。本文提出了一种无提示的扩散图像隐写框架，该框架融合风格语义先验以控制更鲁棒和可靠的隐写图像生成。具体而言，级联仿射耦合模块(CACM)在秘密图像与其潜表示之间建立了双射、确定性的映射。然后，将风格语义集成到扩散过程中以控制潜表示，确保生成的隐写图像具有视觉不可感知性。为缓解无条件反向过程中产生的轨迹偏差，引入了一种预测-校正机制，通过当前状态和预测下一状态的反馈迭代优化生成轨迹。大量实验结果表明，所提方法在安全性、秘密图像重建准确性和可控性方面相比现有最先进方法取得了具有竞争力的性能。

</details>


### [77] [Temporal Training Strategies for Left Atrium and Left Atrial Appendage Segmentation in Dynamic Contrast 4DCT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31444)
*David Montalvo-García,Lauren Severance,Elliot R. McVeigh,María J. Ledesma-Carbayo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文研究了动态4D心脏CT中左心房和左心耳分割任务的时间训练集设计策略，发现使用完整帧序列训练效果最佳，而基于生理学选择的子集在充盈期后表现相当，凸显了时间多样性对鲁棒分割的重要性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 动态增强心脏CT中左心房和左心耳的分割因时间对比度剧烈变化和仅有一帧标注而面临挑战，现有方法在训练鲁棒性和控制标注噪声之间存在权衡，亟需研究如何设计时间训练集以优化分割性能。

Method: 基于nnUNet框架，比较三种时间训练集设计策略：（1）最小两帧数据集（模拟临床实践）；（2）基于生理学选择的帧子集；（3）完整的27帧序列。同时评估基于前景的归一化方法的影响。

Result: 完整帧序列训练在早期低对比度阶段表现最优；生理学子集从充盈期开始性能与完整序列相当；基于完整数据集导出的归一化参数能提升缩减数据集在低对比度帧上的性能，但无法完全弥合差距。

Conclusion: 时间多样性对动态CT中鲁棒分割至关重要，但精心选择的帧子集可以在下游应用的性能和效率之间提供有效的折中方案。

Original Abstract: Dynamic contrast-enhanced cardiac CT enables time-resolved analysis of contrast filling and washout in the left atrium (LA) and left atrial appendage (LAA), with potential applications for assessing blood stasis in atrial fibrillation (AF). Accurate segmentation across all frames is required for such analysis but is challenging due to large temporal contrast variations and the use of a single annotation per registered sequence. This creates a trade-off between training for robustness and limiting label noise. In this study, we investigate how temporal training-set design affects nnUNet-based segmentation of the LA and LAA in dynamic 4DCT. We compare training using a minimal two-frame dataset reflecting standard clinical practice, a physiologically selected subset of frames, and the full 27-frame sequence. We further evaluate the impact of foreground-based normalization. Training with all frames yielded the best performance in early low-contrast phases. However, the physiologically selected subset achieved comparable performance from the filling phase onward. Applying normalization parameters derived from the full dataset improved performance of reduced datasets in low-contrast frames, but did not fully close the gap. These findings highlight the importance of temporal diversity in training data for robust segmentation in dynamic CT, while indicating that carefully selected frame subsets may provide an effective trade-off between performance and efficiency for downstream applications.

中文摘要翻译: 动态增强心脏CT能够对左心房和左心耳中的对比剂充盈和洗脱进行时间分辨分析，在评估心房颤动患者血液淤滞方面具有潜在应用价值。此类分析需要对所有帧进行精确分割，但由于时间对比度剧烈变化以及每个配准序列仅有一帧标注，精确分割面临挑战。这在鲁棒性训练与限制标注噪声之间产生了一种权衡。本研究探讨了时间训练集设计如何影响动态4DCT中左心房和左心室的基于nnUNet的分割。我们比较了三种训练方式：使用反映标准临床实践的最小两帧数据集、基于生理学选择的帧子集以及完整的27帧序列。我们还评估了基于前景的归一化的影响。使用所有帧进行训练在早期低对比度阶段取得了最佳性能。然而，从充盈期开始，基于生理学选择的子集实现了与之相当的性能。应用从完整数据集导出的归一化参数能改善缩减数据集在低对比度帧上的表现，但无法完全弥合性能差距。这些发现凸显了时间多样性对动态CT中鲁棒分割的重要性，同时表明精心选择的帧子集可为下游应用提供性能与效率之间的有效折中。

</details>


### [78] [Towards a foundational model for recognising diastematic Gregorian notation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31454)
*Daniel Kurek,Jan Hajič*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于S-GABC的通用编码方案，将四个不同的格里高利符号数据集统一编码，并训练了一个端到端的基础模型，在所有四个数据集上达到了新的最优性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的四个格里高利符号数据集采用不同的编码格式，导致模型无法跨数据集共享知识，限制了端到端识别方法的泛化能力和性能提升。

Method: 设计了一种基于S-GABC提议的通用编码方案，将四个不同编码的数据集统一转换为该通用编码，然后训练一个共享的端到端基础模型。

Result: 该共享的端到端基础模型在所有四个数据集上均取得了新的最优（state-of-the-art）结果。

Conclusion: 统一的编码方案和共享的基础模型能够有效解决多数据集编码不一致的问题，并显著提升格里高利符号光学识别的性能。

Original Abstract: Optical recognition of Gregorian notation has recently been attempted with end-to-end methods, with four datasets introduced. However, each of these datasets is in a different encoding. We design a common encoding based on the S-GABC proposal, convert all four datasets to this common encoding, and train a shared end-to-end foundational model for diastematic Gregorian notation that establishes a new state of the art across all four datasets.

中文摘要翻译: 近年来，格里高利符号的光学识别已尝试使用端到端方法，并引入了四个数据集。然而，这些数据集各自采用不同的编码格式。我们基于S-GABC提议设计了一种通用编码，将所有四个数据集转换为该通用编码，并训练了一个共享的端到端基础模型用于间隔格里高利符号识别。该模型在所有四个数据集上均建立了新的最优性能水平。

</details>


### [79] [AeroVerse-SatAgent: UAV-Satellite Collaborative Spatial Reasoning Inspired by the Dual Visual Pathway Theory of Cognitive Neuroscience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31467)
*Wenyi Zhang,Fanglong Yao,Youzhi Liu,Peng Hu,Zhengqiu Zhu,Chen Gao,Xian Sun,Kun Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SatAgent 提出了一种无人机-卫星协同空间推理模型，通过双路径视觉机制、3D几何重建和多视图拓扑语义对齐，在复杂城市环境中显著提升了空间推理的准确性和鲁棒性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有无人机空间推理方法受限于单视角感知易受遮挡和视角畸变影响，且多数VLM缺乏显式几何建模，依赖语义线索，在视角和尺度变化下推理不一致。

Method: 提出SatAgent模型，包含三个核心组件：(1) 几何感知3D重建编码器，将2D UAV特征提升为显式3D空间表示；(2) 多视角拓扑-语义对齐模块，在统一BEV坐标系中融合跨视角特征；(3) 多视角一致性损失，鼓励视角不变表示。同时构建了首个大规模无人机-卫星协同多视角空间推理数据集SatAgent-SR130K。

Result: 在多种任务上，SatAgent比最先进的通用基础模型和专用空间推理模型分别高出25.91%和11.69%，在复杂几何关系推理中达到特别高的准确率。

Conclusion: 通过无人机-卫星双视角协同和显式几何建模，SatAgent有效解决了单视角推理的局限性，为复杂环境下的空间推理提供了新范式，验证了多模态协同感知的显著优势。

Original Abstract: With the rapid advancement of aerospace embodied intelligence, enabling Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) to autonomously understand and reason about complex environments has become increasingly important. However, existing UAV-based spatial reasoning approaches face critical limitations: single-view perception renders them vulnerable to occlusions and perspective distortions, while most VLMs lack explicit geometric modeling, relying on semantic cues and yielding inconsistent reasoning under viewpoint and scale variations. To address these challenges, we propose SatAgent, a UAV-Satellite collaborative spatial reasoning model inspired by the dual-pathway mechanism of the human visual system. By jointly leveraging satellite and UAV perspectives, SatAgent enables robust, accurate reasoning in complex urban environments. We first introduce a Geometric-Aware 3D Reconstruction Encoder that elevates 2D UAV features into explicit 3D spatial representations. Next, we design a multi-view topology-semantic alignment module integrating cross-view features within a unified BEV coordinate system. We further introduce a multi-view consistency loss encouraging viewpoint-invariant representations. Finally, we construct SatAgent-SR130K, the first large-scale UAV-Satellite collaborative multi-view spatial reasoning dataset. Experiments show SatAgent outperforms state-of-the-art general-purpose foundation models and specialized spatial reasoning models by 25.91\% and 11.69\%, respectively, across diverse tasks, achieving particularly high accuracy in complex geometric relationship reasoning.

中文摘要翻译: 随着航空航天具身智能的快速发展，使无人机能够自主理解和推理复杂环境变得日益重要。然而，现有的基于无人机的空间推理方法面临关键局限性：单视角感知使其易受遮挡和视角畸变影响，而大多数视觉语言模型缺乏显式几何建模，依赖语义线索，在视角和尺度变化下产生不一致的推理结果。为解决这些挑战，我们提出了SatAgent——一种受人类视觉系统双路径机制启发的无人机-卫星协同空间推理模型。通过联合利用卫星和无人机视角，SatAgent能够在复杂城市环境中实现鲁棒、准确的空间推理。我们首先引入了几何感知3D重建编码器，将2D无人机特征提升为显式3D空间表示。接着，我们设计了一个多视角拓扑-语义对齐模块，在统一的BEV坐标系中融合跨视角特征。我们进一步引入了多视角一致性损失，鼓励视角不变表示。最后，我们构建了SatAgent-SR130K，首个大规模无人机-卫星协同多视角空间推理数据集。实验表明，在多种任务上，SatAgent比最先进的通用基础模型和专用空间推理模型分别高出25.91%和11.69%，在复杂几何关系推理中达到特别高的准确率。

</details>


### [80] [Think While You Map: Asynchronous Vision-Language Agents for Incremental 3D Scene Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31471)
*Deniz Bickici,Michael Pabst,Shohei Mori,Dieter Schmalstieg*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出异步架构，将轻量级在线建图与重量级语义精化解耦，支持探索过程中实时查询且语义不断丰富的开放词汇3D场景图构建方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有开放词汇3D场景图方法通常采用"先重建、后用视觉语言模型丰富"的两阶段串行流程，导致探索期间场景图不可查询，限制了实时交互能力。

Method: 提出异步架构：基于概率体素的轻量级在线建图主干增量维持稳定物体身份；后台VLM智能体渐进丰富语义信息；通过语义闭环检测解决重复物体轨迹；为物体添加细粒度视觉属性并推导空间关系；设计多目标帧调度器，选择少量信息帧联合覆盖多目标以摊销VLM计算成本。

Result: 在ScanNet和Replica的语义分割任务上匹配或超越现有方法；在Sr3D+、Nr3D、ScanRefer三个视觉定位基准上，以A@0.25指标超越此前最优方法15.3至18.8个百分点。

Conclusion: 提出的异步架构有效解耦在线建图与语义精化，使场景图在探索过程中即可查询且语义随推理时间持续增长，实现了精度与效率的更好平衡。

Original Abstract: Open-vocabulary 3D scene graph methods typically operate in two stages: first reconstruct, then enrich with vision-language models, leaving the graph unqueryable during exploration. We argue that this sequential coupling is unnecessary and propose an asynchronous architecture in which lightweight online mapping runs concurrently with heavyweight semantic refinement. A probabilistic voxel-based backbone maintains stable object identities incrementally, while background VLM agents progressively enrich the graph. This framework resolves duplicate object tracks through semantic loop closure, attaches fine-grained visual attributes and derives spatial relations between objects. A multi-target frame scheduler amortizes VLM cost by selecting a small set of informative frames that jointly cover multiple targets. The resulting scene graph is queryable during exploration and grows in semantic richness over time. Our method matches or outperforms existing open-vocabulary 3D scene graph methods on semantic segmentation (ScanNet, Replica) and surpasses the prior state-of-the-art across three visual grounding benchmarks (Sr3D+, Nr3D, ScanRefer) by 15.3 to 18.8 A@0.25. Project page: https://denizbickici.github.io/thinkgraphs/

中文摘要翻译: 开放词汇3D场景图方法通常采用两阶段流程：先重建，再用视觉语言模型丰富，导致探索期间场景图不可查询。本文认为这种顺序耦合并非必要，并提出一种异步架构，其中轻量级在线建图与重量级语义精化并发运行。基于概率体素的骨干网络增量式维护稳定的物体身份，而后台VLM智能体渐进式地丰富场景图。该框架通过语义闭环检测解决重复物体轨迹，附加细粒度视觉属性，并推导物体间的空间关系。多目标帧调度器通过选择一组覆盖多个目标的少量信息帧来摊销VLM计算成本。由此生成的场景图在探索过程中可查询，且语义丰富度随时间增长。我们的方法在语义分割任务（ScanNet、Replica）上匹配或超越现有开放词汇3D场景图方法，并在三个视觉定位基准（Sr3D+、Nr3D、ScanRefer）上以A@0.25指标超越此前最优方法15.3至18.8个百分点。项目页面：https://denizbickici.github.io/thinkgraphs/

</details>


### [81] [DrivingDepth: Sparse-Prompted Pixel-wise Scale Correction for Driving Depth Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31488)
*Chi Huang,Wenhao Zhang,Hang Yin,YuAn Wang,Hao Li,Bosheng Wang,Xun Sun,Liang Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DrivingDepth 提出了一种利用稀疏激光雷达点作为几何提示、通过逐像素残差缩放因子校准冻结的深度基础模型，在不重新训练的情况下实现稠密度量深度估计，同时保持几何一致性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 自动驾驶中的稠密深度估计面临几何-尺度冲突：深度基础模型能提供像素对齐的密集视觉几何但缺乏可靠的度量尺度，而投影激光雷达虽提供度量锚点却稀疏、有噪声且与图像结构不对齐。现有方法从头生成深度会覆盖基础模型的连贯几何结构，在视觉连续表面产生伪影。

Method: DrivingDepth 将稀疏激光雷达视为几何提示，通过残差逐像素尺度校正模块局部校准冻结的深度基础模型先验，仅学习一个从相对几何映射到度量坐标的逐像素尺度因子，从而保留稠密视觉几何结构。

Result: 在 nuScenes 数据集上，使用 4 帧环视输入，DrivingDepth 的 AbsRel 达到 11.19，EdgeCR 达到 5.741，在度量精度和几何一致性上均优于 MapAnything (11.99/1.914)，达到了 SOTA 水平。

Conclusion: 深度基础模型已捕获几何连贯的相对深度，无需额外学习表面结构，仅需逐像素尺度因子即可将相对几何映射到度量坐标，该方法同时实现了最优的度量精度和几何一致性。

Original Abstract: Dense depth estimation for autonomous driving faces a geometry-scale conflict: depth foundation models deliver pixel-aligned dense visual geometry without reliable metric scale, while projected LiDAR provides metric anchors that are sparse, noisy, and misaligned with image structures. Existing sparse-prompted methods incorporate LiDAR by regenerating depth from scratch, overriding the foundation model's coherent geometry and producing structural artifacts on visually continuous surfaces. Our key insight is that foundation models already capture geometrically coherent relative depth; no additional surface structure learning is required-only a per-pixel scale factor mapping relative geometry to metric coordinates. Based on this, we propose DrivingDepth, which treats sparse LiDAR as geometric prompts that locally calibrate a frozen foundation prior through residual pixel-wise scale correction, preserving dense visual geometry by construction. On nuScenes with 4-frame surround-view input, DrivingDepth achieves an AbsRel of 11.19 and an EdgeCR of 5.741, outperforming MapAnything (11.99/1.914) by simultaneously delivering SOTA metric accuracy and geometric consistency.

中文摘要翻译: 自动驾驶中的稠密深度估计面临着几何-尺度冲突：深度基础模型能够提供像素对齐的稠密视觉几何，但缺乏可靠的度量尺度；而投影激光雷达虽然提供了度量锚点，却存在稀疏、有噪声且与图像结构不对齐的问题。现有的稀疏提示方法通过从头生成深度来融入激光雷达信息，这覆盖了基础模型的连贯几何结构，并在视觉连续的表面上产生结构伪影。我们的关键洞察在于：基础模型已经捕获了几何连贯的相对深度，无需额外学习表面结构，仅需一个逐像素尺度因子将相对几何映射到度量坐标。基于此，我们提出了 DrivingDepth，该方法将稀疏激光雷达视为几何提示，通过残差逐像素尺度校正局部校准冻结的基础先验，从而在构造上保留稠密视觉几何。在 nuScenes 数据集上，使用 4 帧环视输入，DrivingDepth 的 AbsRel 达到 11.19，EdgeCR 达到 5.741，在同时实现最先进的度量精度和几何一致性方面均优于 MapAnything (11.99/1.914)。

</details>


### [82] [HVPNet: A Bio-Inspired Network for General Salient and Camouflaged Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31496)
*Jiawei Xu,Qiangqiang Zhou,Zhouping Li,Yanjiao Shi,Yugen Yi,Jiacong Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种受人类视觉过程启发的简洁通用生物计算架构HVPNet，通过视网膜整合模块（RIM）和皮层解码器（CD）实现多模态显著与伪装目标检测，在22个数据集上达到了优秀的精度-效率平衡。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有多模态显著/伪装目标检测方法依赖复杂的跨模态特征融合与解码结构，导致模型参数过大且存在结构冗余；而人类视觉过程无需如此复杂结构即可高效识别目标，启发作者探索更简化的建模策略。

Method: 提出HVPNet，包含两个核心模块：(1) 视网膜整合模块（RIM），借鉴视网膜多层信息整合机制，通过层级特定多阶段融合策略有效整合多模态特征；(2) 皮层解码器（CD），将解码过程分解为低层和高层视觉阶段，模仿人类视觉皮层的层次化处理。

Result: HVPNet可扩展至4种模态的7个任务，在22个数据集上无需复杂技巧即可建立优秀的精度-效率平衡。

Conclusion: 从人类视觉过程汲取概念灵感，可以实现简单而通用的建模策略，在保持高精度的同时提高效率，验证了生物启发设计的有效性。

Original Abstract: In recent years, most research on multimodal salient object detection (SOD) and camouflaged object detection (COD) typically aims to improve performance through complex cross-modal feature fusion and decoding structures. However, this approach leads to an excessively large model parameter scale and often fails to deliver satisfactory detection performance due to structural redundancy. In contrast, the human visual process is able to efficiently perform salient and camouflaged object identification without such complex structures. This contrast raises an important question: Can we draw conceptual inspiration from the human visual process to achieve a simpler modeling strategy, and still realize accurate and efficient object detection? To answer this question, we propose HVPNet, a simple yet general bio-inspired computational architecture. Drawing on the multi-layered information integration of the retina as a conceptual metaphor, we designed a Retinal Integration Module (RIM), which effectively integrates multimodal features through a level-specific multi-stage integration strategy. To fully exploit these features, we further design a cortical decoder (CD) that breaks down the decoding process into low- and high-level visual stages, abstracting the hierarchical processing in the human visual cortex. Benefiting from these designs, HVPNet can readily extend to seven tasks across four modalities. Without bells and whistles, it establishes an excellent accuracy-efficiency trade-off across 22 datasets spanning these seven tasks. Our code is available at https://github.com/jiaweiXu1029/HVPNet.

中文摘要翻译: 近年来，大多数多模态显著目标检测（SOD）与伪装目标检测（COD）研究通常旨在通过复杂的跨模态特征融合与解码结构来提升性能。然而，这种方法导致模型参数量过大，且常因结构冗余而无法提供令人满意的检测性能。相比之下，人类视觉过程能够在无需如此复杂结构的情况下高效完成显著与伪装目标的识别。这一对比引出一个重要问题：我们能否从人类视觉过程中汲取概念灵感，实现更简洁的建模策略，同时仍能完成准确高效的目标检测？为回答这一问题，我们提出HVPNet——一种简洁而通用的生物启发计算架构。借鉴视网膜多层信息整合作为概念隐喻，我们设计了视网膜整合模块（RIM），通过层级特定的多阶段整合策略有效融合多模态特征。为进一步充分利用这些特征，我们设计了皮层解码器（CD），将解码过程分解为低层和高层视觉阶段，抽象模拟了人类视觉皮层中的层次化处理。得益于这些设计，HVPNet可轻松扩展到四种模态的七项任务。无需额外复杂技巧，HVPNet在这七项任务覆盖的22个数据集上建立了优异的精度-效率平衡。我们的代码已开源至https://github.com/jiaweiXu1029/HVPNet。

</details>


### [83] [Fully Automated High-Precision Segmentation of Retinal Atrophy and Ellipsoid Zone Thickness in OCT: A Reliable Tool for Real-World GA Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31502)
*Wolf-Dieter Vogl,Hlynur Skulason,Oliver Leingang,Ursula Schmidt-Erfurth,Amir Sadeghipour,Ariadne Whitby*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于深度学习的全自动框架，用于在OCT图像中对GA（地图样萎缩）相关生物标志物（RPE丢失、EZ丢失、EZ变薄）进行像素级分割，在多样本数据集上取得了高精度和高可靠性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 年龄相关性黄斑变性（AMD）继发的地图样萎缩（GA）需要精确监测相关结构性生物标志物，以评估疾病阶段、进展和治疗反应，但现有方法缺乏全自动、高精度的像素级分割能力。

Method: 提出全自动深度学习框架，使用三个专门的语义分割模型分别分割RPE丢失、EZ边界（含中断）和Bruch膜；基于298个SD-OCT体积（涵盖GA、iAMD、nAMD和健康）训练，并在独立外部数据集（n=43）上验证。

Result: RPE丢失Dice=0.88，EZ丢失Dice=0.87，Pearson's r>0.99；EZ厚度测量亚像素平均偏差2.15μm；重复性ICC>0.98。

Conclusion: 该全自动框架能够准确且一致地量化外层光感受器变性和RPE丢失，为临床试验和常规眼科护理中的GA评估提供了高度可靠的工具。

Original Abstract: Geographic atrophy (GA) secondary to age-related macular degeneration (AMD) requires precise monitoring of relevant structural biomarkers to assess disease stage, progression, and treatment response. This paper presents a fully automated, deep learning-based framework for the high-precision, pixel-wise segmentation of key biomarkers in optical coherence tomography (OCT) imaging: retinal pigment epithelium (RPE) loss, ellipsoid zone (EZ) loss, and EZ thinning. The proposed pipeline uses three specialized semantic segmentation models to delineate RPE loss, EZ boundaries (including interruptions), and Bruch's membrane. To ensure robustness and generalizability, the models were developed on a diverse dataset of 298 SD-OCT volumes representing the full phenotypic spectrum of AMD (GA:222, intermediate AMD: 40, neovascular AMD: 17, healthy: 19) and validated on an independent external dataset (n=43). The comprehensive evaluation was further strengthened using additional datasets to assess repeatability, inter-reader reliability, the impact of B-scan density on measurement accuracy, and subgroup performance stratified by lesion size. Results demonstrated high segmentation accuracy (Dice RPE loss: 0.88, Dice EZ loss: 0.87, Pearson's r > 0.99). Total EZ thickness measurements exhibited a sub-pixel average deviation of 2.15 $μm$, and segmentation reliability was confirmed by a strong reproducibility score (ICC > 0.98). By accurately and consistently quantifying outer photoreceptor degeneration and RPE loss, this fully automated framework provides a highly reliable tool for GA assessment in both clinical trials and routine real-world ophthalmic care.

中文摘要翻译: 继发于年龄相关性黄斑变性（AMD）的地图样萎缩（GA）需要精确监测相关结构性生物标志物，以评估疾病阶段、进展和治疗反应。本文提出了一种全自动、基于深度学习的框架，用于在光学相干断层扫描（OCT）成像中对关键生物标志物进行高精度像素级分割：视网膜色素上皮（RPE）丢失、椭圆体带（EZ）丢失和EZ变薄。所提出的流程使用三个专门的语义分割模型来描绘RPE丢失、EZ边界（包括中断）和Bruch膜。为确保鲁棒性和泛化能力，模型在代表AMD完整表型谱的298个SD-OCT体积（GA:222，中期AMD:40，新生血管性AMD:17，健康:19）的多样化数据集上开发，并在独立外部数据集（n=43）上验证。综合评估通过额外数据集进一步加强，评估了重复性、读者间可靠性、B扫描密度对测量精度的影响以及按病灶大小分层的亚组性能。结果表明分割精度高（Dice RPE丢失:0.88，Dice EZ丢失:0.87，Pearson's r>0.99）。总EZ厚度测量显示出2.15μm的亚像素平均偏差，分割可靠性通过强重复性评分（ICC>0.98）得到确认。通过准确且一致地量化外层光感受器变性和RPE丢失，该全自动框架为临床试验和常规真实世界眼科护理中的GA评估提供了高度可靠的工具。

</details>


### [84] [SimpleSearch-VL: A Simple Recipe for Multimodal Agentic Deep Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31504)
*Ming Dai,Zhihong Lu,Jinjie Gu,Jiedong Zhuang,Yefeng Liu,Wankou Yang,Jian Wang,Chunhua Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SimpleSearch-VL提出了一种高效、可靠且实用的多模态智能搜索框架，通过改进智能体自身的搜索与验证流程（而非扩展数据、工具或辅助模型组件），仅用5K监督轨迹和2K强化学习数据，即在Qwen3-VL基础上提升15.8-16.0个平均点，30B-A3B变体性能媲美Gemini-3-Pro。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有智能搜索方法通常依赖大规模数据、复杂工具或额外模型组件，导致效率低、可靠性差且部署成本高。作者希望在不扩展这些外部资源的前提下，通过改进智能体自身的搜索与验证机制来实现高效、可靠且实用的多模态智能搜索。

Method: 提出三种核心改进：(1) 分解自适应滚动（FAR），通过构建更具信息量的训练组提高采样效率，同时利用冗余样本缓解长尾延迟并暴露难样本；(2) 证据验证推理，使用思维链验证显式评估检索到的视觉和文本线索与原始上下文的相关性；(3) 轻量级工具接口与网页自摘要，无需额外外部依赖。

Result: 仅用5K监督工具交错轨迹和2K强化学习数据，SimpleSearch-VL在Qwen3-VL 8B和30B-A3B变体上分别提升15.8和16.0个平均点。30B-A3B版本性能与Gemini-3-Pro相当。

Conclusion: 通过优化智能体自身的搜索与验证流程，而非扩展外部资源，可以实现高效、可靠且实用的多模态智能搜索，在小规模训练数据下取得显著性能提升，并达到与大规模商业模型竞争的水平。

Original Abstract: We present SimpleSearch-VL, an efficient, reliable, and practical framework for multimodal agentic search. Its core idea is to improve the agent's own search-and-verification process rather than scaling data, tools, or auxiliary model components. For efficiency, Factorized Adaptive Rollout (FAR) improves sampling efficiency by forming more informative training groups while using redundant samples to mitigate long-tail latency and expose hard samples. For reliability, SimpleSearch-VL performs evidence-verified reasoning, explicitly using chain-of-thought verification to assess the relevance of retrieved visual and textual cues to the original context. For practicality, SimpleSearch-VL keeps a lightweight tool interface and performs webpage self-summary within the agent, requiring no additional external dependencies. With only 5K supervised tool-interleaved trajectories and 2K RL data, SimpleSearch-VL improves Qwen3-VL agentic baselines by 15.8 and 16.0 average points for the 8B and 30B-A3B variants, respectively. The SimpleSearch-VL-30B-A3B model further achieves performance competitive with agentic Gemini-3-Pro.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出了SimpleSearch-VL，一个高效、可靠且实用的多模态智能搜索框架。其核心思想是改进智能体自身的搜索与验证流程，而非扩展数据、工具或辅助模型组件。在效率方面，分解自适应滚动（FAR）通过构建更具信息量的训练组来提高采样效率，同时利用冗余样本缓解长尾延迟并暴露难样本。在可靠性方面，SimpleSearch-VL执行证据验证推理，显式使用思维链验证来评估检索到的视觉和文本线索与原始上下文的相关性。在实用性方面，SimpleSearch-VL保持轻量级工具接口，并在智能体内部执行网页自摘要，无需额外外部依赖。仅使用5K监督工具交错轨迹和2K强化学习数据，SimpleSearch-VL在Qwen3-VL智能体基线上分别将8B和30B-A3B变体的平均得分提升了15.8和16.0个点。SimpleSearch-VL-30B-A3B模型进一步实现了与智能体Gemini-3-Pro相竞争的性能。

</details>


### [85] [PRISM: Latent Composition Consistency for Single-Image Reflection Removal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31513)
*Junseong Shin,Tae Hyun Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PRISM利用预训练VAE潜空间的低层间相干性，将单图像反射去除重新表述为潜空间中的线性分离问题，结合流匹配速度场、潜成分一致性策略和层对比分离损失，在六个基准上显著超越现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有SIRR方法在像素空间操作，非线性sRGB形成模型纠缠了透射层和反射层且泛化能力有限；作者观察到预训练VAE潜空间中图像层间的相干性显著低于像素空间，提供了更有利的分解工作空间。

Method: 1. 将SIRR重新解释为潜空间中的线性分离问题，在预训练FLUX骨干上学习流匹配速度场，单次前向传递恢复两层；2. 提出潜成分一致性(LCC)策略，通过跨样本交换反射潜变量构建合成混合物并施加循环损失；3. 提出层对比分离(LCS)损失，通过块级对比学习促进层间语义分离，无需显式反射真值。

Result: 在六个基准数据集上，PRISM以显著优势持续超越最先进方法，且对野外图像具有强泛化能力。

Conclusion: 利用预训练VAE潜空间的有利特性，结合流匹配、一致性约束和对比学习，可以有效解决单图像反射去除这一病态问题，实现更优的分解质量和泛化能力。

Original Abstract: Single-image reflection removal (SIRR) seeks to recover the transmission layer from a mixture corrupted by reflections -- a severely ill-posed problem. Existing methods operate in pixel space, where the nonlinear sRGB formation model entangles the two layers and limits generalization. We observe that pretrained VAE latent spaces exhibit substantially lower coherence between image layers compared to pixel space, providing a more favorable working space for decomposition. Building on this finding, we propose \textbf{PRISM} (Pretrained-latent Reflection Image Separation Model), which reinterprets SIRR as a latent linear separation problem. Under an approximate additive formulation in latent space, PRISM learns a flow matching velocity field on a pretrained FLUX backbone that recovers both transmission and reflection in a single forward pass. To enforce robust disentanglement, we introduce a Latent Composition Consistency (LCC) strategy that constructs synthetic mixtures by swapping reflection latents across samples and enforces consistent decomposition via a cycle loss. We further propose a Layer Contrastive Separation (LCS) loss that promotes semantic separation between layers through patch-level contrastive learning, without requiring explicit reflection targets. Experiments on six benchmarks demonstrate that PRISM consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods by significant margins, with strong generalization to in-the-wild images.

中文摘要翻译: 单图像反射去除旨在从被反射污染的混合图像中恢复透射层——这是一个严重病态的问题。现有方法在像素空间操作，其中非线性sRGB形成模型纠缠了两个层并限制了泛化能力。我们观察到，与像素空间相比，预训练VAE潜空间中的图像层间相干性显著更低，为分解提供了更有利的工作空间。基于这一发现，我们提出PRISM（预训练潜空间反射图像分离模型），将单图像反射去除重新解释为潜空间中的线性分离问题。在潜空间中的近似加性公式下，PRISM在预训练FLUX骨干上学习一个流匹配速度场，在单次前向传递中恢复透射层和反射层。为了实现鲁棒的解纠缠，我们引入潜成分一致性策略，通过跨样本交换反射潜变量构建合成混合物，并通过循环损失确保一致的分解。我们进一步提出层对比分离损失，通过块级对比学习促进层间的语义分离，无需显式的反射真值。在六个基准上的实验表明，PRISM以显著优势持续超越最先进方法，并对野外图像具有强泛化能力。

</details>


### [86] [MV-GEL: Language-Driven Multi-View Geometric Entity Localization on Meshes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31533)
*Kartik Bali,Roland Aydin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MV-GEL框架，通过多视角选择与VLM推理分割，实现从自然语言查询中定位3D网格上的精细几何实体（面、边），相比基线方法在面IoU和边F1上分别提升1.7倍和4.5倍以上。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有VLM在图像域的指代分割已取得进展，但将其扩展到结构化3D几何实体定位面临重大挑战：3D物体外观高度依赖视角，单一视角可能因遮挡或透视缩短导致目标实体不可见。因此需要一种能够选择最优视角并实现语言驱动的3D几何实体定位方法。

Method: 提出MV-GEL框架，核心包含三个模块：(1) GELviews——提示条件排序模块，基于语言提示的几何CAD实体可观测性对视角进行优先级排序；(2) VLM推理分割骨干——处理选定视角的分割任务；(3) 几何感知射线投射——将预测掩码提升到对应网格上。框架完全与CAD无关，仅依赖3D网格。

Result: 面级别IoU提升1.7倍，边级别F1提升4.5倍以上，显著优于CLIP基线和随机视角采样方法，尤其对薄且视角敏感的结构效果突出。

Conclusion: 通过多视角选择策略（GELviews）与VLM推理分割的结合，MV-GEL能够可靠地定位CAD实体（面、边），为语言驱动的3D几何理解提供了有效框架。

Original Abstract: Identifying and grounding precise geometric entities, such as edges, planar regions, and curved surfaces within 3D objects, is foundational to computer-aided design (CAD), robotic manipulation, and scientific simulation. Although modern Vision Language Models (VLMs) have advanced referring segmentation (RIS) in the image domain, extending such language-driven localization to structured 3D geometry is substantially harder. The 3D object appearance is highly sensitive to viewpoints; a single perspective may render a target entity clearly observable, while another may suffer from severe occlusion or foreshortening. In this work, we attempt to solve these challenges with MV-GEL (Multi-View Geometric Entity Localization), a framework for localizing fine-grained geometric entities on polygon meshes from natural language queries. Our key insight is that reliable CAD entity (i.e., faces, edges or solids) localization depends on selecting views that make the queried entity maximally interpretable. We introduce GELviews, a prompt-conditioned ranking module that prioritizes viewpoints based on language prompted observability of geometric CAD entities. Selected views are processed by a VLM-based reasoning segmentation backbone, and predicted masks are lifted to the corresponding meshes via geometry-aware ray casting. Our framework is completely CAD agnostic and relies only on 3D meshes. Experiments show up to a 1.7X improvement in face-level IoU and over 4.5X gains in edge-level F1 compared to vanilla baselines, substantially outperforming CLIP-based and random view sampling, particularly for thin and view-sensitive structures.The dataset, code and trained checkpoints are available at https://github.com/kbali1297/MV-GEL.

中文摘要翻译: 识别和定位3D物体中的精确几何实体（如边缘、平面区域和曲面）是计算机辅助设计（CAD）、机器人操作和科学模拟的基础。尽管现代视觉语言模型（VLM）在图像域中推进了指代分割（RIS）的发展，但将这种语言驱动的定位扩展到结构化3D几何体要困难得多。3D物体的外观对视角高度敏感；单个视角可能使目标实体清晰可见，而另一个视角则可能遭受严重遮挡或透视缩短。在本文中，我们尝试通过MV-GEL（多视角几何实体定位）来解决这些挑战，这是一个从自然语言查询中定位多边形网格上细粒度几何实体的框架。我们的关键见解是，可靠的CAD实体（即面、边或实体）定位取决于选择能使查询实体的可解释性最大化的视角。我们引入了GELviews，一个提示条件排序模块，该模块基于语言提示的几何CAD实体可观测性对视角进行优先级排序。选定视角由基于VLM的推理分割骨干处理，预测的掩码通过几何感知射线投射被提升到对应的网格上。我们的框架完全与CAD无关，仅依赖于3D网格。实验表明，与vanilla基线相比，面级别IoU提升达1.7倍，边级别F1提升超过4.5倍，尤其是对于薄且视角敏感的结构，性能显著优于基于CLIP和随机视角采样的方法。数据集、代码和训练好的检查点可在https://github.com/kbali1297/MV-GEL获取。

</details>


### [87] [DataEvolver: Self-Evolving Multi-Agent Data Construction for Text-Rich Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31537)
*Siyu Yan,Yizhen Gao,Yilin Wang,Dongxing Mao,Alex Jinpeng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DataEvolver，一个自我进化的多智能体框架，利用被拒绝样本的反馈信号迭代改进文本丰富图像数据构建，显著提升OCR-F1指标。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有文本丰富图像数据构建采用静态爬取-过滤-冻结范式，丢弃了大量包含有用失败信号（如OCR错误、语义不匹配）的被拒绝样本，导致后续构建重复相同失败模式。

Method: DataEvolver是一个多智能体框架，包含Retriever（收集候选样本）、Verifier（分配质量分数和拒绝原因）、Critic（总结回合级反馈为语义反馈）和Generator（针对未覆盖区域进行目标合成），通过反馈记忆驱动下一轮构建的迭代进化。

Result: 在0.75M规模下，DataEvolver在PixArt-alpha上相比最强基线在TextScenesHQ上提升OCR-F1达85.3%，在LongTextBench上提升35.3%，且在Show-o2上也表现出一致的迁移能力。

Conclusion: 被拒绝样本能提供可操作的反馈信号，用于改进文本丰富图像数据构建，DataEvolver的有效性不依赖于单一下游生成器。

Original Abstract: Text-rich image generation is one of the most challenging settings in image generation, since models must simultaneously produce visually realistic images and render legible, semantically aligned, and layout-consistent text. Existing data pipelines usually follow a static crawl-filter-freeze paradigm. They collect candidate samples, filter them once, and freeze the accepted data for training. However, rejected samples are usually discarded, although they often contain useful failure signals such as OCR errors and semantic mismatches. As a result, later construction rounds may repeat the same failure modes. To address these limitations, we propose DataEvolver, a self-evolving multi-agent framework for text-rich image data construction. DataEvolver treats data construction as feedback-driven construction policy evolution. A Retriever collects candidate samples, a Verifier assigns quality scores and rejection causes, a Critic summarizes round-level feedback into semantic feedback, and a Generator completes under-covered regions through targeted synthesis. The updated feedback memory then guides the next construction round. Experiments on text-rich image generation benchmarks show that DataEvolver produces more useful training data than fixed-dataset baselines under matched data budgets. At the 0.75M scale on PixArt-alpha, DataEvolver improves OCR-F1 over the strongest baseline by 85.3 percent on TextScenesHQ and 35.3 percent on LongTextBench. The improvements are consistent across both evaluated benchmarks and also transfer to Show-o2, indicating that the benefit of DataEvolver is not tied to a single downstream generator. These results suggest that rejected samples can provide actionable feedback for improving text-rich image data construction.

中文摘要翻译: 文本丰富图像生成是图像生成中最具挑战性的场景之一，因为模型必须同时生成视觉逼真的图像，并呈现可读、语义对齐且布局一致的文本。现有的数据流水线通常遵循静态爬取-过滤-冻结范式：收集候选样本，对其进行一次过滤，然后将接受的数据冻结用于训练。然而，被拒绝的样本虽然通常包含有用的失败信号（如OCR错误和语义不匹配），却往往被丢弃。这导致后续的构建轮次可能重复相同的失败模式。为解决这些局限，我们提出DataEvolver，一个用于文本丰富图像数据构建的自我进化多智能体框架。DataEvolver将数据构建视为反馈驱动的构建策略进化。检索器收集候选样本，验证器分配质量评分和拒绝原因，评论家将回合级反馈总结为语义反馈，生成器通过目标合成完成覆盖不足的区域。更新后的反馈记忆随后引导下一轮构建。在文本丰富图像生成基准上的实验表明，在匹配的数据预算下，DataEvolver比固定数据集基线生成了更有用的训练数据。在PixArt-alpha的0.75M规模上，DataEvolver在TextScenesHQ上将OCR-F1相较于最强基线提高了85.3%，在LongTextBench上提高了35.3%。这些改进在两个评估基准上均表现一致，并且也迁移到了Show-o2上，表明DataEvolver的优势不依赖于单一的下游生成器。这些结果表明，被拒绝的样本可以为改进文本丰富图像数据构建提供可操作的反馈。

</details>


### [88] [AugSplat: Radiance Field-Informed Gaussian Splatting for Sparse-View Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31556)
*Lorenzo Lazzaroni,Riccardo Bollati,Daniel Barath,Michael Niemeyer,Keisuke Tateno*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AugSplat通过辐射场进行视图增强，改善了稀疏视图下高斯泼溅的重建质量，同时保持实时渲染速度。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 高斯泼溅方法在稀疏视图场景下对初始几何质量高度敏感，有限的观测常导致点云重建不完整或带噪声，影响渲染质量。

Method: 先基于稀疏输入视图训练辐射场，合成邻近新视角的额外图像以增加视图覆盖；然后将这些合成视图作为辅助监督用于高斯泼溅优化。提出两种变体：Staged AugSplat（先用合成视图优化，再切换真实图像）和Dual AugSplat（联合训练真实与合成视图，逐步衰减合成损失权重）。

Result: 在稀疏视图mip-NeRF 360场景上，AugSplat优于标准高斯泼溅。Staged AugSplat取得最强平均性能，Dual AugSplat性能接近且全程保持真实图像监督，两者均保留推理时的实时渲染能力。

Conclusion: 利用辐射场进行视图增强可有效缓解高斯泼溅在稀疏视图下的几何敏感性，提升重建质量而不牺牲实时渲染效率。

Original Abstract: Generating high-quality novel views at real-time frame rates remains a central challenge in 3D vision, particularly in sparse-view scenarios. Neural radiance fields have demonstrated robust reconstruction from limited observations, but their reliance on volumetric rendering leads to high computational cost and slow inference. In contrast, Gaussian Splatting methods achieve real-time rendering through rasterization, but their optimization is highly sensitive to the quality of the initial geometry. This sensitivity becomes especially problematic in sparse-view settings, where limited observations often lead to incomplete or noisy point-cloud reconstructions.
