Original Abstract: Autoregressive (AR) modeling has recently achieved remarkable progress in native 3D mesh generation, largely due to its natural ability to handle variable-length, discrete data structures. However, the inherent constraints of the AR paradigm severely restrict the generated meshes, leading to limited face counts, bounded vertex resolutions, and difficulties in supporting textures. To overcome these bottlenecks, we propose the Barycentric Dominance Field (BDF), a continuous representation defined on triangular mesh surfaces that elegantly encodes vertex topological connectivity. BDF bridges the fundamental gap between discrete mesh topology and continuous diffusion-based generative modeling by transforming connectivity into a continuous surface signal. As an intrinsic mesh property, BDF shares strong similarities with texture maps, enabling its seamless integration into existing 3D diffusion pipelines without requiring architectural modifications. Extensive experiments demonstrate that BDF empowers diffusion models to generate native meshes with significantly higher quality, greater scalability, and stronger robustness compared to state-of-the-art autoregressive methods.

中文摘要翻译: 自回归(AR)建模最近在原生3D网格生成方面取得了显著进展，这主要归功于其处理可变长度离散数据结构的自然能力。然而，AR范式的固有限制严重制约了生成网格的质量，导致面数有限、顶点分辨率受限且难以支持纹理。为克服这些瓶颈，我们提出了重心支配场(BDF)，一种定义在三角网格表面的连续表示，能够优雅地编码顶点拓扑连接性。BDF通过将连接性转换为连续的表面信号，弥合了离散网格拓扑与基于连续扩散的生成模型之间的根本性差距。作为网格的固有属性，BDF与纹理图具有高度相似性，使其能够无缝集成到现有3D扩散流程中，无需修改模型架构。大量实验表明，与最先进的自回归方法相比，BDF使扩散模型能够生成质量更高、可扩展性更强、鲁棒性更好的原生网格。

</details>


### [119] [SpikeLogBERT: Energy-Efficient Log Parsing Using Spiking Transformer Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31781)
*Thuan Bui,Duong Do,Tung Vu,Duc-Tho Mai,Cong-Kha Pham*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 SpikeLogBERT，一种基于脉冲神经网络（SNN）的日志解析框架，通过脉冲Transformer架构与BERT知识蒸馏，在HDFS数据集上达到0.99997的解析精度，同时理论能耗降低最多62.6%。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有神经日志解析方法依赖密集矩阵乘法，导致高计算成本和能耗。本文旨在通过脉冲神经网络实现节能的日志解析，在保持语义表示能力的同时降低能耗。

Method: 提出SpikeLogBERT，核心包括：(1) 脉冲Transformer架构，利用稀疏脉冲激活和事件驱动处理；(2) 从BERT教师模型进行知识蒸馏，使学生脉冲模型保留语义表示能力；(3) 推理时通过脉冲驱动计算减少活跃操作数量。

Result: 在HDFS数据集上，SpikeLogBERT解析精度达到0.99997，优于基于ANN的神经日志解析模型；在标准45nm CMOS假设下，理论能耗降低最高达62.6%。

Conclusion: SpikeLogBERT作为首个将脉冲神经网络应用于日志解析的基准研究，证明SNN可在显著降低能耗的同时达到甚至超越ANN模型的解析性能，为节能日志解析提供了可行方向。

Original Abstract: Log parsing is a fundamental step in automated log analysis, transforming raw system logs into structured event templates for downstream tasks such as anomaly detection and system monitoring. Existing log parsing methods range from rule-based and clustering-based approaches to neural models that learn semantic representations from log messages. However, neural approaches typically rely on dense matrix multiplications, which can result in high computational cost and energy consumption. This paper presents SpikeLogBERT, a spiking neural network framework for energy-efficient log parsing. The proposed model integrates a spiking transformer architecture with knowledge distillation from a BERT teacher model, enabling spike-driven computation while preserving semantic representation capability. By leveraging sparse spike activations and event-driven processing, the number of active operations during inference can be significantly reduced. As an initial benchmark study, experiments on the HDFS dataset demonstrate that SpikeLogBERT outperforms ANN-based neural log parsing models with a parsing accuracy of 0.99997, while reducing estimated theoretical energy consumption by up to 62.6% under standard 45nm CMOS assumptions.

中文摘要翻译: 日志解析是自动化日志分析中的基础步骤，它将原始系统日志转换为结构化的事件模板，以支持异常检测和系统监控等下游任务。现有的日志解析方法包括基于规则的方法、基于聚类的方法，以及从日志消息中学习语义表示的神经模型。然而，神经方法通常依赖密集矩阵乘法，这可能导致高计算成本和能耗。本文提出了 SpikeLogBERT，一种用于节能日志解析的脉冲神经网络框架。该模型将脉冲Transformer架构与来自BERT教师模型的知识蒸馏相结合，在保持语义表示能力的同时实现了脉冲驱动计算。通过利用稀疏脉冲激活和事件驱动处理，可以显著减少推理过程中的活跃操作数量。作为一项初步的基准研究，在HDFS数据集上的实验表明，SpikeLogBERT 的性能优于基于ANN的神经日志解析模型，解析精度达到0.99997，同时在标准45nm CMOS假设下，理论能耗降低了最高达62.6%。

</details>


### [120] [Self-Supervised Temporal Regularization for Landmark-Based Cardiac Segmentation with Automatic AHA Regional Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31785)
*David Montalvo-García,Nicolás Gaggion,María J. Ledesma-Carbayo,Enzo Ferrante*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种自监督时间正则化方法作为后训练优化阶段，利用图像序列的时间一致性约束心脏分割和运动估计，在不需逐帧标注的情况下实现时间连续的分割结果，并自动将地标映射到AHA 17节段临床标准，提升临床实用性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 基于图的心脏分割方法在独立帧上训练会产生时间不连续性，影响临床测量的可靠性，尤其在心脏超声中。现有方法缺乏对时序一致性的显式建模。

Method: 在已训练好的图分割模型基础上，引入自监督时间正则化作为后训练精炼阶段，通过对相邻帧间的速度和加速度不连续性施加惩罚，利用图像序列的时间相干性强制实现一致的心脏分割和运动估计，无需逐帧标注。

Result: 在CAMUS数据集上的验证表明，结合时间一致性与自动区域映射具有临床实用性，能够实现标准化的区域评估和病理性心肌运动模式的检测。

Conclusion: 自监督时间正则化能有效提升心脏超声分割的时序一致性，同时保持已学习的解剖对应关系，并支持自动映射到临床标准，具有良好的临床推广价值。

Original Abstract: Graph-based cardiac segmentation with implicit anatomical correspondences provides topological guarantees and population-level analysis capabilities, but models trained on independent frames of image sequences exhibit temporal discontinuities that affect reliable clinical measurements, particularly in cardiac ultrasound. In this work, we introduce self-supervised temporal regularization as a post-training refinement stage that exploits the temporal coherence in image sequences to enforce consistent cardiac segmentation and motion estimation over time, without requiring per-frame annotations. By penalizing velocity and acceleration discontinuities across consecutive frames, our method achieves temporally consistent segmentations while maintaining the learned anatomical correspondences. We further leverage these correspondences to automatically map landmarks to the AHA 17-segment clinical standard, enabling standardized regional assessment and detection of pathological myocardial motion patterns. Validation on CAMUS dataset demonstrates the clinical utility of combining temporal consistency with automatic regional mapping. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/david-montalvoo/MaskHybridGNet-TempReg

中文摘要翻译: 基于图的心脏分割方法通过隐式解剖对应提供了拓扑保证和群体级分析能力，但在独立图像序列帧上训练的模型会表现出时间不连续性，影响可靠的临床测量，尤其是在心脏超声中。在本工作中，我们引入自监督时间正则化作为后训练精炼阶段，利用图像序列中的时间相干性来强制执行随时间一致的心脏分割和运动估计，无需逐帧标注。通过对相邻帧间的速度和加速度不连续性进行惩罚，我们的方法在保持已学习的解剖对应的同时实现了时间一致的分割。我们进一步利用这些对应关系将地标自动映射到AHA 17节段临床标准，从而实现标准化的区域评估和病理性心肌运动模式的检测。在CAMUS数据集上的验证证明了将时间一致性与自动区域映射相结合的临床实用性。代码开源在https://github.com/david-montalvoo/MaskHybridGNet-TempReg

</details>


### [121] [MuSViT: A Foundation Vision Model for Sheet Music Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31811)
*Carlos Penarrubia,Antonio Rios-Vila,Eliseo Fuentes-Martinez,Juan C. Martinez-Sevilla,Francisco J. Castellanos,María Alfaro-Contreras,Jorge Calvo-Zaragoza*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MuSViT是首个面向乐谱的视觉基础模型，采用ViT编码器结合掩码自编码器在970万页IMSLP乐谱上预训练，通过两阶段课程学习策略，在下游任务中显著优于通用视觉编码器，并能直接在表示空间中编码符号化音乐结构。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 通用视觉基础模型在自然图像上表现优异，但乐谱作为音乐语言的视觉编码，具有独特的结构化符号属性，通用视觉表征缺乏针对这一领域专用主干网络。

Method: 采用ViT编码器结合掩码自编码器（MAE）预训练，设计两阶段课程学习策略：先在合成排版乐谱上进行预热，再在包含970万页的真实IMSLP语料库上进行大规模训练。

Result: 在线性探测（冻结编码器）设置下，MuSViT在所有下游任务上持续优于通用视觉编码器；在微调设置下，普遍超越任务特定的最优方法。嵌入-转录一致性分析表明，MuSViT能直接在表示空间中编码符号化音乐结构，而其他编码器的嵌入与乐谱内容无关。

Conclusion: MuSViT建立了乐谱理解领域的基础视觉主干网络，证明了领域专用预训练对于结构化符号视觉任务的重要性。

Original Abstract: Foundation models have transformed vision and language processing by providing rich, reusable representations that transfer across diverse tasks. Sheet music, as a visual encoding of musical language, lacks such a strong domain-specific backbone. We introduce MuSViT (Music Score Vision Transformer): the first foundation vision model for sheet music representation -- a ViT encoder pre-trained via Masked Autoencoders on 9.7 million pages from the IMSLP. To handle the complexity of real-world scores, we adopt a two-stage curriculum: a synthetic warm-up on typeset scores followed by large-scale training on the full IMSLP corpus. We evaluate MuSViT on four downstream tasks -- full-page and staff-level music score recognition, music symbol detection, and score difficulty classification -- under two scenarios: linear probing (frozen encoder) and fine-tuning. Under linear probing, MuSViT consistently outperforms modern vision encoders, revealing that general-purpose representations, regardless of scale, fall systematically short on the structured symbolic properties of musical notation. Under fine-tuning, MuSViT generally improves upon task-specific state-of-the-art methods. An additional embedding-transcription consistency analysis reveals that MuSViT encodes symbolic musical structure directly in its representation space -- unlike other encoders, whose embeddings do not correlate with music notation content. These results establish MuSViT as a foundation backbone for sheet music understanding.

中文摘要翻译: 基础模型通过提供丰富、可复用的跨任务表征，已经彻底改变了视觉和语言处理领域。乐谱作为音乐语言的视觉编码，却缺乏这样一个强大的领域专用主干网络。本文提出MuSViT（Music Score Vision Transformer）——首个用于乐谱表示的基础视觉模型，该模型采用ViT编码器，通过掩码自编码器在IMSLP的970万页乐谱上进行预训练。为了处理真实世界乐谱的复杂性，我们采用了两阶段课程学习策略：先在合成排版乐谱上进行预热训练，随后在完整的IMSLP语料库上进行大规模训练。我们在四个下游任务——全页和谱线层面的乐谱识别、音乐符号检测以及乐谱难度分类上，分别在两种场景下评估MuSViT：线性探测（冻结编码器）和微调。在线性探测设置下，MuSViT持续优于现代视觉编码器，表明无论规模大小，通用表征在乐谱符号化结构属性上系统性地存在不足。在微调设置下，MuSViT普遍超越了任务特定的最优方法。额外的嵌入-转录一致性分析揭示，MuSViT能够在其表示空间中直接编码符号化音乐结构——而其他编码器的嵌入与乐谱符号内容不存在相关性。这些结果确立了MuSViT作为乐谱理解领域基础主干网络的地位。

</details>


### [122] [Generative Lane Topology Reasoning via Autoregressive Model with Geometry Prior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31814)
*Jiahui Fu,Zehao Huang,Han Li,Naiyan Wang,Si Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TopoGPT是一个基于自回归序列建模的生成式框架，通过学习车道图结构的几何先验，解决现有车道拓扑推理方法中端点几何不一致和因遮挡导致的图不完整问题，在OpenLane-V2基准上实现显著性能提升。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有车道拓扑推理方法遵循检测与关联范式，独立处理每条车道实例，导致连接端点处几何不一致，且视觉遮挡造成图结构不完整。为克服这些问题，提出通过学习典型车道图结构的几何先验来生成几何一致且结构完整的车道图。

Method: 首先构建包含330万场景的大规模地图数据集；使用车道分词器将车道图序列化为离散token，场景上下文编码器将车道图转换为栅格化图像并提取全局场景token；通过场景条件化的下一个token预测预训练自回归车道序列Transformer，赋予模型车道图结构的几何先验；在此基础上，感知适配器将多视图图像的BEV特征与预训练场景条件对齐，将学到的几何先验迁移到基于传感器的车道图预测中。

Result: 在OpenLane-V2基准上，TopoGPT在车道级指标上平均提升+6.4，在点级指标上平均提升+11.6，生成的车道图具有几何一致性和结构完整性。

Conclusion: TopoGPT通过自回归序列建模学习车道图结构的几何先验，有效克服了检测与关联范式的局限性，生成几何一致且结构完整的车道图，在车道拓扑推理任务上达到最优性能。

Original Abstract: Lane topology reasoning aims to construct a lane graph from onboard sensor observations. Existing methods follow a detection and association paradigm that treats each lane instance independently, leading to geometric inconsistency at connected endpoints and incomplete graphs due to visual occlusions. To address these issues, we propose TopoGPT, a generative framework that learns the geometry prior from typical lane graph structures through autoregressive sequence modeling. Specifically, we construct a large-scale map dataset comprising 3.3M scenes. For each lane graph, a lane tokenizer serializes it into discrete tokens, while a scene context encoder converts it into a rasterized image and extracts global features as scene tokens. We pre-train an autoregressive lane sequence transformer via scene-conditioned next-token prediction, endowing the model with the geometry prior over lane graph structures. Building upon this prior, a perception adapter aligns BEV features from multi-view images with the pre-trained scene condition, transferring the learned geometry prior to sensor-based lane graph prediction. On the OpenLane-V2 benchmark, TopoGPT outperforms existing methods by an average of +6.4 on lane-level and +11.6 on point-level metrics, and produces geometrically consistent and structurally complete lane graphs.

中文摘要翻译: 车道拓扑推理旨在从车载传感器观测中构建车道图。现有方法遵循检测与关联范式，独立处理每条车道实例，导致连接端点处几何不一致，且因视觉遮挡造成图结构不完整。为解决这些问题，我们提出TopoGPT，一种通过自回归序列建模学习典型车道图结构几何先验的生成式框架。具体而言，我们构建了一个包含330万场景的大规模地图数据集。对于每个车道图，车道分词器将其序列化为离散token，而场景上下文编码器将其转换为栅格化图像并提取全局特征作为场景token。我们通过场景条件化的下一个token预测预训练一个自回归车道序列Transformer，赋予模型对车道图结构的几何先验。在此先验基础上，感知适配器将多视图图像的BEV特征与预训练场景条件对齐，将学到的几何先验迁移至基于传感器的车道图预测。在OpenLane-V2基准上，TopoGPT在车道级指标上平均提升+6.4，在点级指标上平均提升+11.6，并生成几何一致、结构完整的车道图。

</details>


### [123] [Absorption-Feature-Guided Distance-Decoupled Estimation and Band Selection for LWIR Hyperspectral Passive Ranging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31824)
*Shuo Liu,Chen Fan,Zhihe Chen,Xiaolin Huang,Lilian Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种名为ADER的长波红外高光谱被动测距框架，通过吸收引导的距离解耦估计与精化，实现高效、高精度的远距离测距，在真实场景中达到与LiDAR一致的空间距离结构，测距精度提升，速度比现有全波段方法快约两个数量级。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 长波红外高光谱观测中包含随距离变化的大气吸收特征，可用于远距离被动测距。但这些特征与目标温度、材料发射率和路径辐射非线性耦合，使得从观测辐射反演距离存在病态性；现有方法依赖全波段逐像素联合优化，计算成本高且未显式利用尖锐的大气吸收结构。

Method: 提出ADER框架：1)用B样条控制点表示发射率并施加平滑先验，抑制对大气吸收结构的过拟合，实现距离解耦估计；2)利用臭氧吸收线索将像素分为发射主导和反射主导两类；3)对发射主导像素，补偿路径辐射和透过率后通过一维吸收残差最小化估计距离；4)对反射主导像素，基于完整辐射模型利用下涌辐射补偿精化初始估计；5)引入基于多场景有效Fisher信息量的贪婪波段选择策略，降低光谱冗余。

Result: 在真实场景实验中，ADER在全波段和仅20个波段设置下均能恢复与LiDAR一致的空间距离结构，在评估区域内提升了测距精度，相比公开的全波段高光谱测距方法实现了约两个数量级的加速。

Conclusion: ADER通过吸收引导的距离解耦策略，在保持高精度的同时大幅降低了计算复杂度，为长波红外高光谱被动测距提供了一种高效且实用的解决方案。

Original Abstract: Long-wave infrared (LWIR) hyperspectral observations contain distance-dependent atmospheric absorption signatures, providing a physical basis for long-range passive ranging. However, in natural scenes, these signatures are nonlinearly coupled with target temperature, material emissivity, and path radiance, making distance inversion from observed radiance ill posed. Existing methods typically rely on full-band measurements and pixel-wise joint optimization, which is computationally expensive and does not explicitly exploit sharp atmospheric absorption structures. This paper proposes an Absorption-Guided Distance-Decoupled Estimation and Refinement (ADER) framework for LWIR hyperspectral passive ranging. ADER represents emissivity with B-spline control points under a smoothness prior, suppressing overfitting to atmospheric absorption structures and enabling distance-decoupled estimation. It further uses ozone-absorption cues to classify pixels into emission-dominant and reflection-dominant groups. For emission-dominant pixels, ADER compensates path radiance and transmittance and estimates distance by one-dimensional absorption-residual minimization. For reflection-dominant pixels, ADER refines the initial estimate using downwelling-radiance compensation based on the complete radiative model. To reduce spectral redundancy, ADER also introduces a greedy band selection strategy based on multi-scene effective Fisher information for the distance parameter. Experiments on real scenes show that ADER recovers LiDAR-consistent spatial distance structures under both full-band and 20-band settings, improves ranging accuracy in the evaluated regions, and achieves approximately two orders of magnitude speedup over a public full-band hyperspectral ranging method.

中文摘要翻译: 长波红外高光谱观测中包含随距离变化的大气吸收特征，为远距离被动测距提供了物理基础。然而，在自然场景中，这些特征与目标温度、材料发射率和路径辐射呈非线性耦合，使得从观测辐射中反演距离具有病态性。现有方法通常依赖全波段测量和逐像素联合优化，计算成本高昂，且未能显式利用尖锐的大气吸收结构。本文提出了一种基于吸收引导的距离解耦估计与精化（ADER）框架，用于长波红外高光谱被动测距。ADER在平滑先验下用B样条控制点表示发射率，抑制对大气吸收结构的过拟合，从而实现距离解耦估计。该方法进一步利用臭氧吸收线索将像素分为发射主导和反射主导两类。对于发射主导像素，ADER补偿路径辐射和透过率，并通过一维吸收残差最小化来估计距离。对于反射主导像素，ADER基于完整辐射模型，利用下涌辐射补偿来精化初始估计。为降低光谱冗余，ADER还引入了一种基于多场景有效Fisher信息量的贪婪波段选择策略。在真实场景上的实验表明，ADER在全波段和20波段设置下均能恢复与LiDAR一致的空间距离结构，提升了评估区域内的测距精度，并且相比公开的全波段高光谱测距方法实现了约两个数量级的加速。

</details>


### [124] [Breaking Failure Cascades: Step-Aware Reinforcement Learning for Medical Multimodal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31825)
*Junha Jung,Minbyul Jeong,Suhyeon Lim,Sungwook Jung,Jaehoon Yun,Taeyun Roh,Mujeen Sung,Jaewoo Kang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MRPO，一种基于步骤级过程奖励的强化学习算法，通过在不正确推理的早期步骤中分配指数级更大的惩罚来打破级联错误，从而显著提升多模态大语言模型在临床VQA任务上的推理质量和最终答案准确率。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有临床多模态大语言模型的后训练流程以结果为中心，仅依赖最终答案正确性或序列级偏好，导致稀疏的信用分配问题，难以优化临床应用中至关重要的推理过程。分析发现早期推理失败的级联错误是医学VQA中错误预测的主要原因。

Method: 提出Medical Reasoning-aware Policy Optimization (MRPO)，一种强化学习算法。当最终答案错误时，MRPO对早期无效推理步骤中的token分配指数级更大的惩罚，从而打破失败级联，同时不影响成功的推理路径。

Result: 在三种多模态LLM骨干上，MRPO一致优于标准GRPO和近期RL基线；在Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct上甚至超越规模更大的HuatuoGPT-Vision-34B达2.79个点。MRPO将早期推理失败率从64.0%降至13.0%。

Conclusion: 通过针对性地缓解级联失败，MRPO同时提升了推理质量和最终答案准确率，验证了过程级奖励在医学VQA中的有效性。

Original Abstract: Recent multimodal large language models have shown great promise in clinical image reasoning, but existing post-training pipelines remain predominantly outcome-centric, relying on final answer correctness or sequence-level preferences. This suffers from sparse credit assignment, making it difficult to optimize the reasoning process essential for clinical applications. Our analysis reveals that cascading errors from early-stage reasoning failures are a leading cause of incorrect predictions in medical visual question answering (VQA) benchmarks. Motivated by this, we propose Medical Reasoning-aware Policy Optimization (MRPO), an RL algorithm that incorporates step-wise process rewards. When the final answer is incorrect, MRPO assigns exponentially larger penalties to tokens in earlier invalid reasoning steps, breaking failure cascades without compromising successful paths. Across three multimodal LLM backbones, MRPO consistently outperforms standard GRPO and a recent RL baseline, and on Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct even surpasses substantially larger medical MLLMs such as HuatuoGPT-Vision-34B by 2.79 points. Moreover, MRPO reduces early-stage reasoning failures from 64.0% to 13.0%, showing that targeted mitigation of cascading failures improves both reasoning quality and final answer accuracy. Our code is available at https://github.com/dmis-lab/MRPO

中文摘要翻译: 近期多模态大语言模型在临床图像推理中展现出巨大潜力，但现有的后训练流程仍以结果为中心，依赖最终答案的正确性或序列级偏好。这会导致稀疏的信用分配问题，使得优化临床应用所必需的推理过程变得困难。我们的分析表明，早期推理失败导致的级联错误是医学视觉问答（VQA）基准中错误预测的主要原因。基于此，我们提出医学推理感知策略优化（MRPO），一种引入步骤级过程奖励的强化学习算法。当最终答案错误时，MRPO对早期无效推理步骤中的token分配指数级更大的惩罚，从而打破失败级联，同时不影响成功的推理路径。在三种多模态LLM骨干上，MRPO一致优于标准GRPO和近期RL基线；在Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct上甚至超越规模更大的医学多模态大模型（如HuatuoGPT-Vision-34B）达2.79个点。此外，MRPO将早期推理失败率从64.0%降至13.0%，表明针对性的级联失败缓解策略同时提升了推理质量和最终答案准确率。我们的代码已开源至https://github.com/dmis-lab/MRPO。

</details>


### [125] [PriorEye: Geospatial Visual Priors for End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31830)
*Kyuhwan Yeon,Benjamin Ramtoula,Daniele De Martini*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于地理空间视觉先验和双记忆架构的记忆增强模块（PriorEye），可无缝集成到现有端到端自动驾驶方法中，通过提供独立于实时传感器的视觉空间预判能力，提升驾驶性能和对传感器损坏的鲁棒性，并在NAVSIM-v2基准上取得一致改进。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有端到端自动驾驶方法仅依赖瞬时传感器观测，缺乏人类驾驶员通过先前经验获得的预期预判能力（anticipatory foresight），导致行为本质上是反应式的，难以应对复杂多变的驾驶场景。

Method: 提出一种记忆增强模块，包含双记忆架构（上下文记忆用于检索先验、持久回退记忆作为安全兜底）和自适应记忆门（根据当前状态兼容性动态调节记忆影响），可轻松集成到现有端到端方法中。地理空间视觉先验是锚定于预定驾驶路线的街景级视觉上下文，独立于实时传感器。

Result: 在NAVSIM-v2基准上，该方法在不同端到端基线模型上均取得一致的性能提升；由于先验独立于车载传感器，方法天然提升了抗传感器损坏的鲁棒性；双记忆设计在检索到的先验不可靠时仍能提供安全回退。

Conclusion: 地理空间视觉先验结合双记忆架构能有效为端到端自动驾驶注入预期预判能力，同时提升鲁棒性和安全性，是一种轻量且通用的增强方案。

Original Abstract: Most end-to-end autonomous driving methods rely solely on instantaneous sensor observations, limiting them to reactive behavior without the anticipatory foresight human drivers employ through prior experience. We introduce geospatial visual priors, street-level visual context anchored to the intended driving route, providing visual-spatial foresight independent of real-time sensors. We propose a memory augmentation module featuring a dual-memory architecture and an adaptive memory gate, which can be easily integrated into existing end-to-end approaches. This design pairs a contextual memory for retrieved priors with a persistent fallback memory, and dynamically regulates the influence of memories based on current state compatibility. Evaluated on the NAVSIM-v2 benchmark, our approach consistently improves performance across diverse end-to-end baselines. Furthermore, because these priors are independent of onboard sensors, our method inherently improves robustness against sensor corruption, while the dual-memory design ensures safe fallback when the retrieved priors themselves become unreliable. Our project page is available at https://ori-mrg.github.io/PriorEye.

中文摘要翻译: 大多数端到端自动驾驶方法仅依赖瞬时传感器观测，将其限制在反应式行为中，缺乏人类驾驶员通过先前经验所具备的预期预判能力。我们引入了地理空间视觉先验——锚定于预定驾驶路线的街景级视觉上下文，提供独立于实时传感器的视觉空间预判能力。我们提出了一种记忆增强模块，该模块采用双记忆架构和自适应记忆门，可轻松集成到现有的端到端方法中。该设计将用于检索先验的上下文记忆与持久回退记忆配对，并根据当前状态兼容性动态调节记忆的影响。在NAVSIM-v2基准上的评估表明，我们的方法在不同端到端基线模型上均取得一致的性能提升。此外，由于这些先验独立于车载传感器，我们的方法天然提升了对传感器损坏的鲁棒性，而双记忆设计确保在检索到的先验本身不可靠时仍能安全回退。我们的项目页面访问地址为：https://ori-mrg.github.io/PriorEye。

</details>


### [126] [Real-Time Source-Free Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31834)
*Sairam VCR,Varun Gopal,Poornima Jain,Vineeth N Balasubramanian,Muhammad Haris Khan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RT-SFOD，一种基于YOLOv10构建的实时源无关目标检测方法，通过双头伪标签融合（DHF）和多尺度自适应表征多样性损失（MARD），在保证高推理速度和低模型参数的同时，达到了领先的域适应检测精度。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有源无关目标检测（SFOD）方法多采用重型架构，仅追求精度而忽视了实际应用中的延迟和内存约束。作者希望证明精度与效率之间并非必须权衡，可以在保持快速紧凑的同时实现最优的域适应精度。

Method: 1) 基于YOLOv10这一无NMS的双头检测器构建；2) 提出DHF（双头伪标签融合），选择性融合一对一（O2O）和一对多（O2M）头的预测结果，兼顾精度与召回；3) 提出MARD（多尺度自适应表征多样性损失），通过对多尺度特征图施加检测感知的方差和协方差约束，缓解域偏移导致的多尺度特征判别性坍塌问题。两个模块仅在训练时使用，不影响推理。

Result: 在域偏移基准上，相比现有最优SFOD方法，RT-SFOD获得1.4~3.5%的mAP提升，吞吐量提高1.3倍，参数量减少约2倍，在速度-精度-模型大小的权衡上推进了帕累托前沿。该方法在YOLOv10上取得主要结果，并在其他YOLO和DETR双头检测器上验证了泛化性。

Conclusion: RT-SFOD证明了在源无关目标检测中，精度与效率可以兼得。通过双头融合策略和多样性正则化，该方法在保持实时推理能力和轻量化模型的同时，显著提升了域适应性能，为实际部署提供了高效解决方案。

Original Abstract: Real-world detectors for autonomous driving, surveillance, and robotics must handle domain-shifts under strict latency and memory constraints, yet existing source-free object detection (SFOD) methods rely on heavyweight architectures that prioritize accuracy alone. We show this trade-off is unnecessary: building on YOLOv10, an NMS-free dual-head detector, we achieve state-of-the-art adaptation accuracy while being faster and more compact. We observe that directly applying vanilla mean-teacher self-training to dual-head detectors leads to suboptimal adaptation performance due to two key factors. First, simple pseudo-label generation strategies, such as using a single head or directly combining high-confidence predictions from both heads, yield suboptimal supervision under domain-shift. We propose DHF (Dual-Head Pseudo-Label Fusion) which selectively admits one-to-one (O2O) and one-to-many (O2M) head predictions, preserving precision and recovering missed objects. Second, we observe domain-shift collapses multi-scale feature discriminability. We propose the use of our MARD (Multi-scale Adaptive Representation Diversification) loss which mitigates this by enforcing detection-aware variance and covariance constraints on multi-scale feature maps. Both modules are training-time only, leaving inference unchanged. Across domain-shift benchmarks, our method, RT-SFOD yields 1.4 to 3.5\% mAP gains, 1.3$\times$ higher throughput, with $\sim$2$\times$ fewer parameters than prior state-of-the-art SFOD methods, thus advancing the Pareto frontier of the speed-accuracy-model size trade-off. We report main results with YOLOv10, and demonstrate generalizability with additional YOLO- and DETR-based dual-head detectors. Code is available here: https://github.com/Sairam13001/RT-SFOD/

中文摘要翻译: 面向自动驾驶、监控和机器人领域的现实世界检测器必须在严格的延迟和内存约束下处理域偏移问题，然而现有的源无关目标检测（SFOD）方法依赖于仅追求精度的重型架构。我们证明了这种权衡并非必要：基于YOLOv10——一种无需NMS的双头检测器，我们在实现最先进域适应精度的同时，速度更快、模型更紧凑。我们观察到，将普通的均值教师自训练直接应用于双头检测器会导致次优的适应性能，这归因于两个关键因素。首先，简单的伪标签生成策略（如使用单头或直接合并两个头的高置信度预测）在域偏移下会产生次优的监督信号。我们提出DHF（双头伪标签融合），该方法选择性地接收一对一（O2O）和一对多（O2M）头的预测，既保持精度又找回遗漏的目标。其次，我们观察到域偏移会导致多尺度特征判别性坍塌。我们提出使用MARD（多尺度自适应表征多样性损失），通过对多尺度特征图施加检测感知的方差和协方差约束来缓解这一问题。两个模块仅在训练阶段生效，推理过程保持不变。在域偏移基准上，我们的方法RT-SFOD相比现有最优SFOD方法取得了1.4至3.5%的mAP提升，吞吐量提升1.3倍，参数量减少约2倍，从而推进了速度-精度-模型大小的帕累托前沿。我们报告了基于YOLOv10的主要结果，并在其他基于YOLO和DETR的双头检测器上验证了泛化性。代码开源于：https://github.com/Sairam13001/RT-SFOD/

</details>


### [127] [Towards Voxel Spacing Consistency for Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31839)
*Xin You,Runze Yang,Minghui Zhang,Hanxiao Zhang,Han Li,Yi Yu,Jie Yang,Nassir Navab,Yun Gu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Consispace，一种语义感知的重采样框架，在轴向方向上实现一致体素间距，同时保持解剖和语义一致性，并通过ODE约束和语义特征重 weighting 提升下游分割性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有体素分割研究多关注分割架构本身，而忽略了物理体素间距（voxel spacing）的影响；图像重采样作为预处理步骤与下游分割之间的交互尚未被系统性地利用。

Method: 1) 引入基于ODE的解剖约束，通过连续插值器建模切片间动力学，实现复杂解剖过渡下的忠实重建；2) 利用预训练视觉模型的密集特征构建切片内语义相关性图，通过特征重加权注入类别级语义一致性；3) 将切片内与切片间约束集成到隐式神经网络中，支持任意尺度重采样。

Result: 在多数据集上，Consispace在重建质量与感知保真度上优于现有方法，生成更平滑的切片间解剖结构，作为预处理步骤可显著提升下游分割性能。

Conclusion: Consispace通过语义感知的重采样，在保持解剖和语义一致性的同时实现一致体素间距，有效桥接了图像重采样与分割任务之间的鸿沟，为医学图像分割提供了一种有效的预处理新范式。

Original Abstract: Volumetric medical image segmentation is essential for both preoperative diagnosis and intraoperative guidance. While recent years have witnessed rapid progress in segmentation architectures, comparatively little attention is paid to the physical voxel spacing of anatomical data. Indeed, volumetric image resampling is a ubiquitous preprocessing step before segmentation, yet its interaction with downstream segmentation has not been systematically exploited. In this work, we study the correlation between image resampling and segmentation, and propose Consispace, a semantic-aware resampling framework that achieves consistent voxel spacing in the axial direction while preserving anatomical and semantic consistency. Consispace introduces an ODE-based anatomical constraint to model inter-slice dynamics with a continuous interpolator, enabling faithful reconstruction under complex anatomical transitions beyond discrete interpolation. To further couple resampling with segmentation objectives, we leverage dense features from a pretrained vision model to build intra-slice semantic correlation maps and inject class-wise semantic consistency via feature reweighting during resampling. Both intra-slice and inter-slice constraints are integrated into an implicit neural network, supporting arbitrary-scale resampling. Extensive experiments on multiple datasets demonstrate that Consispace achieves superior reconstruction quality and perceptual fidelity, produces smoother inter-slice anatomy, and improves downstream segmentation performance when used as a preprocessing step.

中文摘要翻译: 体积医学图像分割对于术前诊断和术中引导至关重要。尽管近年来分割架构取得了快速进展，但解剖数据的物理体素间距（voxel spacing）却鲜少受到关注。实际上，体积图像重采样是分割前普遍存在的预处理步骤，然而它与下游分割之间的交互尚未被系统性地利用。在这项工作中，我们研究了图像重采样与分割之间的相关性，并提出了Consispace——一种语义感知的重采样框架，能够在轴向方向上实现一致体素间距，同时保持解剖和语义一致性。Consispace引入了一种基于ODE的解剖约束，通过连续插值器建模切片间动力学，从而在复杂解剖过渡下实现超越离散插值的忠实重建。为进一步将重采样与分割目标耦合，我们利用预训练视觉模型的密集特征构建切片内语义相关性图，并通过特征重加权在重采样过程中注入类别级语义一致性。切片内与切片间约束被集成到一个隐式神经网络中，支持任意尺度的重采样。在多个数据集上的大量实验表明，Consispace实现了更优的重建质量和感知保真度，生成更平滑的切片间解剖结构，并在作为预处理步骤使用时显著提升了下游分割性能。

</details>


### [128] [SENSE-VAD: Sentient and Semantic Video Anomaly Detection for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31875)
*Nghia T. Nguyen,Lokman Bekit,Yasin Yilmaz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SENSE-VAD，首个专为自动驾驶场景中社会性复杂异常设计的合成视频异常检测基准，揭示了现有方法无法解决由社会关系而非运动特征定义的异常问题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有自动驾驶异常检测仅关注运动或外观层面的异常，忽略了由社会关系（如追赶、携带、脱离监护）构成的复杂异常场景，而这些场景在真实驾驶中至关重要。

Method: 基于CARLA模拟器和Unreal Engine生成涵盖个体行为、群体行为、人-物交互、骑行者交互、车辆与智能体等多类别的合成异常视频，并设计社会异常与运动/外观异常的分离原则，同时提供真实世界视频用于sim-to-real迁移评估。

Result: 对现有SOTA视频异常检测方法进行评测，结果表明社会性复杂异常构成了当前方法无法解决的独特挑战。

Conclusion: 社会性复杂异常是自动驾驶异常检测中尚未解决的关键问题，SENSE-VAD基准为该方向提供了标准化的评估平台和公开资源。

Original Abstract: Autonomous vehicles (AVs) must navigate not only motion-based hazards but also socially complex situations whose danger is constituted by inter-agent relationships rather than movement statistics alone. A child running away from a guardian, a person being carried by another, or a pursuer chasing a pedestrian across a sidewalk are all anomalous in social context, yet none produces an obvious motion signal that current anomaly detectors are equipped to flag. We introduce SENSE-VAD, the first synthetic video anomaly detection benchmark for autonomous driving explicitly designed around socially complex anomalies. Using the CARLA simulator and Unreal Engine (UE), we generate distinct anomaly scenarios across multiple categories: individual behaviors, group behaviors, person--object interactions, cyclist interactions, vehicle & agent, each annotated with per-frame binary labels. A key design principle is the separation of social anomaly from motion-based or appearance-based anomaly: many scenarios involve motion of objects that appears unremarkable in isolation but is anomalous in relational context. We additionally provide real-world normal and anomalous videos as a sim-to-real transfer probe. We evaluate state-of-the-art video anomaly detection baselines and demonstrate that socially complex anomalies constitute a distinct and currently unsolved challenge. Our dataset, annotations, and generation code are publicly available.

中文摘要翻译: 自动驾驶车辆不仅需要应对基于运动的危险，还必须处理社会性复杂情境——这类情境的危险性源于智能体之间的社会关系，而非单纯的移动统计数据。一个儿童脱离监护人奔跑、一个人被另一个人抱起、或一个追逐者穿过人行道追赶行人，这些在社会语境中都是异常行为，但均未产生当前异常检测器能够识别的明显运动信号。我们提出SENSE-VAD，这是首个专为自动驾驶设计、明确围绕社会性复杂异常的合成视频异常检测基准。利用CARLA模拟器和Unreal Engine，我们生成了覆盖多个类别的不同异常场景：个体行为、群体行为、人-物交互、骑行者交互、车辆与智能体，每个场景均配有逐帧二值标签。一个关键设计原则是将社会异常与基于运动或外观的异常相分离：许多场景中，物体的运动孤立看来毫无异常，但在关系语境中却构成异常。我们额外提供了真实世界的正常与异常视频，作为模拟到现实迁移的探针。我们评估了当前最先进的视频异常检测基线方法，证明社会性复杂异常构成了一个独特且尚未解决的挑战。我们的数据集、标注和生成代码均已公开可用。

</details>


### [129] [RESOLVE: A Multi-Resolution and Multi-Modal Dataset for Roadside Cooperative Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31895)
*Shaozu Ding,Linan Song,Marco De Vincenzi,Dajiang Suo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RESOLVE是一个大规模真实世界路侧多分辨率LiDAR与相机融合基准数据集，用于系统评估单模态和融合架构在路侧3D检测与跟踪中的表现。通过控制LiDAR分辨率变化，揭示了多模态融合如何补偿点云稀疏性，为设计成本高效的路侧多模态感知提供了线索。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有研究缺乏对单模态和相机-LiDAR融合架构在LiDAR点云稀疏性变化（由传感器配置和场景依赖的感知条件引起）下行为表现的系统性理解与评估。

Method: 构建了一个包含超过10万张图像、2.6万帧点云和22万个手动标注边界框的真实世界路侧数据集RESOLVE，涵盖三个LiDAR分辨率级别，在控制其他感知和环境因素固定的条件下进行跨架构公平比较。

Result: 大量基准实验揭示了多模态融合如何补偿LiDAR点云稀疏性，包括分辨率变化、感知距离以及训练-推理分辨率不匹配导致的点云分布偏移下的架构表现差异。

Conclusion: 多模态融合能够有效补偿LiDAR点云稀疏性带来的性能下降，为设计成本高效的路侧多模态感知系统提供了重要线索。

Original Abstract: LiDAR has increasingly been integrated into traffic cameras to expand coverage and mitigate occlusion in roadside cooperative perception. However, how unimodal and camera-LiDAR fusion architectures behave under variations in LiDAR point sparsity induced by sensor configurations and scene-dependent sensing conditions remains underexplored. We introduce RESOLVE, a large-scale real-world benchmark dataset featuring multi-resolution roadside LiDAR and synchronized camera-LiDAR sensing for systematic evaluation of unimodal and fusion-based architectures in roadside 3D detection and tracking. RESOLVE contains over 100k images and 26k point cloud frames with 220k manually annotated bounding boxes, captured at a real-world urban intersection across diverse lighting and weather conditions and spanning 10 classes of traffic participants. In particular, RESOLVE enables controlled evaluation across three LiDAR resolution levels while keeping all other sensing and environmental factors fixed. This allows fair cross-architecture comparisons under point cloud distribution shifts resulting from resolution variations, sensing distance, and training-inference resolution mismatches. Results from extensive benchmark experiments reveal insights into how multimodal fusion can compensate for LiDAR point sparsity, offering clues for designing cost-efficient roadside multimodal perception. The dataset and benchmark codes are available at https://github.com/ASU-Suo-Lab/RESOLVE.

中文摘要翻译: LiDAR已越来越多地集成到交通摄像头中，以扩展覆盖范围并缓解路侧协同感知中的遮挡问题。然而，单模态与相机-LiDAR融合架构在传感器配置和场景依赖感知条件引起的LiDAR点云稀疏性变化下的行为表现仍未得到充分探索。我们提出了RESOLVE，一个大规模真实世界基准数据集，包含多分辨率路侧LiDAR和同步的相机-LiDAR感知数据，用于对路侧3D检测与跟踪中的单模态和基于融合的架构进行系统评估。RESOLVE包含超过10万张图像和2.6万帧点云，带有22万个手动标注的边界框，数据采集自真实世界城市交叉路口，覆盖多样化的光照和天气条件，包含10类交通参与者。特别地，RESOLVE能够在保持所有其他感知和环境因素固定的条件下，对三个LiDAR分辨率级别进行受控评估。这使得在由分辨率变化、感知距离以及训练-推理分辨率不匹配导致的点云分布偏移下，能够进行公平的跨架构比较。大量基准实验的结果揭示了多模态融合如何补偿LiDAR点云稀疏性，为设计成本高效的路侧多模态感知提供了线索。数据集和基准代码可在https://github.com/ASU-Suo-Lab/RESOLVE获取。

</details>


### [130] [Attend, Transform, or Silence: Operator-Level Visual Skipping for Efficient Multimodal LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31903)
*Zhaoyang Luo,Runmin Dong,Miao Yang,Fan Wei,Yushan Lai,Bin Luo,Haohuan Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文从答案可观察性视角发现多模态大模型中视觉token更新存在“答案沉默冗余”，并提出操作符级别的视觉token跳跃框架，通过选择性跳过冗余的注意力或FFN计算来加速推理，在降低33.7%计算量的同时保留了99.5%的模型性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有加速方法通常采用粗粒度的视觉token移除或整层跳过策略，可能丢弃细粒度证据或同时抑制有用和冗余操作符。作者希望从更精细的操作符级别实现视觉token计算加速。

Method: 将每个Transformer层分解为注意力（Attention）和FFN两个操作符，提出操作符级别的视觉token跳跃框架，保留完整视觉token序列，但根据冗余性判断，选择性地跳过冗余的注意力计算、FFN计算，或两者都跳过。

Result: 在三种MLLM架构和10个VQA基准上，方法实现了强效率-精度权衡，在Qwen3-VL上减少33.7% TFLOPs同时保留99.5%的原始模型性能。

Conclusion: 视觉token计算中确实存在答案沉默冗余，且有用的视觉计算具有操作符主导和层依赖特性；通过操作符级别的精细跳过策略，可以在大幅降低计算量的同时保持模型性能。

Original Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) increasingly process long visual-token sequences, increasing the overall inference computation. Existing acceleration methods usually remove visual tokens or skip visual-token updates in entire layers, but these coarse strategies may discard fine-grained evidence or suppress useful operators together with redundant ones. In this paper, we study visual-token computation from an answer-observable perspective and find that late visual-token updates can remain large while having little effect on answer-token representations. Motivated by this answer-silent redundancy, we decompose each Transformer layer into attention and FFN operators and show that useful visual computation is often operator-dominant and layer-dependent. We propose an operator-level visual-token skipping framework that preserves the full visual-token sequence while selectively bypassing redundant attention, FFN, or both. Experiments across three MLLM architectures and 10 VQA benchmarks show that our method achieves strong efficiency-accuracy trade-offs, reducing \textbf{33.7\%} TFLOPs on Qwen3-VL while retaining \textbf{99.5\%} of the vanilla model performance.

中文摘要翻译: 多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在处理长视觉token序列时，整体推理计算量不断增加。现有加速方法通常移除视觉token或跳过整层中的视觉token更新，但这些粗粒度策略可能丢弃细粒度证据，或同时抑制有用操作符与冗余操作符。本文从答案可观察性视角研究视觉token计算，发现后期视觉token更新虽保持较大幅度，但对答案token表示影响甚微。受这种答案沉默冗余启发，我们将每个Transformer层分解为注意力（Attention）和FFN操作符，表明有用的视觉计算通常具有操作符主导性和层依赖性。我们提出了一种操作符级别的视觉token跳跃框架，该框架保留完整视觉token序列，同时选择性地绕过冗余的注意力、FFN或两者。在三种MLLM架构和10个VQA基准上的实验表明，我们的方法实现了强效率-精度权衡，在Qwen3-VL上减少33.7%的TFLOPs，同时保留了99.5%的原始模型性能。

</details>


### [131] [DriveWeaver: Point-Conditioned Video Inpainting for Controllable Vehicle Insertion in Autonomous Driving Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31918)
*Junzhe Jiang,Zipei Ma,Zijie Pan,Li Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DriveWeaver提出了一种基于视频修复的车辆可控插入框架，利用车辆点云条件生成时序一致、光照融合自然的前景车辆，并结合全局到局部的分层修复策略和3D高斯表示，实现自动驾驶仿真场景的高质量、可扩展增强。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有自动驾驶仿真中的前景车辆插入方法依赖预重建的3D资产，存在光照不一致、泛化能力差、难以大规模部署等问题。

Method: 提出DriveWeaver框架：1）基于车辆点云条件进行视频修复，实现前景与背景的无缝融合；2）设计全局到局部的分层修复策略，保证长序列生成中车辆身份和外观的一致性；3）通过城市重建管线提取显式3D高斯表示，支持实时渲染。

Result: 在多个数据集上的大量实验表明，该方法在视觉真实感和几何一致性上优于现有基线，为可扩展的自动驾驶场景增强提供了鲁棒工具。

Conclusion: DriveWeaver通过视频修复与点云条件相结合的方式，有效解决了光照不一致和3D资产依赖问题，实现了高质量、可扩展的自动驾驶仿真场景车辆插入。

Original Abstract: A pivotal step in autonomous driving simulation involves inserting foreground vehicles with predefined trajectories into simulated scenes. This process enhances scene diversity and facilitates the creation of various corner cases for testing and improving autonomous driving models. However, existing methods often rely on pre-reconstructed 3D assets, which frequently lead to lighting inconsistencies between the inserted foreground and the background. Moreover, the reliance on limited, manually-curated 3D assets hinders large-scale deployment. To address these challenges, we propose DriveWeaver, a novel framework for controllable vehicle insertion in autonomous driving simulation. Specifically, for a masked target insertion area, DriveWeaver performs video inpainting conditioned on vehicle point clouds to generate high-quality, temporally consistent vehicles. This video-inpainting-based approach ensures seamless blending between the foreground and background, while the readily available point cloud conditions enable superior generalization. To support long-term generation, we further design a global-to-local hierarchical inpainting strategy, ensuring the consistent identity and appearance of the inserted vehicles. Meanwhile, we extract explicit 3D Gaussian representations of the inserted vehicles through an urban reconstruction pipeline to enable real-time rendering for autonomous driving simulation. Extensive experiments across diverse datasets demonstrate that our method outperforms existing baselines in visual realism and geometric consistency, providing a robust tool for scalable autonomous driving scene augmentation.

中文摘要翻译: 自动驾驶仿真中的一个关键步骤是将具有预设轨迹的前景车辆插入到模拟场景中。这一过程增强了场景多样性，并有助于创建各种边缘案例，用于测试和改进自动驾驶模型。然而，现有方法通常依赖预先重建的3D资产，这经常导致插入的前景与背景之间存在光照不一致问题。此外，对有限的人工筛选3D资产的依赖阻碍了大规模部署。为了解决这些挑战，我们提出了DriveWeaver，一种用于自动驾驶仿真中可控车辆插入的新型框架。具体而言，对于掩码标记的目标插入区域，DriveWeaver以车辆点云为条件进行视频修复，生成高质量、时序一致的车辆。这种基于视频修复的方法确保了前景与背景之间的无缝融合，而易于获取的点云条件则实现了优异的泛化能力。为了支持长时序生成，我们进一步设计了从全局到局部的分层修复策略，确保插入车辆的身份和外观一致性。同时，我们通过城市重建管线提取插入车辆的显式3D高斯表示，以实现自动驾驶仿真的实时渲染。在多个数据集上的大量实验表明，我们的方法在视觉真实感和几何一致性方面优于现有基线，为可扩展的自动驾驶场景增强提供了鲁棒工具。

</details>


### [132] [InstanceControl: Controllable Complex Image Generation without Instance Labeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31924)
*Xiaoyu Liu,Huan Wang,Fan Li,Zhixin Wang,Jiaqi Xu,Ming Liu,Wangmeng Zuo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出InstanceControl方法，利用视觉-语言模型自动建立文本描述与视觉条件间的实例级对应，无需人工标注即可实现多实例可控图像生成。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有可控图像生成方法（如ControlNet）在多实例场景中常出现实例属性混淆问题，而近期方法依赖人工实例标注，劳动成本高。

Method: 利用视觉-语言模型（VLM）自动从文本提示中解析实例描述，同时基于视觉条件预测实例掩码；并提出自适应掩码精炼策略，在生成过程中动态优化掩码质量。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法在生成保真度和精确实例级控制方面均优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: InstanceControl有效解决了多实例可控生成中的属性混淆问题，消除了人工标注需求，实现了更精确的实例级控制。

Original Abstract: Controllable image generation methods, such as ControlNet, have demonstrated a remarkable capacity to introduce visual conditions(e.g., depth maps) to guide image generation. However, these methods often struggle with complex multi-instance scenes, frequently leading to attribute confusion among instances. While recent approaches attempt to mitigate this via manual instance labeling, such requirements are labor-intensive. In this paper, we propose InstanceControl, a novel multi-instance controllable generation method that eliminates the need for instance labeling. We identify the primary bottleneck in existing methods as the inability to accurately associate instance descriptions with their corresponding regions within visual conditions. To address this, we leverage the Vision-Language Model (VLM) to establish instance-level correspondences between text prompts and visual conditions. Specifically, the VLM automatically parses instance descriptions from the text prompts and simultaneously predicts instance masks based on the visual conditions. Furthermore, since the predicted masks may contain noise, we introduce an adaptive mask refinement strategy that dynamically refines these instance masks during the generation process. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our approach outperforms state-of-the-art methods, achieving superior fidelity and precise instance-level control.

中文摘要翻译: 可控图像生成方法（如ControlNet）在引入视觉条件（如深度图）引导图像生成方面展现出了显著的能力。然而，这些方法在处理复杂多实例场景时常常遇到困难，频繁导致实例间的属性混淆。虽然近期的方法尝试通过人工实例标注来缓解这一问题，但这种标注需求是劳动密集型的。在本文中，我们提出InstanceControl，一种新颖的多实例可控生成方法，它消除了实例标注的需求。我们发现现有方法的主要瓶颈在于无法准确地将实例描述与其在视觉条件中的对应区域关联起来。为解决这一问题，我们利用视觉-语言模型（VLM）在文本提示与视觉条件之间建立实例级的对应关系。具体来说，VLM自动从文本提示中解析实例描述，并同时基于视觉条件预测实例掩码。此外，由于预测的掩码可能包含噪声，我们引入了一种自适应掩码精炼策略，在生成过程中动态优化这些实例掩码。大量实验表明，我们的方法优于现有最先进方法，实现了更高的生成保真度和精确的实例级控制。

</details>


### [133] [No Place to Hide: Benchmarking Video Hallucination with Background-Controlled Pairs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31933)
*Haojian Huang,Harold Haodong Chen,Meng Luo,Junjia Du,Shanqing Xu,Ziheng Chen,Yanxiang Huang,Yinchuan Li,Ying-Cong Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了VidPair-Halluc基准，利用背景相似但前景语义不同的视频对来评估大视频模型的幻觉问题，通过PairFlow流水线构建了1K对抗性视频对和11K时空QA对，实验表明主流模型在对抗性环境下对细粒度视频理解存在持续困难。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基准主要依赖文本扰动或对抗性问题，忽略了视觉背景的一致性，无法准确将模型错误归因于真实幻觉而非背景变化。

Method: 提出PairFlow流水线，利用文本到图像和视频生成技术，系统化地创作故事、生成连贯视频片段并组装成对抗性视频对；涵盖空间和时间推理，跨越十个语义方面，构建了1K高质量对抗性视频对和11K时空QA对，并对背景和前景变化进行控制。

Result: 在主流大视频模型上的评估显示，模型在对抗性环境下对鲁棒的细粒度视频理解存在持续困难。

Conclusion: VidPair-Halluc为评估视频幻觉提供了更严格和可控的条件，揭示了当前模型在细粒度视频理解方面的不足。

Original Abstract: We introduce VidPair-Halluc, a new benchmark for evaluating video hallucination in large video models (LVMs) under rigorous and controlled conditions. Unlike previous benchmarks that primarily rely on text-based perturbations or adversarial questions while neglecting the consistency of visual backgrounds, VidPair-Halluc features video pairs with highly similar backgrounds but distinctly different foreground semantics, enabling precise attribution of model errors to genuine hallucination rather than background variation. The benchmark is constructed through PairFlow, a pipeline that leverages recent advances in text-to-image and video generation to systematically compose stories, generate coherent video clips, and assemble them into adversarial pairs. Covering both spatial and temporal reasoning across ten semantic aspects, VidPair-Halluc comprises 1K high-quality adversarial video pairs and 11K spatio-temporal QA pairs with control over background and foreground variations. Evaluations on mainstream LVMs show persistent difficulty with robust fine-grained video understanding in adversarial settings, and code and data are available at the https://jethrojames.github.io/VidPair-Halluc/.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出了VidPair-Halluc，这是一个新的基准，用于在严格和受控条件下评估大视频模型中的视频幻觉问题。与以往主要依赖文本扰动或对抗性问题而忽视视觉背景一致性的基准不同，VidPair-Halluc采用背景高度相似但前景语义明显不同的视频对，从而能够将模型错误精确归因于真实的幻觉而非背景变化。该基准通过PairFlow流水线构建，该流水线利用文本到图像和视频生成领域的最新进展，系统地创作故事、生成连贯的视频片段并将其组装成对抗性对。VidPair-Halluc涵盖跨越十个语义方面的空间和时间推理，包含1K高质量对抗性视频对和11K时空QA对，并对背景和前景变化进行控制。在主流大视频模型上的评估显示，模型在对抗性环境下对鲁棒的细粒度视频理解存在持续困难。代码和数据已在https://jethrojames.github.io/VidPair-Halluc/公开。

</details>


### [134] [World Narrative Model for Highly Controllable Video Generation: A Paradigm Shift from Pixel Sampling to Physical World Orchestration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31946)
*Ye Chen,Xuanhong Chen,Yupeng Zhu,Liming Tan,Zhewen Wan,Yuxuan Xiong,Tielong Wang,Jinfan Liu,Wuze Zhang,Xiongzhen Zhang,Feifei Li,Xianglin Luo,Zhehan Zhao,Zhifan Zhang,Laisheng Kou,Zhujing Liang,Yugang Chen,Muchun Chen,Xu Miao,Yijing Zhang,Xiaojie Sheng,Qiang Hu,Jialiang Chen,Weimin Zhang,Wenjun Zhang,Bingbing Ni*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出世界叙事模型（WNM），将视频生成解耦为结构化4D世界表征（几何、运动、光照等）和像素渲染两个阶段，通过确定性蓝图驱动基础模型，显著提升可控性，减少“抽卡”式概率生成。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有视频生成模型缺乏可控性，将视频视为像素分布采样问题，忽略了显式的实例级4D物理世界，导致内容创作者无法定量指定几何、运动、相机参数和光照，陷入低效且昂贵的“抽卡”循环。

Method: WNM将“渲染什么”（结构化物理叙事）与“如何渲染”（像素生成过程）解耦。协作智能体将稀疏多模态输入（文本、参考视频、草图）转化为具有场景几何、物体布局、骨骼运动、轨迹、相机运动和光照的全可编辑4D世界表征，作为确定性蓝图驱动现有视频基础模型（冻结或轻量适配）进行渲染。

Result: 实验表明WNM大幅减少了概率性“抽卡”调用，生成的视频在布局、运动、摄影方面紧密遵循创作者意图。框架开放模块化，各组件可独立改进。

Conclusion: WNM通过结构化4D预可视化的范式，将视频生成从黑箱采样转变为可编辑、可控制的确定性流程，为工业级专业视频创作提供了高效平台。

Original Abstract: The fundamental obstacle to industrial grade video generation is the lack of controllability: existing models treat video as a pixel distribution sampling problem, bypassing the explicit, instance level $4D$ $(3D + T)$ physical world. Consequently, content creators cannot specify geometry, motion, camera parameters, or lighting in a deterministic, quantitative way, leading to the infamous ''gacha'' loop that makes professional content creation prohibitively inefficient and expensive. To address this, we introduce the World Narrative Model (WNM), a paradigm that decouples what to render -- the structured physical narrative -- from how to render -- the pixel generation process. WNM replaces end-to-end black-box sampling with orchestrated $4D$ pre-visualization for media generation. Collaborative agents translate sparse multimodal inputs, including text, reference videos, and sketches, into a fully editable world representation with scene geometry, object layouts, character/animal skeleton motion, trajectories, camera motion, and lighting at quantitative, physically meaningful granularity. This representation acts as a deterministic structural blueprint that drives existing video foundation models, either frozen or lightly adapted, to render final footage, turning the base model into a faithful neural shader. Built on this engine, our human-AI platform supports automatic world generation and pre-visualization aligned with professional filmmaking pipelines, while director consoles enable seamless human refinement. Experiments show that WNM greatly reduces probabilistic ``gacha'' calls and produces videos whose layout, motion, and cinematography closely follow creator intent. The framework is open and modular, allowing each component, such as world representation, control agents, and adapters, to be independently improved. Project website: https://glassroom.sjtu.edu.cn/WNM/.

中文摘要翻译: 工业级视频生成的根本障碍在于缺乏可控性：现有模型将视频视为像素分布采样问题，绕过了显式的、实例级4D（3D+T）物理世界。因此，内容创作者无法以确定性的量化方式指定几何、运动、相机参数或光照，导致臭名昭著的"抽卡"循环，使专业内容创作效率极低且成本高昂。为解决此问题，我们提出了世界叙事模型（WNM），该范式将"渲染什么"——结构化的物理叙事——与"如何渲染"——像素生成过程——解耦。WNM用编排好的4D预可视化替代了端到端的黑箱采样。协作智能体将稀疏的多模态输入（包括文本、参考视频和草图）转化为完全可编辑的世界表征，包含场景几何、物体布局、人物/动物骨骼运动、轨迹、相机运动以及具有量化物理意义粒度的光照。该表征作为确定性的结构蓝图，驱动现有的视频基础模型（冻结或轻量适配）渲染最终画面，将基础模型转化为忠实的神经着色器。基于该引擎，我们的人机协作平台支持自动化的世界生成和与专业电影制作流程对齐的预可视化，同时导演控制台支持无缝的人工精调。实验表明，WNM大幅减少了概率性的"抽卡"调用，生成的视频在布局、运动和摄影技法上紧密遵循创作者意图。该框架开放且模块化，允许世界表征、控制智能体和适配器等各个组件独立改进。项目网站：https://glassroom.sjtu.edu.cn/WNM/。

</details>


### [135] [AnyBokeh: Physics-Guided Any-to-Any Bokeh Editing with Optical Fingerprint Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31959)
*Xinyu Hou,Xiaoming Li,Zongsheng Yue,Chen Change Loy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AnyBokeh提出了一种物理引导的任意到任意景深编辑框架，通过估计源图像的符号弥散圆图和视差图，利用光学指纹实现源到目标光学特性的迁移，无需重建全聚焦图像即可完成可控的景深编辑。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有方法通常假设输入为全聚焦图像，或先恢复全聚焦图像再渲染新景深，这会丢弃源图像中的有用模糊线索，并将重建伪影传播到最终编辑结果中。

Method: 1) 估计源图像的符号弥散圆图（signed CoC）和视差图；2) 建模符号弥散圆与视差差值的线性关系，估计源特定的光学指纹并转移到目标焦点和光圈设置；3) 基于源和目标弥散圆图的条件生成编辑器进行相对模糊合成，实现空间自适应去模糊、保真和散焦渲染。

Result: 在真实世界基准上的实验表明，AnyBokeh在任意到任意景深编辑、全聚焦到景深渲染和散焦去模糊任务中均实现了忠实且可控的编辑，无需全聚焦重建和测试时景深级标定。

Conclusion: AnyBokeh通过物理引导的框架有效解决了单张图像的后捕获景深编辑难题，避免了传统流水线的信息损失和伪影传播问题，在多个任务上取得了优越性能。

Original Abstract: Depth-of-field control is a fundamental tool in photography, yet post-capture bokeh editing from a single image remains challenging. A practical editor should handle images captured under arbitrary focus and aperture settings. Existing methods typically assume an all-in-focus input, or first recover an all-in-focus image before rendering new bokeh. Such pipelines can discard useful blur cues from the source image and propagate reconstruction artifacts into the final edit. We introduce AnyBokeh, a physics-guided framework for any-to-any bokeh editing. Instead of treating source blur merely as a degradation to be removed, AnyBokeh estimates the source blur state with a signed circle-of-confusion map and a disparity map. By modeling the linear relation between signed circle of confusion and disparity difference, AnyBokeh estimates a source-specific optical fingerprint and transfers the source optical characteristics to the desired focus and aperture setting. A generative editor conditioned on both source and target circle-of-confusion maps then performs relative blur synthesis, enabling spatially adaptive deblurring, preservation, and defocus rendering. To support physically supervised learning, we further construct a high-fidelity synthetic dataset with accurate depth, focus distance, and full EXIF metadata. Experiments on real-world benchmarks show that AnyBokeh achieves faithful and controllable editing across any-to-any bokeh editing, all-in-focus-to-bokeh rendering, and defocus deblurring, while avoiding all-in-focus reconstruction and test-time bokeh-level calibration commonly required by existing approaches. The code and dataset will be available at https://github.com/itsmag11/AnyBokeh.

中文摘要翻译: 景深控制是摄影中的基本工具，然而从单张图像进行后捕获散景编辑仍然具有挑战性。一个实用的编辑器应能处理在任意焦点和光圈设置下拍摄的图像。现有方法通常假设输入为全聚焦图像，或者先恢复全聚焦图像再渲染新的散景。这类流程会丢弃源图像中有用的模糊线索，并将重建伪影传播到最终编辑结果中。我们提出了AnyBokeh，一个物理引导的任意到任意散景编辑框架。AnyBokeh并非将源模糊仅视为需要去除的退化，而是通过符号弥散圆图和视差图来估计源模糊状态。通过建模符号弥散圆与视差差值之间的线性关系，AnyBokeh估计源特定的光学指纹，并将源光学特征迁移到目标焦点和光圈设置下。一个以源和目标弥散圆图为条件的生成编辑器随后执行相对模糊合成，实现空间自适应去模糊、保真和散焦渲染。为了支持物理监督学习，我们进一步构建了一个高保真合成数据集，包含精确的深度、对焦距离和完整的EXIF元数据。在真实世界基准上的实验表明，AnyBokeh在任意到任意散景编辑、全聚焦到散景渲染和散焦去模糊任务中均实现了忠实且可控的编辑，同时避免了现有方法通常需要的全聚焦重建和测试时散景级别标定。代码和数据集将发布在https://github.com/itsmag11/AnyBokeh。

</details>


### [136] [Planar-SfM: Camera Pose Estimation via Homography Graph Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31979)
*Gabi Pragier,Matan Karklinsky,David Ungarish,Avi Ben-Cohen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种将平面场景从SfM系统的缺陷转化为优势的统一框架。通过从多个视图中的平面区域进行单应性分解估计相机位姿，并构建位姿图结合谱嵌入滤除不可靠边，最终提取最大一致生成树实现鲁棒位姿恢复。在篮球场等高平面场景和IMC Phototourism无约束户外场景上均表现优异。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统SfM系统在处理平面场景时，基于极线几何的方法会出现退化（退化），导致位姿估计失败。作者不将平面视为限制，而是将其作为一种几何约束的来源加以利用，从而在传统方法失效的场景中实现鲁棒位姿恢复。

Method: 提出一种基于图的方法：(1) 对多视图间可见的每个平面进行单应性分解，得到独立的相对相机位姿估计；(2) 构建位姿图，每个节点代表相机，边代表单应性估计的位姿；(3) 采用谱嵌入将位姿估计映射到实线上，基于几何一致性和视觉一致性识别并滤除不可靠边；(4) 提取最大一致生成树用于位姿恢复。该方法统一处理高度平面场景和一般3D环境。

Result: 在篮球场等高平面场景中，传统方法难以处理，本文方法表现优异；在IMC Phototourism基准的无约束户外场景中，本文方法达到或超过了现有最先进水平。

Conclusion: 将平面场景从SfM的退化条件转化为可利用的几何约束来源，通过单应性分解和图谱方法有效提升了平面场景下的位姿恢复鲁棒性，同时不损失一般3D场景的性能。

Original Abstract: Structure from Motion (SfM) systems traditionally struggle with planar scenes, where standard epipolar geometry-based methods become degenerate. Rather than viewing planar surfaces as a limitation, we propose a unified framework that leverages them as a source of geometric constraints. Our key insight is that each planar surface visible across multiple views provides an independent estimate of relative camera poses through homography decomposition. By aggregating estimates from multiple planes or even from a single dominant plane we achieve robust pose recovery in scenarios where traditional methods fail. We introduce a novel graph-based approach that constructs a pose-graph from homography estimates and employs spectral embedding to identify and filter unreliable edges. Our method maps homography-based pose estimates onto the real line based on their geometric and visual consistency, enabling efficient extraction of a maximally consistent spanning tree for pose recovery. This approach naturally handles both highly planar scenes, such as indoor sports arenas, and general $3$D environments. We demonstrate superior performance on basketball court imagery where existing methods struggle, while matching or exceeding state-of-the-art results on unconstrained outdoor scenes from the IMC Phototourism benchmark.

中文摘要翻译: 运动恢复结构（SfM）系统传统上难以处理平面场景，因为基于标准对极几何的方法在此类场景中会退化。我们不将平面表面视为限制，而是提出一个统一框架，将其作为几何约束的来源加以利用。我们的关键洞察在于，在多视图中可见的每个平面表面都能通过单应性分解提供独立的相对相机位姿估计。通过聚合来自多个平面甚至单个主导平面的估计，我们在传统方法失效的场景中实现了鲁棒的位姿恢复。我们引入了一种新颖的基于图的方法，从单应性估计构建位姿图，并采用谱嵌入来识别和过滤不可靠的边。该方法将基于单应性的位姿估计依据其几何和视觉一致性映射到实线上，从而能够高效提取最大一致生成树用于位姿恢复。该方法天然适用于高度平面场景（如室内体育场馆）和一般3D环境。我们在篮球场图像上展示了优越性能——此类场景现有方法难以处理，同时在IMC Phototourism基准的无约束户外场景上达到或超越了最先进的结果。

</details>


### [137] [LUNA: Learning Universal 3D Human Animation Beyond Skinning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31981)
*Peng Li,Rawal Khirodkar,Junxuan Li,Yuan Dong,Chen Cao,Yuan Liu,Wenhan Luo,Yike Guo,Shunsuke Saito*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LUNA是一个无LBS的神经动画模型，通过Transformer运动回归器将多种2D控制信号直接映射到3D高斯变形，实现无需显式人体拟合的逼真3D人像动画，具备零样本跨身份泛化能力。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于LBS和参数化身体模型的3D人像动画方法受限于表达力不足且因拟合不佳引入伪影，亟需一种无需显式身体拟合、能直接从2D控制信号驱动3D人像动画的通用模型。

Method: 提出LUNA模型，核心包括：(1) 基于Transformer的运动回归器，解耦全局刚体运动与局部非刚体动力学；(2) 混合监督策略，从LBS教师模型蒸馏软结构先验，并设计损失函数支持在有限拟合数据和大规模无标签视频上联合训练；(3) 直接将多种2D控制（图像、关键点、草图等）映射为3D高斯变形。

Result: 实验表明LUNA在视觉保真度上与LBS方法相当，能生成逼真的人体运动，并实现零样本跨身份泛化，支持多种驱动模态。

Conclusion: LUNA是首个支持隐式2D驱动的端到端3D可动画化模型，在无需LBS的情况下实现了与现有方法可比的视觉效果和更强的泛化能力。

Original Abstract: Creating photorealistic, animatable 3D human avatars from monocular images still largely depends on Linear Blend Skinning (LBS) and parametric body models, which constrain expressivity and often introduce artifacts due to imperfect fitting. We propose LUNA, an LBS-free universal neural animation model that directly maps multiple 2D controls like images, keypoints, sketches, and unseen characters into 3D Gaussian deformations, bypassing explicit body fitting. At its core, a transformer-based motion regressor disentangles global rigid motion from fine-grained local dynamics to capture both coherent movement and subtle non-rigid effects. To resolve the inherent ambiguity of 2D-to-3D lifting while scaling beyond fitted datasets, we introduce hybrid supervision that distills soft structural priors from an LBS teacher and a loss that supports training on both limited fitted data and large in-the-wild unlabeled videos. Extensive experiments show LUNA achieves competitive visual fidelity compared to LBS-based approaches, while delivering realistic human motion and zero-shot cross-identity generalization across diverse driving modalities. To the best of our knowledge, LUNA is the first end-to-end 3D animatable model that supports implicit 2D driving.

中文摘要翻译: 从单目图像创建逼真且可动画化的3D人体虚拟形象仍然在很大程度上依赖于线性混合蒙皮（LBS）和参数化身体模型，这限制了表达力，并且常常因拟合不完美而引入伪影。我们提出LUNA，一个无LBS的通用神经动画模型，它直接将多种2D控制信号（如图像、关键点、草图以及未见角色）映射为3D高斯变形，绕过了显式的人体拟合过程。其核心是一个基于Transformer的运动回归器，它将全局刚体运动与细粒度局部动力学解耦，从而同时捕捉连贯运动和细微的非刚体效应。为了解决2D到3D提升中固有的歧义性，同时扩展到超越已拟合数据集的规模，我们引入了混合监督策略：从LBS教师模型中蒸馏软结构先验，并设计了一种损失函数，支持在有限的已拟合数据和大规模野外无标签视频上联合训练。大量实验表明，与基于LBS的方法相比，LUNA在视觉保真度上具有竞争力，同时能够生成逼真的人体运动，并在多种驱动模态下实现零样本的跨身份泛化。据我们所知，LUNA是首个支持隐式2D驱动的端到端3D可动画化模型。

</details>


### [138] [ERA: Entropy-Guided Visual Token Pruning with Rectified Attention for Efficient MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31982)
*Yuhao Wang,Mu Qiao,Haiwen Diao,Yunzhi Zhuge,Pingping Zhang,Xindong Zhang,Lei Zhang,Huchuan Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ERA提出了一种熵引导的视觉token剪枝框架，通过修正注意力机制解决多模态大模型因视觉token缩减导致的注意力logit崩塌问题，实现高效推理。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 多模态大模型因长视觉token序列导致推理成本高昂，现有训练无关的视觉token缩减方法会扭曲注意力分布，引发注意力logit崩塌现象。

Method: ERA包含三个核心组件：双视角熵剪枝（DEP）联合建模视觉多样性与头级显著性来识别代表性锚点token；偏置感知token回收（BTR）将剪枝token回收至对应锚点并估计聚类级logit偏置；logit保持注意力修正（LAR）将估计的偏置注入注意力logit以修正token缩减导致的崩塌。

Result: ERA在单图像、多图像和视频等多种场景下，对多种MLLMs均能保持鲁棒性能，同时实现实际加速效果。

Conclusion: ERA将logit保持的视觉token剪枝确立为高效MLLMs的原则性框架，统一了理论基础、算法设计和实际部署。

Original Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) incur prohibitive inference costs due to long visual token sequences. Training-free visual token reduction provides an efficient solution. However, existing methods distort attention distributions, giving rise to a phenomenon we term Attention Logit Collapse. To address this issue, we propose ERA, an Entropy-guided visual token pruning framework with Rectified Attention for efficient MLLMs. Specifically, ERA comprises three crucial components: Dual-view Entropy Pruning (DEP), Bias-aware Token Recycling (BTR), and Logit-preserving Attention Rectification (LAR). First, DEP identifies representative anchor tokens by jointly modeling visual diversity and head-wise saliency. BTR then recycles pruned tokens into their corresponding anchors while estimating a cluster-level logit bias. Building upon this, LAR injects the estimated bias into attention logits, effectively rectifying the collapse induced by token reduction. Together, these components preserve visual evidence even under aggressive compression, enabling robust performance across single-image, multi-image, and video settings on a wide range of MLLMs. Beyond delivering practical acceleration, ERA establishes logit-preserving visual token pruning as a principled framework for efficient MLLMs, unifying theoretical foundation, algorithmic design, and practical deployment. The code is at https://github.com/924973292/ERA.

中文摘要翻译: 多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）由于长视觉token序列而面临高昂的推理成本。训练无关的视觉token缩减提供了一种高效的解决方案。然而，现有方法会扭曲注意力分布，引发一种我们称之为注意力Logit崩塌的现象。为解决此问题，我们提出ERA——一种面向高效MLLMs的熵引导视觉token剪枝框架，并配有修正注意力机制。具体而言，ERA包含三个关键组件：双视角熵剪枝（DEP）、偏置感知token回收（BTR）和logit保持注意力修正（LAR）。首先，DEP通过联合建模视觉多样性与头级显著性来识别具有代表性的锚点token。随后，BTR将剪枝的token回收至其对应锚点，同时估计聚类级别的logit偏置。在此基础上，LAR将估计的偏置注入注意力logit中，有效修正由token缩减引起的崩塌。这些组件共同确保了即使在激进压缩下也能保留视觉证据，使ERA在单图像、多图像和视频等多种场景下对各类MLLMs均能保持鲁棒性能。除实现实际加速外，ERA将logit保持的视觉token剪枝确立为高效MLLMs的原则性框架，统一了理论基础、算法设计和实际部署。代码已开源：https://github.com/924973292/ERA。

</details>


### [139] [CoLT: Teaching Multi-Modal Models to Think with Chain of Latent Thoughts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31986)
*Lianyu Hu,Shengqian Qin,Zeqin Liao,Qing Guo,Liang Wan,Wei Feng,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CoLT提出了一种链式隐变量推理框架，用少量隐状态替代冗长文本进行多模态推理，通过外部解码器的双向监督和内部一致性约束来训练，在8个基准上超越现有隐推理方法，推理速度提升10.1倍。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于文本的思维链（CoT）推理存在推理速度慢（生成数千tokens）且受限于自然语言表达能力的问题，亟需一种更高效且表达能力更强的隐式推理范式。

Method: 提出CoLT框架，用链式隐变量表示替代文本tokens进行推理。训练时引入轻量级外部解码器提供双向步级监督：前向模式将隐变量解码为下一步文本推理；反向模式将解码器隐状态与模型隐变量对齐，同时加入内部监督确保隐变量步间一致性。推理时移除解码器和内部监督以保持高效。

Result: 在8个基准上，CoLT优于现有隐推理方法（CODI、SIM-CoT）以及依赖昂贵标注的隐视觉推理方法；相比文本CoT，推理时间降低10.1倍，文本解码时间降低22.6倍。

Conclusion: 链式隐变量推理可以替代冗长文本CoT，在保持甚至提升推理性能的同时显著降低推理时延，为多模态大模型的高效推理提供了新方向。

Original Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning has enabled multi-modal large language models (MLLMs) to tackle complex visual reasoning tasks by generating explicit intermediate reasoning steps in natural language. However, this text-based reasoning paradigm is inherently slow at inference time with even thousands of tokens and fundamentally constrained by the expressiveness of natural language. In this paper, we propose CoLT, (Chain of Latent Thoughts), a novel framework that teaches multi-modal models to reason through a chain of latent thought representations instead of verbose text tokens, which can perform thinking with as few as 3 steps. Naively forcing the model to think with latent states easily produces meaningless semantics and makes training unstable. To effectively regulate the latent reasoning process, we introduce a lightweight external decoder that provides step-level supervision for each latent reasoning step in two complementary directions: a forward mode that decodes latent thoughts into the textual reasoning of the next step, and a backward mode that aligns decoder hidden states with the model's latent thoughts given preceding textual context. We further incorporate internal supervision that encourages coherent step-by-step latent transitions. The decoder and internal supervision are removed during inference to maintain high efficiency of latent reasoning. Extensive experiments on eight benchmarks demonstrate that CoLT not only outperforms existing latent reasoning methods such as CODI and SIM-CoT, but also surpasses latent visual reasoning approaches that rely on auxiliary images with costly annotation requirements. Compared to text CoT methods, CoLT can notably reduce the inference time by 10.1$\times$ and text decoding time by 22.6$\times$. Code is released at https://github.com/hulianyuyy/CoLT.

中文摘要翻译: 思维链推理使得多模态大语言模型能够通过生成显式的中间自然语言推理步骤来处理复杂的视觉推理任务。然而，这种基于文本的推理范式在推理时本质上速度缓慢（甚至生成数千个token），且从根本上受到自然语言表达能力的限制。本文提出CoLT（Chain of Latent Thoughts，隐变量思维链），一个新颖的框架，它教会多模态模型通过链式隐变量表示而非冗长的文本token进行推理，仅需3步即可实现思考。简单强制模型用隐状态进行思考容易产生无意义的语义并导致训练不稳定。为了有效调控隐式推理过程，我们引入了一个轻量级外部解码器，从两个互补方向为每个隐推理步骤提供步级监督：前向模式将隐变量解码为下一步的文本推理，反向模式在给定前文文本上下文的条件下将解码器隐状态与模型的隐变量对齐。我们进一步加入了内部监督，以鼓励连贯的步级隐状态过渡。推理时移除解码器和内部监督，以保持隐式推理的高效率。在八个基准上的大量实验表明，CoLT不仅优于现有的隐推理方法（如CODI和SIM-CoT），还超越了依赖昂贵标注辅助图像的隐视觉推理方法。与文本CoT方法相比，CoLT可将推理时间降低10.1倍，文本解码时间降低22.6倍。代码已开源：https://github.com/hulianyuyy/CoLT。

</details>


### [140] [Cross-Space Distillation: Teaching One-Step Students with Modern Diffusion Teachers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.32020)
*Anh Nguyen,Ngan Nguyen,Duc Vu,Trung Dao,Viet Nguyen,Quan Dao,Kien Nguyen,Chi Tran,Phong Nguyen,Khoi Nguyen,Cuong Pham,Dimitris Metaxas,Vishal M. Patel,Anh Tran*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Bridge，一种轻量级潜空间接口，解决了教师模型（如SD 3.5/Flux）与学生模型（如SD 1.5）因潜空间不兼容而无法进行知识蒸馏的问题，实现了跨空间蒸馏，显著提升了紧凑型一步生成模型的性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有一步扩散模型蒸馏方法假设教师和学生模型必须共享相同的潜空间（Shared-Space约束），这阻止了现代高容量教师模型（如SD 3.5、Flux）向紧凑、适合部署的学生模型（如SD 1.5）进行知识迁移，因为它们的潜分辨率和VAE参数化不同。

Method: 提出Bridge，一种轻量级潜空间接口，包含冻结的学生VAE解码器作为空间先验和紧凑的可学习投影器，通过潜重建和注意力保真度损失进行训练，实现学生潜空间到教师潜空间的稳定对齐，且不修改学生骨干网络。

Result: 在多种现代教师模型上，Bridge使紧凑的一步学生模型取得显著提升，例如SD 1.5的HPSv3从5.4提升至9.4，同时保持一步推理、低延迟和广泛的生态系统兼容性。

Conclusion: 异构的大容量教师模型可以通过轻量级潜空间接口蒸馏到高效、可部署的学生骨干网络中，突破了共享潜空间的限制。

Original Abstract: Modern one-step diffusion models achieve impressive quality through distribution-based timestep distillation. Yet, they rely on a critical assumption: Teacher and Student must inhabit the same latent space. This Shared-Space constraint prevents knowledge transfer from modern high-capacity Teachers (e.g., SD 3.5 and Flux) into compact, deployment-friendly Students such as SD 1.5, whose latent resolution and VAE parameterization differ from the Teacher. We formalize this overlooked regime as Cross-Space Distillation, where Teacher and Student differ in both latent resolution and VAE space. To enable distillation under this mismatch, we introduce the Bridge, a lightweight latent interface that maps Student latents into the Teacher space without modifying the Student backbone. Bridge combines a frozen Student VAE decoder as a spatial prior with a compact learnable projector, and is trained with latent reconstruction and attention fidelity objectives for stable Teacher-space alignment. Across diverse modern Teachers, Bridge enables substantial gains for compact one-step Students; for example, it improves SD 1.5 from 5.4 to 9.4 HPSv3 while preserving one-step inference, low latency, and broad ecosystem compatibility. These results show that heterogeneous large Teachers can be distilled into efficient, deployable backbones through a lightweight latent-space interface.

中文摘要翻译: 现代一步扩散模型通过基于分布的步长蒸馏实现了令人印象深刻的生成质量。然而，它们依赖一个关键假设：教师和学生必须处于相同的潜空间。这种共享空间约束阻止了从现代高容量教师模型（如SD 3.5和Flux）向紧凑、适合部署的学生模型（如SD 1.5）的知识迁移，因为后者的潜分辨率和VAE参数化与教师不同。本文将这一被忽视的场景形式化为跨空间蒸馏，即教师和学生在潜分辨率和VAE空间上均存在差异。为了在这种不匹配下实现蒸馏，我们引入了Bridge，一种轻量级潜空间接口，将学生潜编码映射到教师空间，而无需修改学生骨干网络。Bridge将冻结的学生VAE解码器作为空间先验，与紧凑的可学习投影器相结合，并通过潜重建和注意力保真度目标进行训练，以实现稳定的教师空间对齐。在多种现代教师模型上，Bridge使紧凑的一步学生模型获得了显著提升；例如，它将SD 1.5的HPSv3从5.4提升至9.4，同时保持了一步推理、低延迟和广泛的生态系统兼容性。这些结果表明，异构的大容量教师模型可以通过轻量级潜空间接口蒸馏到高效、可部署的骨干网络中。

</details>


### [141] [FLORA: A deep learning approach to predict forest attributes from heterogeneous LiDAR data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.32023)
*Emilie Vautier,Clément Mallet,Cédric Vega*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FLORA框架，利用八叉树深度学习网络融合生态与时空辅助变量，从异构LiDAR点云中预测六项森林属性。在法国32,052个NFI样地数据上，单一模型在落叶/非落叶采集条件下均表现优异，树高rRMSE约12.3%，总体积rRMSE约39%，为大规模异构LiDAR数据的森林属性估计提供了鲁棒基线。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 随着欧洲各国LiDAR项目扩展，传感器、飞行参数、季节和扫描角度的差异导致现有模型（通常针对局部条件校准）鲁棒性不足，难以在异构LiDAR数据条件下实现全覆盖预测。

Method: 提出FLORA（Forest LiDAR Octree Regression with Auxiliary Data）深度学习框架：采用基于八叉树（octree）的主干网络处理异构LiDAR点云，通过后融合门控机制（late-fusion gating mechanism）整合生态和时空辅助变量，预测六项森林属性（优势木高、总蓄积量、阔叶蓄积量、针叶蓄积量、胸高断面积、立木密度）。

Result: 在法国本土32,052个NFI样地数据上，单一模型同时训练落叶和非落叶数据优于季节专用模型；辅助变量整体贡献有限，但对树种-specific蓄积量预测帮助更大；FLORA对优势木高rRMSE约12.3%（R²=0.88），总蓄积量rRMSE约39%（R²=0.74）。

Conclusion: FLORA为从异构国家LiDAR计划中大规模估计森林属性提供了鲁棒基线，能够有效应对传感器、季节等采集条件差异，推动LiDAR在国家级森林资源监测中的实际应用。

Original Abstract: Forest attributes are essential for national-scale resource monitoring. Airborne LiDAR metrics are among the auxiliary variables most strongly correlated with forest attributes used in National Forest Inventory (NFI) estimates. However, producing wall-to-wall predictions remains challenging when LiDAR data are acquired under heterogeneous conditions. As national LiDAR programs expand across Europe, variability in sensors, flight parameters, seasons, and scan angles limits the robustness of existing models, which are often calibrated for local conditions. We present FLORA (Forest LiDAR Octree Regression with Auxiliary Data), a deep learning framework that predicts six forest attributes: dominant height, total volume, deciduous volume, coniferous volume, basal area, and stem density from heterogeneous LiDAR point clouds. FLORA combines an octree-based backbone with ecological and spatiotemporal auxiliary variables through a late-fusion gating mechanism. Models are trained and evaluated on 32,052 National Forest Inventory plots across mainland France using data from the French LiDAR HD program. A single model trained on both leaf-on and leaf-off acquisitions outperforms season-specific models and improves cross-season robustness. Auxiliary variables provide modest overall gains but contribute more strongly to species-specific volume prediction. FLORA achieves an rRMSE of about 12.3% (R2 = 0.88) for dominant height and 39% (R2 = 0.74) for total volume, providing a robust baseline for large-scale forest attribute estimation from heterogeneous national LiDAR programs.

中文摘要翻译: 森林属性对于国家尺度的资源监测至关重要。机载LiDAR指标是与国家森林清查（NFI）估计中使用的森林属性相关性最强的辅助变量之一。然而，当LiDAR数据在异质条件下采集时，生成全覆盖预测仍具有挑战性。随着欧洲各国LiDAR项目的扩展，传感器、飞行参数、季节和扫描角度的差异限制了现有模型（通常针对局部条件校准）的鲁棒性。我们提出了FLORA（Forest LiDAR Octree Regression with Auxiliary Data），一个深度学习框架，能够从异质LiDAR点云中预测六项森林属性：优势木高、总蓄积量、阔叶蓄积量、针叶蓄积量、胸高断面积和立木密度。FLORA通过后融合门控机制将基于八叉树的骨干网络与生态和时空辅助变量相结合。模型在法国本土32,052个国家森林清查样地上进行训练和评估，数据来自法国LiDAR HD计划。在落叶和非落叶采集数据上共同训练的单一模型优于季节专用模型，并提高了跨季节鲁棒性。辅助变量总体贡献有限，但对树种-specific蓄积量预测的贡献更大。FLORA在优势木高上达到约12.3%的rRMSE（R²=0.88），在总蓄积量上达到约39%的rRMSE（R²=0.74），为从异质国家LiDAR计划中进行大规模森林属性估计提供了鲁棒基线。

</details>


### [142] [SpheRoPE: Zero-Shot Optimization-Free 360 Panorama Generation with Spherical RoPE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.32033)
*Or Hirschorn,Aaron Olender,Eli Alshan,Ianir Ideses,Lior Fritz,Sagie Benaim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种零样本、无需训练和优化的框架SpheRoPE，通过将球面先验注入预训练的扩散Transformer，直接生成360°全景图像和视频，无需微调或迭代优化。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有360°全景生成方法要么依赖昂贵的微调且受限于稀缺的全景数据，要么需要多步优化导致推理延迟过高。作者观察到现有生成模型虽有一定全景先验，但无法满足等距柱状投影（ERP）的严格拓扑约束。

Method: 提出Spherical RoPE替代标准旋转位置编码：低频通道重参数化为3D笛卡尔坐标以编码球面流形，高频通道采用谐波量化强制精确周期性。同时结合语义失真无分类器引导（CFG）显式引导几何结构。

Result: 在Flux.1、Flux.2和LTX-Video等多个骨干网络上实现文本到全景图的生成，达到与基线方法相竞争的性能，且完全无需训练。

Conclusion: 该方法无需重新训练即可继承最先进模型的全部创作广度，能泛化到不同骨干网络和360°生成模态，为全景生成提供了高效实用的零样本方案。

Original Abstract: We present a zero-shot, training-free and optimization-free framework for generating 360 panoramic images and videos by directly injecting spherical priors into pre-trained diffusion transformers. Existing methods either rely on costly fine-tuning on scarce panoramic data that limits generalization, or leverage multi-step optimization that incurs prohibitive inference latency. We observe that contemporary generative models natively exhibit some panoramic priors from large-scale training. However, these emergent capabilities are insufficient, as the models fundamentally fail to satisfy the rigorous topological constraints imposed by equirectangular projection (ERP). We introduce a zero-shot and optimization-free approach that resolves these constraints at inference time. Spherical RoPE replaces standard rotary position embeddings: low-frequency channels are re-parameterized as 3D Cartesian coordinates to natively encode the spherical manifold, while high-frequency channels are harmonically quantized to enforce exact periodicity. Coupled with complementary Semantic Distortion classifier-free guidance (CFG) that explicitly steers geometry, we avoid retraining and inherit the full creative breadth of state-of-the-art models. Our approach generalizes across diverse backbones and 360 generation modalities. We demonstrate this across text-to-panorama using Flux.1, Flux.2, and LTX-Video backbones, achieving competitive performance against baselines, all while remaining training-free. Project page: https://orhir.github.io/SpheRoPE

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出了一种零样本、无需训练和无需优化的框架，通过将球面先验直接注入预训练的扩散Transformer来生成360°全景图像和视频。现有方法要么依赖在稀缺全景数据上进行昂贵的微调，从而限制了泛化能力，要么利用多步优化导致推理延迟过高。我们观察到，当代生成模型从大规模训练中本征地展现了一些全景先验。然而，这些涌现能力并不充分，因为模型从根本上无法满足等距柱状投影（ERP）强加的严格拓扑约束。我们提出了一种零样本且无需优化的方法，在推理时解决了这些约束。Spherical RoPE替代了标准旋转位置编码：低频通道被重参数化为3D笛卡尔坐标以本征地编码球面流形，而高频通道通过谐波量化强制实现精确周期性。结合显式引导几何结构的互补性语义失真无分类器引导（CFG），我们避免了重新训练，并继承了最先进模型的全部创作广度。我们的方法能够泛化到不同的骨干网络和360°生成模态。我们使用Flux.1、Flux.2和LTX-Video骨干网络在文本到全景图任务上进行了演示，在保持无需训练的同时实现了与基线方法相竞争的性能。项目页面：https://orhir.github.io/SpheRoPE

</details>


### [143] [PointSplat: Compact Gaussian Splatting via Human-Centric Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.32036)
*Yujie Guo,Yudong Jin,Lingteng Qiu,Zehong Shen,Zhen Xu,Jing Zhang,Xianchao Shen,Hujun Bao,Sida Peng,Xiaowei Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PointSplat提出了一种直接在3D空间中预测高斯原语的人体重建方法，通过点云裁剪和Point-Image Transformer融合几何与外观特征，在减少高斯数量的同时提升了新视图渲染质量和效率。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有前馈式重建方法在多个视图间重复编码相同主体内容，导致显著的视图间冗余。为了在有限的计算能力和传输带宽下实现紧凑且高质量的人体3D表示，需要一种直接在3D空间进行预测的方法。

Method: 首先估计粗粒度几何代理并通过光线投射裁剪冗余点并建立显式2D-3D对应关系；然后使用Point-Image Transformer融合外观和几何特征，在单次前向传播中预测高斯属性。该设计将预测限制在前景感兴趣区域，显著减少高斯总数。

Result: 在多个数据集上的大量实验表明，PointSpat在实现更高效率和更高质量的同时，对视图数量和图像分辨率的变化表现出强鲁棒性。

Conclusion: PointSplat通过在3D空间中直接预测高斯原语，有效减少了视图间冗余，在保证高保真度的同时实现了紧凑表示，为沉浸式直播系统等实时应用提供了高效的人体3D重建方案。

Original Abstract: Producing 3D human representations from input views on the fly is essential for immersive live streaming systems, where representation compactness is as critical as high fidelity given limited computational power and transmission bandwidth. Although recent feed-forward reconstruction methods achieve impressive quality through the view-centric prediction of 3D representations, they repeatedly encode the same subject content across multiple views, leading to significant inter-view redundancy. Our key insight is to perform predictions directly in 3D space, enabling the network to learn and produce a highly compact representation. To this end, we propose PointSplat, a novel human-centric approach that directly infers Gaussian primitives from an input point set. The proposed method first estimates a coarse geometric proxy and performs ray casting to prune redundant points and establish explicit 2D--3D correspondences. Subsequently, it employs a Point-Image Transformer to fuse appearance and geometry features, predicting Gaussian attributes in a single forward pass. This design restricts predictions to foreground regions of interest, substantially reducing the total number of Gaussians while improving novel-view rendering quality. Extensive experiments demonstrate that PointSplat achieves higher efficiency and quality while exhibiting strong robustness to variations in view count and image resolution across multiple datasets.

中文摘要翻译: 从输入视图即时生成3D人体表示对沉浸式直播系统至关重要，在有限计算能力和传输带宽下，表示的紧凑性与高保真度同等重要。尽管近期前馈式重建方法通过视图中心的3D表示预测取得了令人印象深刻的质量，但它们在多个视图间重复编码相同的主体内容，导致显著的视图间冗余。我们的关键洞察是直接在3D空间中进行预测，使网络能够学习并生成高度紧凑的表示。为此，我们提出了PointSplat，一种新颖的以人为中心的方法，直接从输入点集推断高斯原语。该方法首先估计粗粒度几何代理，并执行光线投射以裁剪冗余点并建立显式2D-3D对应关系。随后，它采用Point-Image Transformer融合外观和几何特征，在单次前向传播中预测高斯属性。该设计将预测限制在前景感兴趣区域，在显著减少高斯总数的同时提高了新视图渲染质量。大量实验表明，PointSpat在多个数据集上实现了更高的效率和更高质量，同时对视图数量和图像分辨率的变化表现出强鲁棒性。

</details>


### [144] [GEAR: Guided End-to-End AutoRegression for Image Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.32039)
*Bin Lin,Zheyuan Liu,Chenguo Lin,Sixiang Chen,Yunyang Ge,Yunlong Lin,Jianwei Zhang,Miles Yang,Zhao Zhong,Liefeng Bo,Li Yuan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GEAR提出了一种端到端的联合训练方法，通过表示对齐引导VQ分词器与自回归生成器共同训练，解决了传统两阶段训练中分词器与生成器脱节的问题，在ImageNet生成上实现高达10倍的收敛加速。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统的视觉生成模型采用两阶段训练：先训练分词器（重建）再冻结，然后训练生成器。这种解耦方式导致分词器不了解生成器的建模偏好，分词器生成的离散编码可能包含生成器难以建模的模式，限制了生成效率和质量。

Method: 提出GEAR方法，端到端联合训练VQ分词器和AR生成器。核心是码本分配的双重读出机制：1）硬one-hot分支用于AR的下一token预测训练；2）可微分的软分支传递表示对齐损失反馈指导分词器。AR模型引导分词器生成更易预测的索引分布，将对齐负担从分词器转移到AR模型。

Result: 1）ImageNet gFID收敛速度相比LlamaGen-REPA基线提升高达10倍；2）学习到显著更好的patch级别且空间一致的特征；3）方法适用于多种量化器（VQVAE、LFQ、IBQ）并泛化到文生图任务；4）与扩散模型方案相反，分词器特征变得不那么DINOv2-like，而AR特征变得更DINOv2-like。

Conclusion: 通过端到端联合训练和表示对齐引导，GEAR有效解决了分词器与生成器之间的语义脱节问题，大幅提升了自回归视觉生成的训练效率和特征质量，为视觉生成模型训练范式提供了新思路。

Original Abstract: Visual generative models are typically trained in two stages. A tokenizer is first trained for reconstruction and then frozen, after which a generator is trained on its discrete indices or continuous latents. This decoupling leaves the tokenizer unaware of what the generator finds easy to model. We present GEAR (Guided End-to-end AutoRegression), which trains a vector-quantized (VQ) tokenizer and an autoregressive (AR) generator jointly and end-to-end, guided by representation alignment. The key obstacle is that the VQ index fed to the AR model is non-differentiable, so gradients cannot reach the tokenizer, and a straight-through estimator collapses. GEAR resolves this with a dual read-out of the codebook assignment. A hard, one-hot branch trains the AR with next-token prediction, while a differentiable soft branch carries a representation-alignment loss that flows back to guide only the tokenizer. The AR model thereby steers its tokenizer toward an index distribution it can predict more easily. This shifts the alignment burden from the tokenizer to the AR: the tokenizer's own features become less DINOv2-like while the AR's become more so, the opposite of diffusion-side recipes that make the latent itself semantic. GEAR speeds up ImageNet gFID convergence by up to 10x relative to the strong LlamaGen-REPA baseline, learns markedly better patch-level and spatially-coherent features, and generalizes across quantizers (VQVAE, LFQ, IBQ) and to text-to-image generation.

中文摘要翻译: 视觉生成模型通常采用两阶段训练。首先训练分词器用于重建，然后将其冻结，之后在其离散索引或连续潜变量上训练生成器。这种解耦导致分词器不了解生成器容易建模的内容。本文提出GEAR（引导式端到端自回归），通过表示对齐引导，联合且端到端地训练向量量化（VQ）分词器和自回归（AR）生成器。关键障碍在于，输入到AR模型的VQ索引是不可微的，因此梯度无法到达分词器，而直通估计器会崩溃。GEAR通过码本分配的双重读出机制解决了这一问题。硬one-hot分支以下一token预测方式训练AR，而可微分的软分支则携带表示对齐损失，反向传播仅用于引导分词器。由此，AR模型引导其分词器朝向更易于预测的索引分布。这将对齐负担从分词器转移到了AR：分词器自身的特征变得不那么像DINOv2，而AR的特征变得更像DINOv2，这与使潜变量本身具有语义的扩散方案相反。相对于强基线LlamaGen-REPA，GEAR将ImageNet gFID的收敛速度提升了高达10倍，学习到了显著更好的patch级别且空间一致的特征，并且可推广到多种量化器（VQVAE、LFQ、IBQ）以及文生图生成任务。

</details>


### [145] [FaceMoE: Mixture of Experts for Low-Resolution Face Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.32040)
*Kartik Narayan,Vishal M. Patel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FaceMoE提出了一种基于混合专家（MoE）Transformer架构的低分辨率人脸识别方法，通过引入多个专用FFN专家和top-k路由器实现分辨率感知的特征提取和稀疏专家激活，在11个数据集上显著优于现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 低分辨率人脸识别面临两大挑战：一是探针图像因模糊、遮挡、低对比度等极端退化导致身份信息有限，特征提取困难；二是高分辨率画廊图像与低分辨率探针图像之间存在域差异，单一特征编码器难以在两个域上泛化，且在低分辨率数据集上微调时还会出现灾难性遗忘问题。

Method: 提出FaceMoE架构：1）在Transformer中引入多个专用前馈网络（FFN）专家；2）采用top-k路由器动态分配token给合适的专家，实现不同面部语义区域的专家专业化；3）通过top-k路由实现稀疏专家激活，在微调时保留预训练知识的同时增加模型容量且不带来比例计算开销；4）使用组合的人脸识别损失、路由器z-loss和负载均衡损失进行训练。

Result: 在11个覆盖高分辨率、混合质量和低分辨率基准的数据集上的大量实验表明，FaceMoE显著优于最先进的方法。

Conclusion: FaceMoE是首个将MoE架构用于低分辨率人脸识别的工作，通过专家专业化和稀疏激活有效解决了域差异和灾难性遗忘问题，为低分辨率人脸识别提供了新的有效范式。

Original Abstract: Low-resolution face recognition (LR-FR) remains a challenging task due to poor feature extraction and aggregation, as probe images often contain limited identity information resulting from extreme degradations such as blur, occlusion, and low contrast. Additionally, the domain gap between high-resolution (HR) gallery images and low-resolution (LR) probe images poses a significant challenge. A single feature encoder struggles to generalize effectively across both domains when fine-tuned on an LR dataset, and this issue is further magnified by catastrophic forgetting. To address these challenges, we propose FaceMoE, an effective adaptation of Mixture of Experts (MoE) transfomer architecture for low-resolution face-recognition . Specifically, we introduce multiple specialized feed-forward network (FFN) experts and incorporate a top-k router, which dynamically assigns tokens to appropriate experts. This design emergently promotes specialization across experts for different semantic regions of the face, which enables FaceMoE to perform resolution-aware feature extraction. Moreover, the top-k router facilitates sparse expert activation, enabling the model to preserve pretrained knowledge when finetuned on a LR dataset, while increasing model capacity without proportional computational overhead. FaceMoE is trained with a combined face recognition loss, router z-loss, and load balancing loss to ensure expert specialization and stable training. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first work leveraging MoE for LR-FR. Extensive experiments across eleven datasets, spanning HR, mixed-quality, and LR benchmarks, demonstrate that FaceMoE significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods. Code: https://github.com/Kartik-3004/FaceMoE

中文摘要翻译: 低分辨率人脸识别（LR-FR）仍然是一项具有挑战性的任务，原因在于特征提取和聚合效果较差，因为探针图像通常包含因模糊、遮挡和低对比度等极端退化导致的有限身份信息。此外，高分辨率（HR）画廊图像与低分辨率（LR）探针图像之间的域差异也构成了重大挑战。当在低分辨率数据集上微调时，单一特征编码器难以在两个域上有效泛化，而灾难性遗忘进一步放大了这一问题。为解决这些挑战，我们提出了FaceMoE，一种针对低分辨率人脸识别的混合专家（MoE）Transformer架构的有效适配。具体而言，我们引入了多个专用前馈网络（FFN）专家，并结合了一个top-k路由器，该路由器动态地将token分配给合适的专家。这种设计促使专家针对人脸的不同语义区域产生专业化分工，从而使FaceMoE能够进行分辨率感知的特征提取。此外，top-k路由器促进了稀疏专家激活，使模型在低分辨率数据集上微调时能够保留预训练知识，同时在不增加比例计算开销的前提下提升模型容量。FaceMoE使用组合的人脸识别损失、路由器z-loss和负载均衡损失进行训练，以确保专家专业化和稳定训练。据我们所知，这是首个利用MoE进行低分辨率人脸识别的工作。在涵盖高分辨率、混合质量和低分辨率基准的11个数据集上进行的大量实验表明，FaceMoE显著优于最先进的方法。代码：https://github.com/Kartik-3004/FaceMoE

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [146] [Joint discovery of governing partial differential equations from multi-source datasets by competitive optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30699)
*Hao Xu,Siyu Lou,Yuntian Chen,Dongxiao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于竞争性优化框架的多源数据驱动偏微分方程发现方法（MCO-PDE），通过软竞争加权机制融合异构数据集，自动识别控制方程的结构与参数。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有数据驱动方法通常在单一数据集上运行，当观测数据受限时性能不佳；而实际中同一物理系统往往存在多个仅初始/边界条件不同的数据集，亟需一种能融合多源数据以提升方程发现能力的方法。

Method: 首先为每个数据源训练独立的神经代理模型，然后采用软竞争加权机制动态评估各数据集的可靠性并聚合得到全局一致系数；结合遗传算法进行结构搜索，同时识别控制定律的函数形式和参数。

Result: 在七个案例中，每个数据集仅需50个观测值即可高精度恢复经典方程；该方法能处理二维和三维不规则边界及非均匀系数问题，并成功从真实波浪水槽实验中提取出具有物理意义的规律。

Conclusion: 该工作为通过异构数据融合实现自动化科学发现建立了一条有前景的路径。

Original Abstract: Discovering governing equations directly from observational data is a key step towards interpretable scientific machine learning. Current data-driven approaches typically operate on a single dataset, inherently limiting their performance when faced with restricted observations. In practice, multiple datasets are often available for the same physical system, distinguished only by distinct initial conditions or boundary configurations. Here, we present a competitive optimization framework designed to discover shared partial differential equations (PDEs) from multi-source datasets, termed MCO-PDE. The framework first trains independent neural surrogates for each data source, and then employs a soft-competitive weighting mechanism to dynamically assess dataset credibility and aggregate a consensus global coefficient. Integrated with a genetic algorithm for structural search, this approach simultaneously identifies the functional forms and parameters of the governing laws. We demonstrate that fusing as few as 50 observations per dataset across seven cases recovers canonical equations with high accuracy. The framework inherently handles two- and three-dimensional domains characterized by irregular boundaries and heterogeneous coefficients, and successfully extracts physically meaningful laws from real-world wave-tank experiments. Overall, this work establishes a promising route for automated scientific discovery via heterogeneous data fusion.

中文摘要翻译: 直接从观测数据中发现控制方程是实现可解释科学机器学习的关键步骤。当前的数据驱动方法通常在单一数据集上运行，当观测受限时其性能固有地受到限制。实际上，对于同一物理系统，往往存在多个数据集，它们仅因初始条件或边界配置不同而有所区分。本文提出了一种名为MCO-PDE的竞争性优化框架，旨在从多源数据中发现共享的偏微分方程。该框架首先为每个数据源训练独立的神经代理模型，然后采用软竞争加权机制动态评估数据集的可信度并聚合得到全局一致系数。结合遗传算法进行结构搜索，该方法能同时识别控制定律的函数形式和参数。我们证明，在七个案例中，每个数据集仅需融合少至50个观测值，即可高精度地恢复经典方程。该框架天然地适用于具有不规则边界和异质系数的二维及三维区域，并成功从真实波浪水槽实验中提取出具有物理意义的规律。总体而言，这项工作为通过异构数据融合实现自动化科学发现开辟了一条有前景的途径。

</details>


### [147] [Accelerometry-Derived Digital Biomarkers for Cardiometabolic Risk: A Population-Representative Tabular Benchmark with Uncertainty Quantification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30702)
*Federico Felizzi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于NHANES 2003-2006加速度计数据的心血管代谢基准数据集，评估了三种表格学习方法（岭回归、XGBoost、TabPFN v2）预测HbA1c、甘油三酯和CRP的性能，并利用分裂共形预测评估了不同性别和种族/民族亚组间的覆盖率公平性，发现边际覆盖与亚组条件覆盖之间存在差距。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
