However, its practical deployment remains challenging due to severe performance degradation caused by feature distribution discrepancies across different subjects and recording sessions. Although domain adaptation (DA) techniques are commonly employed to mitigate such discrepancies, conventional methods often struggle to effectively aligning sEMG features, primarily due to their inherent stochasticity and the scarcity of labeled data. To address these limitations, this paper proposes a novel Pressure-Guided Unsupervised Domain Adaptation (PGUDA) framework, which leverages the robustness and stability of pressure signals to introduce a cross-modal knowledge distillation strategy that transfers consistent physical semantics across modalities. Specifically, a teacher network trained on pressure signals guides an sEMG student network on unlabeled target domains, thereby regularizing the representation learning process with transferable and modality-invariant knowledge. Extensive experiments conducted on a self-collected multimodal dataset involving eleven subjects validate the effectiveness of the proposed PGUDA framework. The results demonstrate that our proposed PGUDA achieves leading performance in both cross-subject and cross-session classification tasks, achieving average accuracies of 58.08% and substantially outperforming existing DA approaches. Notably, PGUDA exhibits remarkable label efficiency: it attains classification accuracy comparable to fully supervised benchmarks while requiring only 5% of labeled data for teacher network training. This framework offers a robust and data-efficient solution that can significantly reduce the calibration burden in practical sEMG-based gesture recognition systems.

中文摘要翻译: 基于表面肌电信号（sEMG）的手势识别已成为自然人机交互中一项前景广阔的技术。然而，由于不同受试者和记录会话间特征分布差异导致的严重性能下降，其实际部署仍然面临挑战。尽管域自适应（DA）技术常被用于缓解此类差异，但传统方法通常难以有效对齐sEMG特征，这主要是由于sEMG信号固有的随机性以及标注数据的稀缺性。为解决这些局限，本文提出了一种新颖的压力引导无监督域自适应（PGUDA）框架，该框架利用压力信号的鲁棒性和稳定性，引入跨模态知识蒸馏策略，在模态间传递具有一致物理语义的知识。具体而言，由压力信号训练的教师网络在未标注的目标域上引导sEMG学生网络，从而利用可迁移且模态不变的知识规范化表征学习过程。在涉及11名受试者的自采集多模态数据集上进行的大量实验验证了所提PGUDA框架的有效性。结果表明，PGUDA在跨受试者和跨会话分类任务中均取得了领先性能，平均准确率达到58.08%，显著优于现有的DA方法。值得注意的是，PGUDA展现出卓越的标签效率：在仅需5%的标注数据训练教师网络的情况下，即可达到与全监督基准相当的分类精度。该框架提供了一种鲁棒且数据高效的解决方案，能够显著降低实际sEMG手势识别系统中的校准负担。

</details>


### [236] [Transformer-Hypernetwork-Controlled Deep-Unfolded Phase-Aware Channel Estimation Refinement for Phase-Drift-Robust Backscatter Links](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31400)
*Hanyeol Ryu,Nohgyeom Ha,Sangkil Kim*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种变压器-超网络控制的深度展开相位感知信道估计细化方法（THUNDER），用于相位漂移反向散射链路。该方法通过展开高斯-牛顿步骤在精确相位指数模型上细化估计，并使用变压器提取相位上下文、超网络生成控制权重，在相位漂移场景下实现了比最强学习基线高8.9 dB的归一化均方误差增益。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 残留载波相位漂移导致反向散射观测相位非平稳，现有的闭合形式相位感知信道估计（PACE）只能补偿一阶相位分量，在高信噪比下留下确定性误差地板，需要更精确的信道估计细化方法。

Method: 以PACE为初始化，在精确相位指数模型上执行展开的高斯-牛顿步骤进行细化估计；使用变压器提取导频宽度的相位上下文信息，超网络生成有界控制量和导频可靠性权重。

Result: 在归一化均方误差上，相比最强的基于学习的信道估计基线，获得了8.9 dB的增益。

Conclusion: THUNDER有效抑制了相位漂移场景下的高信噪比误差地板，显著提升了信道估计精度。

Original Abstract: This paper proposes a transformer-hypernetwork-controlled deep-unfolded phase-aware channel estimation refinement (THUNDER) for phase-drifting backscatter links. Residual carrier-phase drift across the pilot block renders the backscattered observation phase-nonstationary, and a closed-form phase-aware channel estimation (PACE) compensates only the first-order phase component, leaving a deterministic high signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) error floor. THUNDER suppresses this floor by initializing from PACE and refining the estimate through unfolded Gauss-Newton steps on the exact phase-exponential model. A transformer extracts pilot-wide phase context, and a hypernetwork generates bounded controls and pilot-reliability weights. Evaluations show an 8.9 dB normalized mean square error gain over the strongest learning-based channel estimation baseline.

中文摘要翻译: 本文提出了一种变压器-超网络控制的深度展开相位感知信道估计细化方法（THUNDER），用于相位漂移反向散射链路。残余载波相位漂移跨导频块传播，导致反向散射观测相位呈现非平稳性，而闭式相位感知信道估计（PACE）仅能补偿一阶相位分量，在高信噪比（SNR）下留下确定的误差地板。THUNDER 以 PACE 为初始值，通过在精确相位指数模型上执行展开的高斯-牛顿步骤来细化估计，从而抑制该误差地板。变压器用于提取导频宽度的相位上下文信息，超网络则生成有界控制量和导频可靠性权重。评估结果表明，与最强的基于学习的信道估计基线相比，该方法在归一化均方误差上获得了8.9 dB的增益。

</details>


### [237] [Rethinking Energy Efficiency in Cell-Free Massive MIMO: The Role of Processing and Optical Fronthaul](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31412)
*Ozan Alp Topal,Özlem Tuğfe Demir,Cicek Cavdar*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文针对无小区大规模MIMO系统，提出了一个模块化功耗模型，揭示了前传和云计算处理在能效中的主导作用，并分析了功能拆分、预编码策略及天线分布密度对能效的影响。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 随着无小区大规模MIMO依赖密集前传连接和虚拟化云RAN架构，其能耗已不再主要由射频组件驱动，而是由前传和处理环节主导，因此需要新的视角来定义和优化能效。

Method: 提出了一个模块化功耗模型，涵盖射频、前传和云处理三个环节，分析不同功能拆分和预编码策略对前传数据负载和总功耗的影响。

Result: 集中式预编码能以更少的资源利用提供更强性能；灵活激活射频和处理单元可避免不必要的开销；天线的密集分布能提升系统能效。

Conclusion: 无小区大规模MIMO的能效随天线分布密度的增加而提升，尤其在结合端到端资源分配时效果更显著。

Original Abstract: Cell-free massive MIMO promises uniformly high performance by combining densely distributed radio units, coherent transmission, and centralized processing. Unlike earlier radio generations, it depends on dense fronthaul connectivity and a virtualized cloud-RAN architecture. In this setting, energy use is no longer driven primarily by active radio components; instead, fronthaul and processing play a dominant role, calling for a fresh perspective on what defines energy efficiency. This work introduces a modular power model that captures the interplay between radios, fronthaul, and cloud processing. The analysis highlights how design choices, such as functional splits and precoding strategies, shape both fronthaul data load and total power consumption. Centralized precoding provides stronger performance with less resource utilization, while flexible activation of radios and processing elements avoids unnecessary overhead. Overall, the energy efficiency of cell-free massive MIMO grows as antennas are more densely distributed across the coverage area, particularly when combined with end-to-end resource allocation.

中文摘要翻译: 无小区大规模MIMO通过结合密集分布的无线电单元、相干传输和集中式处理，有望提供均匀的高性能。与早期无线世代不同，它依赖于密集的前传连接和虚拟化的云RAN架构。在此背景下，能耗不再主要由有源射频组件驱动；相反，前传和处理环节扮演主导角色，这要求我们以全新的视角定义能效。本文引入了一个模块化功耗模型，用于捕捉射频、前传和云处理之间的相互影响。分析揭示了功能拆分和预编码策略等设计选择如何影响前传数据负载和总功耗。集中式预编码能以更少的资源利用提供更强的性能，而灵活激活射频和处理单元则可避免不必要的开销。总体而言，无小区大规模MIMO的能效随天线在覆盖区域内更密集地分布而提升，尤其在结合端到端资源分配时效果更为显著。

</details>


### [238] [Towards a Joint Task-Oriented and Generative Semantic Communication Framework for 6G Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31426)
*Soheyb Ribouh,Phil Polo Ditsia Di Ngoma*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于OFDM的端到端语义通信框架，将视觉场景编码为图结构表示，在接收端通过ST-GNN进行碰撞风险估计，并利用扩散模型重建图像，在MIMO车载信道下实现99.1%数据压缩，同时保持高推理性能和感知 fidelity。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统通信传输原始数据带宽消耗大，而语义通信通过传输任务相关语义信息可显著降低带宽，但现有语义通信框架在复杂场景（如车载通信）下的任务推理与重建能力仍待提升。

Method: 提出一种端到端OFDM语义通信框架，包含：1) 语义编码器将视觉场景转换为基于图的表示（对象级特征+关系结构）；2) 经OFDM神经接收机传输后，接收端使用ST-GNN进行碰撞风险估计；3) 扩散模型语义解码器从恢复的语义中重建视觉场景，实现双功能（安全预测+图像重建）。

Result: 相比像素域传输实现99.1%数据压缩；优于JPEG和HEVC等传统压缩方法；在保持下游推理性能的同时，扩散重建的FID分数显著优于现有语义通信方法。

Conclusion: 所提框架在车载通信场景下实现了极高效的数据压缩与高质量语义重建的平衡，验证了语义通信在6G无线系统中的巨大潜力。

Original Abstract: Semantic Communication (SC) has emerged as a key enabler for 6G wireless systems by transmitting task-relevant meaning rather than raw data, thereby significantly reducing bandwidth consumption while preserving communication intent. In this work, we propose an end-to-end OFDM-based semantic communication framework that integrates a semantic encoder-decoder pipeline with a neural receiver operating over a 3GPP vehicular channel. The semantic encoder extracts the underlying meaning of a visual scene by transforming it into a graph-based representation consisting of object-level features and relational structure. At the receiver, the reconstructed scene graph is processed by a spatio-temporal graph neural network (ST-GNN)-based module for collision-risk estimation, enabling task-oriented inference. In parallel, a diffusion-based semantic decoder reconstructs the visual scene from the recovered semantics, providing dual functionality: safety prediction and image reconstruction. The proposed framework is evaluated in a MIMO configuration under varying SNR conditions. Experimental results show that it achieves up to 99.1% data compression relative to pixel-domain transmission, outperforming conventional compression-based methods (JPEG and HEVC) while preserving downstream inference performance. Furthermore, the diffusion-based reconstruction attains significantly lower frechet inception distance (FID) scores than existing semantic communication approaches, reflecting superior semantic and perceptual fidelity.

中文摘要翻译: 语义通信（SC）通过传输与任务相关的语义信息而非原始数据，成为6G无线系统的关键使能技术，从而在保留通信意图的同时显著降低带宽消耗。本文提出了一种基于OFDM的端到端语义通信框架，该框架将语义编码-解码流水线与工作在3GPP车载信道上的神经接收机集成。语义编码器将视觉场景转换为基于图的表示（包含对象级特征和关系结构），提取其底层语义信息。在接收端，重建的场景图由基于时空图神经网络（ST-GNN）的模块处理，用于碰撞风险估计，从而实现面向任务的推理。同时，基于扩散模型的语义解码器从恢复的语义中重建视觉场景，提供双重功能：安全预测和图像重建。所提出的框架在MIMO配置下、不同信噪比条件下进行了评估。实验结果表明，相比像素域传输，该方法实现了高达99.1%的数据压缩，在保持下游推理性能的同时优于传统基于压缩的方法（JPEG和HEVC）。此外，基于扩散模型的重建在弗雷歇初始距离（FID）分数上显著低于现有语义通信方法，体现了更优的语义和感知保真度。

</details>


### [239] [Sensing for Reliable UAV Communication: Robust Trajectory and Resource Optimization in Low-Altitude Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31447)
*Yifan Jiang,Qingqing Wu,Hongxun Hui,Wen Chen,Wei Feng,Shanpu Shen*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文针对低空无线网络中多蜂窝连接无人机的移动不确定性，提出基于连续置信椭圆（CE）和离散逆白化扇区（IWS）的两种鲁棒优化方案，联合优化无人机预测位置、基站发射功率和带宽分配，以最大化最小中断容量。仿真验证了所提方案的有效性和优越性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在低空无线网络中， sensing-aided通信为无人机跟踪与通信提供了新的ISAC范式，但多无人机在移动不确定性下的可靠通信面临挑战，现有方法难以有效处理非凸且棘手的最大容忍中断概率约束。

Method: 提出两种鲁棒优化方案：1）基于连续置信椭圆（CE）的方案，通过块连续凸逼近优化无人机预测位置，再进行凸资源分配；2）基于离散逆白化扇区（IWS）的方案，提出IWS中断概率近似和低复杂度IWS选择方法，基于投影梯度下降进行顺序优化。两种算法统一为轨迹-资源联合优化框架。

Result: 仿真结果验证了所提中断概率近似的有效性，表明所提鲁棒优化方案相比基准方案显著提升了中断容量性能，且IWS方案优于CE方案。

Conclusion: 本文提出的基于CE和IWS的两种鲁棒优化方案能有效应对无人机移动不确定性，提升低空无线网络的中断容量性能，其中IWS方案在性能和复杂度上更具优势，为鲁棒无人机轨迹与资源管理提供了低复杂度结构。

Original Abstract: In low-altitude wireless networks, sensing-aided communication has emerged as a promising integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) paradigm for unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) tracking and communication. This paper investigates reliable sensing-aided communication for multiple cellular-connected UAVs under mobility uncertainties. Specifically, we maximize the minimum outage capacity among UAVs by jointly optimizing their real-time predicted positions, as well as the base station (BS) transmit power and bandwidth allocations. To address the non-convex and intractable maximum tolerable outage probability (OP) constraints, two robust optimization schemes are proposed based on a continuous confidence ellipse (CE) and discretized inverse-whitened sectors (IWSs), respectively. For the CE-based scheme, an efficient algorithm is proposed to optimize the predicted UAV positions individually via block successive convex approximation, followed by convex resource allocation. For the IWS-based scheme, an IWS-based OP approximation is proposed to facilitate the robust optimization, based on which a low-complexity IWS selection method is proposed to decouple the optimization variables. Then, a similar sequential optimization algorithm is proposed based on the projected gradient descent approach. The two algorithms are further unified into a common trajectory-resource optimization framework, revealing a low-complexity structure for robust UAV trajectory and resource management. Simulation results validate the effectiveness of our proposed OP approximation, demonstrate the significant outage capacity improvement of the proposed robust optimization schemes over benchmark schemes, and illustrate the superiority of the IWS-based scheme over the CE-based scheme.

中文摘要翻译: 在低空无线网络中，感知辅助通信已成为一种用于无人机跟踪与通信的 promising 的集成感知与通信（ISAC）范式。本文研究了在移动不确定性下多蜂窝连接无人机的可靠感知辅助通信问题。具体而言，我们通过联合优化无人机的实时预测位置以及基站的发射功率和带宽分配，来最大化无人机之间的最小中断容量。为了处理非凸且棘手的最大容忍中断概率约束，分别基于连续置信椭圆（CE）和离散逆白化扇区（IWS）提出了两种鲁棒优化方案。对于基于CE的方案，提出了一种高效算法，通过块连续凸逼近逐一优化无人机预测位置，随后进行凸资源分配。对于基于IWS的方案，提出了一种基于IWS的中断概率近似以促进鲁棒优化，并在此基础上提出了一种低复杂度的IWS选择方法以解耦优化变量。随后，基于投影梯度下降方法提出了一种类似的顺序优化算法。这两种算法被进一步统一为一个通用的轨迹-资源优化框架，揭示了鲁棒无人机轨迹与资源管理的低复杂度结构。仿真结果验证了所提中断概率近似的有效性，表明所提鲁棒优化方案相比基准方案显著提升了中断容量，并展示了基于IWS的方案优于基于CE的方案。

</details>


### [240] [Von Mises Based Uncertainty Quantification for Closely Spaced Automotive Radar Targets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31473)
*Vinay Kulkarni,V. V. Reddy*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于不确定性感知的深度学习DOA估计方法，比较了基于冯·米塞斯分布的集成学习（ENS）与基于正态逆伽马的证据深度学习（EDL）框架。ENS在几何一致性和异常检测灵敏度上更优，而EDL在排序一致性上略胜一筹。ENS可提供闭环VM似然，便于直接集成到检测-跟踪管道中。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 针对车载雷达DOA估计中不确定性量化问题，探索能够兼顾方向几何特性与下游任务集成的不确定性感知深度学习方法。

Method: 分别构建了基于圆形统计学的冯·米塞斯集成学习框架（输出参数μ, κ）和基于正态逆伽马公式的证据深度学习框架（导出学生t预测分布），并在分布内和多种分布外条件下通过风险覆盖率和ROC/AUROC进行分析比较。

Result: ENS在标称条件下不确定性更低，对严重扰动敏感性更强；EDL不确定性变化更平滑，排序一致性略优。ENS可通过闭环VM似然直接实现概率集成，支持统一的检测-跟踪管道。

Conclusion: 不确定性感知DOA估计中需要在几何一致性与统计泛化性之间进行权衡，ENS更适合需要方向几何一致性的下游集成任务。

Original Abstract: This work investigates uncertainty-aware deep learning approaches for direction of arrival (DOA) estimation in automotive radar, focusing on probabilistic modeling and downstream integration. A circular-statistics-based von Mises (VM) ensemble (ENS) is compared with an evidential deep learning (EDL) framework based on a normal inverse gamma formulation, yielding a Student t predictive distribution in the Euclidean domain. The ENS framework produces angular predictions parameterized by (mu, kappa), enabling interpretable uncertainty aligned with directional geometry. Performance is evaluated under in distribution and multiple out-of-distribution conditions using risk coverage and ROC or AUROC analyses. Results indicate that ENS achieves lower uncertainty under nominal conditions and exhibits stronger sensitivity to severe perturbations, whereas EDL provides smoother uncertainty variation and slightly improved ranking consistency. Importantly, the ENS representation enables direct probabilistic integration into association modules via closed form VM likelihoods, facilitating a unified detection tracking pipeline. These findings highlight a trade-off between geometric consistency and statistical generality in uncertainty-aware DOA estimation.

中文摘要翻译: 本研究探讨了面向车载雷达波达方向估计的不确定性感知深度学习方法，重点关注概率建模与下游集成。基于圆形统计学的冯·米塞斯集成学习与基于正态逆伽马公式的证据深度学习框架进行了比较，后者在欧几里得域中导出学生t预测分布。ENS框架产生由(μ, κ)参数化的角度预测，能够提供与方向几何对齐的可解释不确定性。在分布内和多种分布外条件下，使用风险覆盖率和ROC或AUROC分析评估性能。结果表明，ENS在标称条件下具有更低的不确定性，并对严重扰动表现出更强的敏感性，而EDL则提供更平滑的不确定性变化和略优的排序一致性。重要的是，ENS表示能够通过闭环VM似然实现直接的概率集成到关联模块中，从而促进统一的检测-跟踪管道。这些发现突显了不确定性感知DOA估计中几何一致性与统计泛化性之间的权衡。

</details>


### [241] [Resource-Efficient WiFi CSI Sensing via Exploiting the Age of Samples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31690)
*Abolfazl Zakeri,Nhan Thanh Nguyen,Markku Juntti*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出了一种年龄感知的WiFi感知框架，通过将CSI样本的新鲜度（年龄）编码并与CSI嵌入进行乘法融合，在感知速率约束下显著提升了人体活动识别和身份识别的性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: WiFi CSI感知需与数据通信共享信道，导致CSI测量采集速率受限。现有方法未充分考虑在感知速率约束下（如固定时隙采样或随机丢包）样本新鲜度对识别性能的影响。

Method: 提出低成本的年龄感知WiFi感知框架：先对每个保留CSI样本的年龄（新鲜度）进行编码，再通过乘法融合方式将年龄编码与CSI嵌入特征结合，联合训练模型。考虑两种固定CSI采样策略：确定性策略和随机伯努利策略。

Result: 在NTU-Fi人体活动识别和身份识别数据集上，所提模型持续优于仅CSI基线和UniFi基准中的时间感知注意力模型；在身份识别任务中，相比UniFi方法提升高达10个百分点，且在严格感知预算下增益最大。

Conclusion: 将样本年龄显式建模并融入CSI特征学习，能有效缓解感知速率约束带来的性能损失，尤其适用于采样资源受限或丢包不规则的WiFi感知场景。

Original Abstract: WiFi channel state information (CSI) sensing must coexist with data communications, which constrains the acquisition rate of fresh CSI measurements. To model this, we formulate CSI-based human activity and identity recognition under a sensing rate constraint that limits the fraction of time slots, within a measurement session, where CSI samples are available. This framework captures sensing-communication resource sharing and uncontrolled packet loss or traffic-driven irregularity. To satisfy the sensing constraint, two fixed CSI sampling policies are considered: a deterministic policy and a stochastic Bernoulli policy. We propose a low-cost age-aware WiFi sensing framework that explicitly incorporates sample freshness into the model training. The age of each retained CSI sample is first encoded and then fused with the CSI embedding via multiplicative fusion. On the NTU-Fi human activity recognition and person identification datasets, the proposed model consistently outperforms both a CSI-only baseline and the state-of-the-art time-aware attention model from the UniFi benchmark. For example, it yields up to a 10-percentage-point improvement over the UniFi method for person identification, with the largest gains observed under strict sensing budgets.

中文摘要翻译: WiFi信道状态信息（CSI）感知必须与数据通信共存，这制约了新鲜CSI测量的采集速率。为建模该问题，我们在感知速率约束下构建了基于CSI的人体活动和身份识别任务，该约束限制了测量会话中可获取CSI样本的时隙比例。该框架同时刻画了感知-通信资源共享以及不受控的丢包或流量驱动的非规则采样。为满足感知约束，考虑了两种固定CSI采样策略：确定性策略和随机伯努利策略。我们提出了一种低成本的年龄感知WiFi感知框架，将样本新鲜度显式纳入模型训练。首先对每个保留CSI样本的年龄进行编码，然后通过乘法融合方式与CSI嵌入特征融合。在NTU-Fi人体活动识别和身份识别数据集上，所提模型持续优于仅使用CSI的基线模型以及UniFi基准中最先进的时间感知注意力模型。例如，在身份识别任务中，该方法相比UniFi方法提升了高达10个百分点，且在严格感知预算下增益最为显著。

</details>


### [242] [Trade-Offs in Decentralized Gigantic MIMO with Hard-Boundary Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31911)
*Juan Vidal Alegría,Joao Vieira,Ove Edfors*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文探讨了6G基站使用FR3频段时，因天线数量大幅增加（相比5G FR1系统增加4-8倍）带来的处理复杂度和互连带宽挑战，并提出了一种基于WAX框架的改进方案，通过非协作硬件模块实现分散式处理，以在实际gigantic MIMO部署中平衡复杂度与性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 6G基站采用FR3频段时，为维持与5G FR1相当的孔径面积，天线数量需增加4-8倍，形成gigantic MIMO系统。这带来了巨大的处理复杂度和互连带宽挑战，亟需灵活的架构来应对这些困难。

Method: 对WAX框架进行改进，提出一种新的分散式处理架构，其中各硬件模块（可通过现有大规模MIMO基带单元实现）之间不进行协作，独立完成处理任务。

Result: 结果表明所提出的框架能够有效探索实际gigantic MIMO实现中复杂度与性能之间的权衡关系。

Conclusion: 该框架在存在硬边界约束的条件下依然适用，具有在实际gigantic MIMO部署中利用复杂度-性能权衡的潜力。

Original Abstract: To maintain the antenna apertures offered by 5G massive MIMO systems operating at the sub-6GHz band, known as FR1, 6G base stations (BSs) using the upper-mid band, FR3, should increase the number of antennas by a factor 4-8, giving rise to gigantic MIMO. This poses challenges in terms of processing complexity and interconnection bandwidth. The WAX framework, previously introduced for exploring trade-offs in decentralized architectures, may offer the flexibility needed to tackle these challenges. However, no results have been established on the applicability of this framework in the presence of hard-boundary constraints. The current work explores gigantic MIMO implementations based on a novel adaptation of the WAX framework, where the decentralized processing is performed by non-cooperating hardware modules. These modules may be implemented through state-of-the-art massive MIMO baseband units (BBUs). The results show the potential of the proposed framework towards exploiting trade-offs between complexity and performance in practical gigantic MIMO implementations.

中文摘要翻译: 为维持工作在sub-6GHz频段（FR1）的5G大规模MIMO系统所提供的天线孔径，使用上中频段（FR3）的6G基站需将天线数量增加4-8倍，从而产生巨型MIMO系统。这给处理复杂度和互连带宽带来了挑战。WAX框架此前被提出用于探索分散式架构中的权衡关系，可能提供应对这些挑战所需的灵活性。然而，该框架在硬边界约束条件下的适用性尚未得到验证。本文基于WAX框架的一种新型改进方案探索巨型MIMO实现，其中分散式处理由非协作的硬件模块完成。这些模块可通过现有的大规模MIMO基带单元实现。结果表明，所提出的框架在探索实际巨型MIMO实现中复杂度与性能之间的权衡方面具有潜力。

</details>


<div id='eess.IV'></div>

# eess.IV [[Back]](#toc)

### [243] [Accelerating Merge with Motion Vector Difference via Filter Difference Analysis for VVenC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31084)
*Xinmin Feng,Shengyang Xu,Jianhua Chen,Li Li,Dong Liu,Feng Wu*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于分数运动矢量滤波差异分析的快速MMVD算法，通过2抽头滤波器近似8抽头插值滤波器来估计候选增益，在VVenC编码器中将MMVD搜索比率从21.07%降至11.05%，效率-复杂度指标η从11.79降至7.10。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: VVC中MMVD工具采用穷举搜索策略，给编码器带来了显著的计算负担，因此需要一种快速算法来降低其计算复杂度。

Method: 基于分数运动矢量滤波差异分析，用2抽头滤波器近似8抽头插值滤波器，推导出基于空间梯度和预测残差的准则来估计MMVD候选的潜在增益；将该准则推广到适用于移位整数参考样本和二维可分离滤波；通过对称偏移推断和十字形下采样点积计算等实现优化来减少开销。

Result: 与VVenC中现有快速MMVD算法相比，该方法将平均MMVD搜索比率从21.07%降低到11.05%，效率-复杂度指标η从11.79降低到7.10（fast预设下）。

Conclusion: 提出的快速MMVD算法有效降低了VVenC编码器中MMVD的搜索比例和计算复杂度，提高了编码效率与复杂度的权衡。

Original Abstract: Merge with Motion Vector Difference (MMVD) is a key coding tool in Versatile Video Coding for improving motion prediction accuracy. However, its exhaustive search strategy imposes a significant computational burden on the encoder. To address this issue, we propose a novel fast MMVD algorithm for the VVenC encoder based on fractional motion vector filter difference analysis. By approximating the 8-tap interpolation filter with a 2-tap filter, we derive a criterion based on spatial gradients and prediction residuals for estimating the potential gain of MMVD candidates. We further generalize this criterion to accommodate both shifted integer reference samples and 2D separable filtering. To minimize the overhead of the proposed method, we introduce implementation optimizations, including symmetric offset inference and cross-shaped downsampled dot-product computation. Compared with existing fast MMVD algorithms in VVenC, our method reduces the average MMVD search ratio from 21.07\% to 11.05\% and decreases the efficiency-complexity metric $η$ from 11.79 to 7.10 under the fast preset.

中文摘要翻译: 合并运动矢量差（MMVD）是通用视频编码（VVC）中用于提高运动预测精度的关键编码工具。然而，其穷举搜索策略给编码器带来了显著的计算负担。为解决此问题，我们基于分数运动矢量滤波差异分析，提出了一种适用于VVenC编码器的新型快速MMVD算法。通过使用2抽头滤波器近似8抽头插值滤波器，我们推导出一个基于空间梯度和预测残差的准则，用于估计MMVD候选的潜在增益。我们进一步将该准则推广，使其同时适用于移位整数参考样本和二维可分离滤波。为最小化所提方法的开销，我们引入了实现优化，包括对称偏移推断和十字形下采样点积计算。与VVenC中现有的快速MMVD算法相比，我们的方法在fast预设下将平均MMVD搜索比率从21.07%降低到11.05%，并将效率-复杂度指标η从11.79降低到7.10。

</details>


### [244] [Distortion-Corrected Diffusion MRI Using Rotated-View EPI and Joint Field-Map/Image Estimation with Gaussian Primitives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31521)
*Wenqi Huang,Zhitao Li,Nan Wang,Yimeng Lin,Mengze Gao,Yurui Qian,Sevgi Gokce Kafali,Xiaozhi Cao,Kawin Setsompop,Daniel Rueckert,Congyu Liao*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出了一种物理信息驱动的联合估计框架，直接从k空间数据同时估计B0场和无失真图像，用于校正EPI的几何失真，在高b值和高加速因子下优于传统顺序方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有EPI失真校正方法采用先重建后校正的顺序流程，在高加速因子下存在重建伪影，在高扩散b值下信噪比低，导致B0场估计退化，限制了整体校正质量。

Method: 将图像和B0场分别表示为高斯基函数的叠加，嵌入MRI物理前向模型中；采用显式连续参数化，支持旋转视角EPI采集而无须插值；将扩散加权图像建模为实值非负，图像相位被吸收到每拍相位因子中。

Result: 在体内脑扩散EPI数据上，方法达到与无失真结构参考最接近的脑边界一致性，在高b值和高加速因子下相比顺序方法提升最大；视觉比较显示细节保真度和噪声抑制均有改善。

Conclusion: 该物理信息驱动的联合估计方法有效克服了顺序校正流程的局限，显著提升了高b值和高加速因子下的EPI失真校正质量。

Original Abstract: Echo Planar Imaging (EPI) is the standard acquisition technique for diffusion and functional neuroimaging, enabling rapid imaging but suffering from geometric distortions caused by B0 field inhomogeneities. Existing correction methods first reconstruct distorted images using parallel imaging, then estimate the B0 field and correct the distortion in the image domain. In this sequential process, reconstruction artifacts at high acceleration factors and low SNR at high diffusion b-values degrade B0 estimation and limit the overall correction quality. We propose a physics-informed framework that jointly estimates the B0 field and distortion-free image directly from k-space data, without depending on an intermediate parallel-imaging reconstruction for the correction. The image and the B0 field are each represented as a superposition of Gaussian primitives embedded within an MRI physics forward model. The explicit, continuous parameterization captures both smooth regions and tissue boundaries and supports rotated-view EPI acquisitions without interpolation. The diffusion-weighted image is modeled as real and non-negative, with the image phase absorbed into a per-shot phase factor. Rotated views distribute distortions across multiple phase-encoding orientations, improving point spread function isotropy and providing stronger constraints for B0 estimation. On in vivo brain diffusion EPI, the proposed method attains the closest brain-boundary agreement with a distortion-free structural reference, with the largest improvement over sequential methods at high b-value and high acceleration. Extensive visual comparisons further show improved detail fidelity and noise suppression.

中文摘要翻译: 回波平面成像（EPI）是扩散和功能神经成像的标准采集技术，能够实现快速成像，但存在由B0场不均匀性引起的几何失真。现有校正方法首先使用并行成像重建失真图像，然后估计B0场并在图像域中校正失真。在这种顺序流程中，高加速因子下的重建伪影和高扩散b值下的低信噪比会退化B0场估计，限制整体校正质量。我们提出了一种物理信息驱动的框架，直接从k空间数据联合估计B0场和无失真图像，无需依赖中间并行成像重建来进行校正。图像和B0场分别表示为嵌入MRI物理前向模型中的高斯基函数的叠加。这种显式连续参数化既能捕捉平滑区域又能捕捉组织边界，并支持旋转视角EPI采集而无须插值。扩散加权图像被建模为实值非负，图像相位被吸收到每拍相位因子中。旋转视角将失真分布在多个相位编码方向上，改善了点扩散函数的各向同性，并为B0场估计提供了更强的约束。在体内脑扩散EPI上，所提方法达到了与无失真结构参考最接近的脑边界一致性，在高b值和高加速因子下相比顺序方法提升最大。广泛的视觉比较进一步显示了改进的细节保真度和噪声抑制。

</details>
